LUNAR AI — public/ FOLDER (frontend)
======================================================================

All files inside the public/ folder. Create each one at the path shown after 'FILE:'.
This folder also works on its own as a static website (e.g. GitHub Pages) in local mode.

   LUNAR AI — design system

   Light: proper light theme (not an inversion)
   ========================================================= */

   is always sunlight gold, Earth-related data always Earth blue,
   terrain always regolith grey. No other decorative colour.
   ========================================================= */

# FILE 3/38: public/src/charts/charts.js

/**
 * Visualization layer — thin wrapper over Chart.js with theme-aware styling.
 * Charts are re-created on theme/language change so colours and labels always match.
 */
import { cssVar, esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';

const registry = new Set();

export const chartsAvailable = () => typeof window.Chart === 'function';

export function destroyCharts() {
  registry.forEach((c) => c.destroy());
  registry.clear();
}

/** Vertical marker line at index `options.plugins.marker.index` */
const markerPlugin = {
  id: 'marker',
  afterDatasetsDraw(chart, _args, opts) {
    if (opts == null || opts.index == null) return;
    const x = chart.scales.x.getPixelForValue(opts.index);
    if (!Number.isFinite(x)) return;
    const { top, bottom } = chart.chartArea;
    const ctx = chart.ctx;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.strokeStyle = opts.color || '#888';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, top);
    ctx.lineTo(x, bottom);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

function base() {
  const text = cssVar('--chart-text');
  const grid = cssVar('--chart-grid');
  const rtl = document.documentElement.dir === 'rtl';
  return {
    responsive: true,
    maintainAspectRatio: false,
    animation: { duration: 250 },
    interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
    plugins: {
      legend: { rtl, labels: { color: text, boxWidth: 12, font: { family: 'Inter, Noto Kufi Arabic, sans-serif' } } },
      tooltip: { rtl, textDirection: rtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr' },
    },
    scales: {
      x: { ticks: { color: text, maxRotation: 0, autoSkip: true, maxTicksLimit: 9, font: { family: 'Inter, Noto Kufi Arabic, sans-serif', size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid }, reverse: false },
      y: { ticks: { color: text, font: { family: 'Inter, Noto Kufi Arabic, sans-serif', size: 10 } }, grid: { color: grid }, position: rtl ? 'right' : 'left' },
    },
  };
}

function fallback(canvas) {
  const box = canvas.parentElement;
  box.innerHTML = `<div class="chart-fallback">${esc(t('chart.unavailable'))}</div>`;
  return null;
}

export const palette = () => [cssVar('--c1'), cssVar('--c2'), cssVar('--c3'), cssVar('--c4')];

export function lineChart(canvas, { labels, datasets, yTitle, yMin, yMax, marker, stepped }) {
  if (!canvas) return null;
  if (!chartsAvailable()) return fallback(canvas);
  const opts = base();
  if (yTitle) opts.scales.y.title = { display: true, text: yTitle, color: cssVar('--chart-text') };
  if (yMin !== undefined) opts.scales.y.min = yMin;
  if (yMax !== undefined) opts.scales.y.max = yMax;
  opts.plugins.marker = marker ? { index: marker, color: cssVar('--accent') } : { index: null };
  const chart = new window.Chart(canvas, {
    type: 'line',
    data: {
      labels,
      datasets: datasets.map((d) => ({
        label: d.label,
        data: d.data,
        borderColor: d.color,
        backgroundColor: d.fill ? d.color + '33' : d.color,
        fill: d.fill ? 'origin' : false,
        borderWidth: d.dash ? 1.2 : 2,
        borderDash: d.dash ? [5, 4] : undefined,
        pointRadius: 0,
        tension: d.stepped || stepped ? 0 : 0.25,
        stepped: d.stepped || stepped ? 'before' : false,
      })),
    },
    options: opts,
    plugins: [markerPlugin],
  });
  registry.add(chart);
  return chart;
}

export function barChart(canvas, { labels, datasets, yTitle, yMax, horizontal }) {
  if (!canvas) return null;
  if (!chartsAvailable()) return fallback(canvas);
  const opts = base();
  if (horizontal) opts.indexAxis = 'y';
  if (yTitle) opts.scales.y.title = { display: true, text: yTitle, color: cssVar('--chart-text') };
  if (yMax !== undefined) opts.scales[horizontal ? 'x' : 'y'].max = yMax;
  opts.scales[horizontal ? 'x' : 'y'].min = 0;
  const chart = new window.Chart(canvas, {
    type: 'bar',
    data: { labels, datasets: datasets.map((d) => ({ label: d.label, data: d.data, backgroundColor: d.color, borderRadius: 4, maxBarThickness: 34 })) },
    options: opts,
  });
  registry.add(chart);
  return chart;
}

export function radarChart(canvas, { labels, datasets }) {
  if (!canvas) return null;
  if (!chartsAvailable()) return fallback(canvas);
  const text = cssVar('--chart-text');
  const grid = cssVar('--chart-grid');
  const chart = new window.Chart(canvas, {
    type: 'radar',
    data: {
      labels,
      datasets: datasets.map((d) => ({ label: d.label, data: d.data, borderColor: d.color, backgroundColor: d.color + '22', pointRadius: 2, borderWidth: 2 })),
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      plugins: { legend: { rtl: document.documentElement.dir === 'rtl', labels: { color: text, boxWidth: 12 } } },
      scales: { r: { min: 0, max: 100, angleLines: { color: grid }, grid: { color: grid }, pointLabels: { color: text, font: { size: 11 } }, ticks: { display: false } } },
    },
  });
  registry.add(chart);
  return chart;
}

export function setMarker(chart, index) {
  if (!chart) return;
  chart.options.plugins.marker.index = index;
  chart.update('none');
}

/** Labels for an hourly grid starting at grid.start */
export function hourLabels(points, from, to) {
  return points.slice(from, to).map((p) => {
    const d = new Date(p.t);
    const day = Math.floor((d - new Date(points[0].t)) / 86400000) + 1;
    return `${t('unit.dayShort')}${day} ${d.toISOString().slice(11, 16)}`;
  });
}

# FILE 4/38: public/src/components/assistantPanel.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Integrated Mission Assistant UI.
 *
 * One conversation, two surfaces: the floating panel (available on every screen)
 * and the full "AI Assistant" screen. Both render the same history, the same
 * "Popular Questions" chips and the same input; answers come from services/assistant.js,
 * which reads the CURRENT mission analysis (LLM mode when the server has a key,
 * rule-based otherwise).
 */
import { esc, icons } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { ask } from '../services/assistant.js';

export const POPULAR = ['ai.pq.best', 'ai.pq.ice', 'ai.pq.illum', 'ai.pq.risks', 'ai.pq.comm', 'ai.q.date', 'ai.q.power', 'ai.q.chart'];
const history = []; // [{role:'user'|'bot', text}]
let busy = false;
let isOpen = false;

function msgsHTML() {
  return `<div class="msg bot">${esc(t('ai.welcome'))}</div>${history.map((m) => `<div class="msg ${m.role}">${esc(m.text)}</div>`).join('')}${busy ? `<div class="msg bot typing" aria-label="${esc(t('ai.thinking'))}"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>` : ''}`;
}

/** Chat block shared by the floating panel and the AI Assistant screen. */
export function chatHTML(variant) {
  return `
  <div class="chat chat-${variant}">
    <div class="msgs" role="log" aria-live="polite">${msgsHTML()}</div>
    <div class="popular">
      <div class="popular-title">${esc(t('ai.popular'))}</div>
      <div class="chips">${POPULAR.slice(0, variant === 'panel' ? 5 : POPULAR.length).map((k) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-ask="${esc(t(k))}">${esc(t(k))}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    </div>
    <form class="chat-form">
      <label class="sr-only" for="chat-input-${variant}">${esc(t('ai.placeholder'))}</label>
      <input id="chat-input-${variant}" class="chat-input" autocomplete="off" placeholder="${esc(t('ai.placeholder'))}" ${busy ? 'disabled' : ''}/>
      <button class="btn primary" type="submit" aria-label="${esc(t('ai.send'))}" ${busy ? 'disabled' : ''}>${icons.send}</button>
    </form>
  </div>`;
}

export function engineLabel() {
  return window.__lunarHealth?.llmAssistant ? t('ai.engine.llm') : t('ai.engine.rules');
}

export function renderAssistant(route) {
  if (route === 'assistant') return ''; // the full screen replaces the floating panel
  return `
  <button class="assistant-fab no-print" data-action="assistant-toggle" aria-expanded="${isOpen}" aria-controls="assistant-panel">
    ${icons.spark}<span class="label-text">${esc(t('ai.title'))}</span>
  </button>
  <section class="assistant ${isOpen ? 'open' : ''}" id="assistant-panel" aria-label="${esc(t('ai.title'))}">
    <header>
      <div><b>${esc(t('ai.title'))}</b><div class="tiny muted">${esc(engineLabel())}</div></div>
      <div class="row" style="gap:6px">
        <a class="icon-btn" href="#assistant" aria-label="${esc(t('ai.expand'))}" title="${esc(t('ai.expand'))}">${icons.ext}</a>
        <button class="icon-btn" data-action="assistant-toggle" aria-label="${esc(t('common.close'))}">${icons.close}</button>
      </div>
    </header>
    ${chatHTML('panel')}
  </section>`;
}

function refresh(root) {
  root.querySelectorAll('.chat .msgs').forEach((el) => {
    el.innerHTML = msgsHTML();
    el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('.chat-form input, .chat-form button').forEach((el) => (el.disabled = busy));
}

export async function submitQuestion(root, q) {
  q = String(q || '').trim();
  if (!q || busy) return;
  history.push({ role: 'user', text: q });
  busy = true;
  refresh(root);
  try {
    const { text } = await ask(q);
    history.push({ role: 'bot', text });
  } catch (e) {
    history.push({ role: 'bot', text: `${t('ai.error')}: ${e.message}` });
  } finally {
    busy = false;
    refresh(root);
    root.querySelector('.chat-input:not([disabled])')?.focus();
  }
}

export function bindAssistant(root) {
  const toggle = () => {
    isOpen = !isOpen;
    root.querySelector('#assistant-panel')?.classList.toggle('open', isOpen);
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-action="assistant-toggle"]').forEach((b) => b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen));
    if (isOpen) {
      refresh(root);
      root.querySelector('#assistant-panel .chat-input')?.focus();
    }
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const tog = e.target.closest('[data-action="assistant-toggle"]');
    if (tog) return toggle();
    const chip = e.target.closest('[data-ask]');
    if (chip) submitQuestion(root, chip.dataset.ask);
  });
  root.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
    const form = e.target.closest('.chat-form');
    if (!form) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const input = form.querySelector('.chat-input');
    const q = input.value;
    input.value = '';
    submitQuestion(root, q);
  });
}

/** Open the floating panel (or the full screen) and ask a question. */
export function openAssistantWith(question) {
  const root = document.getElementById('app');
  if (!document.querySelector('.chat-page') && !isOpen) document.querySelector('.assistant-fab')?.click();
  submitQuestion(root, question);
}

/** Workflow order = the mission data flow */
export const NAV_GROUPS = [
  ['nav.group.plan', [['planner', 'nav.planner'], ['dashboard', 'nav.dashboard']]],
  ['nav.group.analyze', [['analysis', 'nav.analysis'], ['explorer', 'nav.explorer'], ['compare', 'nav.compare'], ['risk', 'nav.risk']]],
  ['nav.group.deliver', [['reports', 'nav.reports'], ['assistant', 'nav.assistant']]],
  ['nav.group.reference', [['resources', 'nav.resources'], ['settings', 'nav.settings']]],
];
export const NAV_ITEMS = NAV_GROUPS.flatMap(([, items]) => items);

const PAGE_TITLES = {
  planner: 'nav.planner', dashboard: 'nav.dashboard', analysis: 'nav.analysis', explorer: 'nav.explorer',
  compare: 'nav.compare', risk: 'nav.risk', reports: 'nav.reports', resources: 'nav.resources', settings: 'nav.settings', assistant: 'nav.assistant',
};

function statusDot(src) {
  const color = { LIVE: 'var(--live)', ESTIMATED: 'var(--est)', DEMO: 'var(--demo)', IMPORTED: 'var(--imported)', MIXED: 'var(--imported)', EXTERNAL: 'var(--text-3)', UNAVAILABLE: 'var(--bad)' }[src] || 'var(--text-3)';
  return `<i class="dot" style="background:${color}"></i>`;
}

function sidebar(route) {
  const { mission: m, analysis: a } = getState();
  const site = presetById(m.siteId) ? t(presetById(m.siteId).nameKey) : m.siteName || t('site.custom');
  let step = 0;
  const groups = NAV_GROUPS.map(([gk, items]) => `
    <div class="side-group">
      <div class="side-label">${esc(t(gk))}</div>
      ${items.map(([r, k]) => {
        const n = gk === 'nav.group.reference' ? '' : String(++step).padStart(2, '0');
        return `<a href="#${r}" class="side-link ${route === r ? 'active' : ''}" ${route === r ? 'aria-current="page"' : ''}>
          ${icon(r)}<span>${esc(t(k))}</span>${n ? `<em>${n}</em>` : ''}</a>`;
      }).join('')}
    </div>`).join('');

  const eph = a?.sources?.ephemeris;
  const sources = RESOURCES.map((r) => {
    const st = r.id === 'horizons' ? (eph === 'LIVE' ? 'LIVE' : 'UNAVAILABLE') : r.status;
    return `<a class="side-src" href="${r.url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" title="${esc(t(`src.${st}`))}">${statusDot(st)}<span>${esc(r.name)}</span>${icons.ext}</a>`;
  }).join('');

  return `
  <aside class="sidebar" id="sidebar" aria-label="${esc(t('nav.main'))}">
    <a class="brand serif-brand" href="#dashboard" aria-label="Lunar AI">${crescent}<span>Lunar AI</span></a>
    <a class="mission-chip" href="#planner">
      <span class="tiny muted upper">${esc(t('nav.activeMission'))}</span>
      <b>${esc(m.name || t('dash.untitled'))}</b>
      <span class="tiny muted">${esc(site)}</span>
      ${a ? `<span class="mini-score"><span class="mono">${num(a.scores.overall, 0)}</span>/100 <span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('metric.overall'))}</span></span>` : `<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('status.draft'))}</span>`}
    </a>
    <img class="side-moon" alt="" src="${moonImage(180)}"/>
    <div class="side-sources">
      <div class="side-label">${esc(t('nav.nasaSources'))}</div>
      ${sources}
    </div>
  </aside>
  <div class="side-backdrop" data-action="menu"></div>`;
}

let clockTimer = null;
export function startClock() {
  clearInterval(clockTimer);
  const tick = () => {
    const el = document.getElementById('utc-clock');
    if (el) el.textContent = new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19);
  };
  tick();
  clockTimer = setInterval(tick, 1000);
}

# FILE 6/38: public/src/components/sourcesPanel.js

import { esc, icons } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { sourceBadge } from './ui.js';
import { utc } from '../utils/format.js';
import { RESOURCES } from '../pages/resources.js';

/**
 * "Data Sources" panel on every analysis page: all five official NASA/JPL sources,
 * each with its real integration status for THIS analysis — never a false "live" claim.
 */
export function sourcesPanel(analysis) {
  const s = analysis?.sources || {};
  const eph = s.ephemeris || 'UNAVAILABLE';
  const terr = s.terrain || 'DEMO';
  const statusFor = (id) => {
    if (id === 'horizons') return eph === 'LIVE' ? 'LIVE' : 'UNAVAILABLE';
    if (id === 'trek' || id === 'quickmap') return terr === 'DEMO' ? 'EXTERNAL' : 'IMPORTED';
    if (id === 'lro') return 'IMPORTED';
    return 'EXTERNAL';
  };
  return `
  <div class="card">
    <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('sources.title'))}</span>
      <a class="tiny" href="#resources">${esc(t('nav.resources'))} →</a></div>
    <div class="src-grid">
      ${RESOURCES.map((r) => `
        <a class="src-item" href="${r.url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
          <span class="row between" style="flex-wrap:nowrap"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b>${icons.ext}</span>
          ${sourceBadge(statusFor(r.id))}
          <span class="tiny muted">${esc(t(`sources.role.${r.id}`))}</span>
        </a>`).join('')}
      <div class="src-item">
        <b>${esc(t('sources.ephemerisModel'))}</b>
        ${eph === 'ESTIMATED' ? sourceBadge('ESTIMATED') : `<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('sources.standby'))}</span>`}
        <span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('sources.role.meeus'))}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="src-item">
        <b>${esc(t('sources.terrain'))}</b>
        ${sourceBadge(terr === 'MIXED' ? 'MIXED' : terr)}
        <span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('sources.role.terrain'))}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <p class="tiny muted mt-s">${esc(sourceExplanation(analysis))}</p>
  </div>`;
}

export function sourceExplanation(analysis) {
  const s = analysis?.sources || {};
  const parts = [];
  if (s.ephemeris === 'LIVE') parts.push(t('sources.exp.live', { time: utc(s.ephemerisMeta?.retrievedAt) }));
  else if (s.ephemerisReason === 'LIVE_FAILED') parts.push(t('sources.exp.liveFailed', { err: s.liveError || '' }));
  else if (s.ephemerisReason === 'DEMO_MODE') parts.push(t('sources.exp.demo'));
  else parts.push(t('sources.exp.noBackend'));
  parts.push(s.terrain === 'IMPORTED' ? t('sources.exp.terrainImported') : s.terrain === 'MIXED' ? t('sources.exp.terrainMixed') : t('sources.exp.terrainDemo'));
  return parts.join(' ');
}

/** Top-of-page status banner */
export function dataBanner(analysis) {
  const s = analysis?.sources || {};
  if (s.demoMode) return `<div class="banner demo" role="note">${esc(t('banner.demo'))}</div>`;
  if (s.ephemeris === 'LIVE') return `<div class="banner live" role="note">${esc(t('banner.live'))}</div>`;
  if (s.ephemerisReason === 'LIVE_FAILED') return `<div class="banner est" role="note">${esc(t('banner.liveFailed'))}</div>`;
  return `<div class="banner est" role="note">${esc(t('banner.estimated'))}</div>`;
}

# FILE 7/38: public/src/components/ui.js

import { esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';

export function toast(message, type = 'info', { force = false } = {}) {
  if (!force && !getState().settings.notifications && type !== 'error') return;
  const host = document.getElementById('toasts');
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = `toast ${type}`;
  el.textContent = message;
  host.appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => el.remove(), type === 'error' ? 7000 : 4200);
}

let loaderEl = null;
export function showLoader(text) {
  hideLoader();
  loaderEl = document.createElement('div');
  loaderEl.className = 'loader-overlay';
  loaderEl.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
  loaderEl.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
  loaderEl.innerHTML = `<div class="spinner"></div><div class="muted" id="loader-text">${esc(text)}</div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(loaderEl);
}
export function updateLoader(text) {
  const el = document.getElementById('loader-text');
  if (el) el.textContent = text;
}
export function hideLoader() {
  loaderEl?.remove();
  loaderEl = null;
}

/** Accessible modal; resolves true/false (confirm) or undefined (info). */
export function modal({ title, body, confirmText, cancelText, danger = false, info = false }) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const prevFocus = document.activeElement;
    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className = 'modal-backdrop';
    wrap.innerHTML = `
      <div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="modal-title">
        <h2 id="modal-title">${esc(title)}</h2>
        <div class="muted mt-s" style="margin-bottom:18px">${body}</div>
        <div class="row" style="justify-content:flex-end">
          ${info ? '' : `<button class="btn ghost" data-act="cancel">${esc(cancelText || t('common.cancel'))}</button>`}
          <button class="btn ${danger ? 'danger' : 'primary'}" data-act="ok">${esc(confirmText || t('common.ok'))}</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    const close = (v) => {
      wrap.remove();
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      prevFocus?.focus?.();
      resolve(v);
    };
    const onKey = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') close(false);
    };
    wrap.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === wrap) close(false);
      const act = e.target.closest('[data-act]')?.dataset.act;
      if (act) close(act === 'ok');
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    document.body.appendChild(wrap);
    wrap.querySelector('[data-act="ok"]').focus();
  });
}

export function sourceBadge(source) {
  const s = source || 'UNAVAILABLE';
  const label = t(`src.${s}`);
  return `<span class="badge ${esc(s)}" title="${esc(t(`src.${s}.tip`))}">${esc(label)}</span>`;
}

export function levelChip(level) {
  return `<span class="level ${esc(level)}">${esc(t(`risk.level.${level}`))}</span>`;
}

export function emptyState(titleKey, textKey, actionHref, actionKey) {
  return `<div class="card empty">
    <h2>${esc(t(titleKey))}</h2>
    <p>${esc(t(textKey))}</p>
    <a class="btn primary" href="${actionHref}">${esc(t(actionKey))}</a>
  </div>`;
}

export function scoreRing(score, { size = 190, label = '' } = {}) {
  const r = 78;
  const c = 2 * Math.PI * r;
  const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score || 0));
  const color = v >= 70 ? 'var(--ok)' : v >= 40 ? 'var(--warn)' : 'var(--bad)';
  return `<div class="score-ring" style="width:${size}px;height:${size}px" role="img" aria-label="${esc(label)} ${Math.round(v)} / 100">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 180 180"><circle cx="90" cy="90" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="var(--border)" stroke-width="12"/>
    <circle cx="90" cy="90" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="12" stroke-linecap="round"
      stroke-dasharray="${(c * v) / 100} ${c}"/></svg>
    <div class="center"><div class="num">${Math.round(v)}</div><div class="den">/ 100</div></div>
  </div>`;
}

/**
 * Preset landing-site candidates.
 *
 * Coordinates are APPROXIMATE feature locations (rounded) for convenience only.
 * Verify exact coordinates in NASA QuickMap (https://quickmap.lroc.asu.edu/)
 * or NASA Moon Trek (https://trek.nasa.gov/moon/) before using them for planning.
 * Longitudes are selenographic EAST (negative = west).
 */
export const PRESET_SITES = [
  { id: 'shackleton', region: 'south', nameKey: 'site.shackleton', lat: -89.67, lon: 129.78 },
  { id: 'degerlache', region: 'south', nameKey: 'site.degerlache', lat: -88.5, lon: -87.1 },
  { id: 'haworth', region: 'south', nameKey: 'site.haworth', lat: -87.4, lon: -5.0 },
  { id: 'malapert', region: 'south', nameKey: 'site.malapert', lat: -86.0, lon: 2.9 },
  { id: 'cabeus', region: 'south', nameKey: 'site.cabeus', lat: -85.3, lon: -42.1 },
  { id: 'peary', region: 'north', nameKey: 'site.peary', lat: 88.6, lon: 33.0 },
  { id: 'hermite', region: 'north', nameKey: 'site.hermite', lat: 86.0, lon: -89.9 },
];

export function presetById(id) {
  return PRESET_SITES.find((s) => s.id === id);
}

######################################################################
# FILE 11/38: public/src/i18n/i18n.js
######################################################################

import { en } from './en.js';
import { ar } from './ar.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';

const DICTS = { en, ar };
const missing = new Set();

export function lang() {
  return getState().settings.lang === 'ar' ? 'ar' : 'en';
}

/** Translate key with {var} interpolation. Never mixes languages: falls back to the key name. */
export function t(key, vars = {}) {
  const d = DICTS[lang()];
  let s = d[key];
  if (s === undefined) {
    if (!missing.has(key)) {
      missing.add(key);
      console.warn(`[i18n] missing ${lang()} key: ${key}`);
    }
    s = key;
  }
  return s.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, k) => (vars[k] !== undefined ? vars[k] : `{${k}}`));
}

export function applyDocumentLang() {
  const l = lang();
  document.documentElement.lang = l;
  document.documentElement.dir = l === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
  document.title = t('app.title');
}

export function missingKeys() {
  return [...missing];
}


######################################################################
# FILE 12/38: public/src/main.js
######################################################################

/**
 * LUNAR AI — application entry: router, shell, global subscriptions.
 */
import { getState, setState, subscribe, updateSettings, resetSession } from './state/store.js';
import { applyDocumentLang, t } from './i18n/i18n.js';
import { renderShell, startClock } from './components/navbar.js';
import { renderAssistant, bindAssistant } from './components/assistantPanel.js';
import { initBackend, verifySession, logout } from './services/api.js';
import { analyzeCurrentMission, refreshHistory } from './services/missionService.js';
import { destroyCharts } from './charts/charts.js';
import * as auth from './pages/auth.js';
import * as planner from './pages/planner.js';
import * as dashboard from './pages/dashboard.js';
import * as analysis from './pages/analysis.js';
import * as explorer from './pages/explorer.js';
import * as compare from './pages/compare.js';
import * as risk from './pages/risk.js';
import * as reports from './pages/reports.js';
import * as resources from './pages/resources.js';
import * as settings from './pages/settings.js';
import * as assistant from './pages/assistant.js';

const PAGES = { auth, planner, dashboard, analysis, explorer, compare, risk, reports, resources, settings, assistant };
const app = document.getElementById('app');
let cleanup = null;
let currentRoute = null;

function applyTheme() {
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = getState().settings.theme === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark';
}

function drawStars() {
  // Deep-space star field: thousands of faint stars, a few bright ones with soft glow,
  // and a faint diagonal Milky-Way haze. Drawn once per resize.
  const c = document.getElementById('starfield');
  const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
  const w = innerWidth;
  const h = innerHeight;
  c.width = w * dpr;
  c.height = h * dpr;
  c.style.width = '100%';
  c.style.height = '100%';
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
  let seed = 42;
  const rnd = () => ((seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647);
  // milky-way haze
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(w * 0.5, h * 0.5);
  ctx.rotate(-0.5);
  const band = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, -h * 0.35, 0, h * 0.35);
  band.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(120,150,255,0)');
  band.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(140,165,255,0.07)');
  band.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(120,150,255,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = band;
  ctx.fillRect(-w, -h * 0.35, w * 2, h * 0.7);
  ctx.restore();
  const n = Math.round((w * h) / 1400);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const x = rnd() * w;
    const y = rnd() * h;
    const big = rnd();
    const r = big > 0.985 ? 1.4 : big > 0.9 ? 0.9 : 0.55;
    const tint = rnd();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25 + rnd() * 0.65;
    ctx.fillStyle = tint < 0.12 ? '#bcd3ff' : tint < 0.18 ? '#ffe9c4' : '#ffffff';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    if (big > 0.993) {
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, 9);
      g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(190,210,255,0.55)');
      g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(190,210,255,0)');
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.fillStyle = g;
      ctx.fillRect(x - 9, y - 9, 18, 18);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(220,232,255,0.6)';
      ctx.fillRect(x - 6, y - 0.4, 12, 0.8);
      ctx.fillRect(x - 0.4, y - 6, 0.8, 12);
    }
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}

function parseRoute() {
  const r = location.hash.replace(/^#\/?/, '').split('?')[0] || 'dashboard';
  return PAGES[r] ? r : 'dashboard';
}

function render() {
  let route = parseRoute();
  const { user } = getState();
  if (!user && route !== 'auth') route = 'auth';
  if (user && route === 'auth') {
    location.replace('#planner');
    return;
  }
  cleanup?.();
  destroyCharts();
  cleanup = null;
  currentRoute = route;
  setState({ route }, { silent: true });

  if (route === 'auth') {
    app.innerHTML = '<div id="page-root"></div>';
  } else {
    app.innerHTML = `${renderShell(route)}${renderAssistant(route)}`;
    startClock();
  }
  const pageRoot = document.getElementById('page-root');
  cleanup = PAGES[route].render(pageRoot) || null;
  // keep the active top tab visible on narrow screens
  const activeTab = document.querySelector('.toptab.active');
  if (activeTab) activeTab.parentElement.scrollLeft = activeTab.offsetLeft - activeTab.parentElement.clientWidth / 2 + activeTab.clientWidth / 2;
}

// ---- global UI events (navbar, assistant) ----
bindAssistant(app);
app.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-action]');
  const dd = document.getElementById('profile-dropdown');
  if (!el) {
    dd?.classList.remove('open');
    return;
  }
  const a = el.dataset.action;
  if (a === 'toggle-lang') updateSettings({ lang: getState().settings.lang === 'ar' ? 'en' : 'ar' });
  if (a === 'toggle-theme') updateSettings({ theme: getState().settings.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark' });
  if (a === 'profile') {
    dd?.classList.toggle('open');
    el.setAttribute('aria-expanded', dd?.classList.contains('open'));
  }
  if (a === 'menu') {
    const open = document.body.classList.toggle('side-open');
    document.querySelector('.hamburger')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open);
  }
  if (a === 'logout') {
    await logout();
    resetSession();
    location.hash = '#auth';
  }
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
    document.getElementById('profile-dropdown')?.classList.remove('open');
    document.body.classList.remove('side-open');
  }
});
window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => {
  document.body.classList.remove('side-open');
  render();
  document.getElementById('page-root')?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  window.scrollTo(0, 0);
});
let resizeT;
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  clearTimeout(resizeT);
  resizeT = setTimeout(drawStars, 200);
});

// ---- react to state changes ----
const ANALYTIC_SETTINGS = ['demoMode', 'panelType', 'panelEfficiency', 'panelAreaM2', 'minDteElevation', 'weightsPreset'];
let reanalyzeT;
subscribe((s, prev) => {
  if (s.settings.lang !== prev.settings.lang) applyDocumentLang();
  if (s.settings.theme !== prev.settings.theme) applyTheme();
  if (!s.user && prev.user) return render();
  const settingsChanged = s.settings !== prev.settings;
  if (settingsChanged && s.analysis && ANALYTIC_SETTINGS.some((k) => s.settings[k] !== prev.settings[k])) {
    clearTimeout(reanalyzeT);
    reanalyzeT = setTimeout(() => analyzeCurrentMission(), 500);
  }
  const historyOnHome = s.history !== prev.history && currentRoute === 'dashboard';
  if (historyOnHome || settingsChanged || s.mission !== prev.mission || s.analysis !== prev.analysis || s.comparison !== prev.comparison) {
    // keep scroll position on in-place re-render
    const y = window.scrollY;
    render();
    window.scrollTo(0, y);
  }
});

// ---- boot ----
(async function boot() {
  applyTheme();
  applyDocumentLang();
  drawStars();
  await initBackend();
  if (getState().user && !(await verifySession())) resetSession();
  if (getState().user) refreshHistory();
  if (!location.hash) location.replace(getState().user ? '#planner' : '#auth');
  render();
  window.__lunar = { getState, t, currentRoute: () => currentRoute }; // debugging hook
})();


######################################################################
# FILE 13/38: public/src/pages/analysis.js
######################################################################

import { esc, icons, cssVar } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { emptyState, sourceBadge, levelChip } from '../components/ui.js';
import { sourcesPanel, dataBanner } from '../components/sourcesPanel.js';
import { lineChart, destroyCharts, setMarker, hourLabels } from '../charts/charts.js';
import { num, deg, pct, elev, hm } from '../utils/format.js';
import { riskLevel } from '../services/analysisEngine.js';

const TIMELINE_HOURS = 24;

function stat(label, value, src) {
  return `<div class="card metric"><div class="label"><span>${esc(label)}</span>${src ? sourceBadge(src) : ''}</div><div class="value" style="font-size:1.25rem">${value}</div></div>`;
}

/** Horizon panorama: azimuth 0–360 on x, elevation on y; terrain mask, Sun and Earth disks. */
export function skyPanorama(a, idx) {
  const p = a.points[idx];
  const W = 720;
  const H = 260;
  const elMin = Math.min(-4, Math.floor(Math.min(p.sunEl, p.earthEl) - 2));
  const elMax = Math.max(12, Math.ceil(Math.max(p.sunEl, p.earthEl, ...a.terrain.horizonProfile) + 3));
  const x = (az) => (az / 360) * W;
  const y = (el) => H - ((el - elMin) / (elMax - elMin)) * H;
  const prof = [...a.terrain.horizonProfile, a.terrain.horizonProfile[0]];
  const terrain = `M0,${H} ` + prof.map((h, i) => `L${x(i * 10)},${y(h)}`).join(' ') + ` L${W},${H} Z`;
  const ticks = [0, 90, 180, 270, 360];
  const dirs = ['dir.N', 'dir.E', 'dir.S', 'dir.W', 'dir.N'].map((k) => t(k));
  const elTicks = [];
  for (let e = Math.ceil(elMin / 2) * 2; e <= elMax; e += 2) elTicks.push(e);
  const sunR = Math.max(5, (0.27 / (elMax - elMin)) * H);
  const earthR = Math.max(6, (0.95 / (elMax - elMin)) * H);
  return `<svg viewBox="-34 -8 ${W + 44} ${H + 30}" role="img" aria-label="${esc(t('analysis.skyAria'))}" style="direction:ltr">
    <defs><linearGradient id="skyg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="var(--sky-top)"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="var(--sky-bottom)"/></linearGradient></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="url(#skyg)"/>
    ${elTicks.map((e) => `<line x1="0" x2="${W}" y1="${y(e)}" y2="${y(e)}" stroke="var(--chart-grid)"/><text x="-6" y="${y(e) + 3}" font-size="10" text-anchor="end" fill="var(--chart-text)" font-family="Inter">${e}°</text>`).join('')}
    <line x1="0" x2="${W}" y1="${y(0)}" y2="${y(0)}" stroke="var(--text-3)" stroke-dasharray="4 4"/>
    <path d="${terrain}" fill="var(--regolith)" stroke="var(--regolith-edge)" stroke-width="1.2"/>
    ${ticks.map((az, i) => `<text x="${x(az)}" y="${H + 16}" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle" fill="var(--chart-text)" font-family="Inter">${dirs[i]} ${az}°</text>`).join('')}
    <g><circle cx="${x(p.sunAz)}" cy="${y(p.sunEl)}" r="${sunR}" fill="var(--sun)" opacity="${p.sunFrac > 0 ? 1 : 0.35}"/>
      <text x="${x(p.sunAz)}" y="${y(p.sunEl) - sunR - 5}" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle" fill="var(--text)">${esc(t('common.sun'))}</text></g>
    <g><circle cx="${x(p.earthAz)}" cy="${y(p.earthEl)}" r="${earthR}" fill="var(--earth)" opacity="${p.earthVisible ? 1 : 0.35}"/>
      <text x="${x(p.earthAz)}" y="${y(p.earthEl) - earthR - 5}" font-size="11" text-anchor="middle" fill="var(--text)">${esc(t('common.earth'))}</text></g>
  </svg>`;
}

export function render(root) {
  const { analysis: a, settings } = getState();
  if (!a) {
    root.innerHTML = `<div class="page">${emptyState('empty.title', 'empty.text', '#planner', 'empty.action')}</div>`;
    return () => {};
  }
  const m = a.metrics;
  const eph = a.sources.ephemeris;
  const terr = a.sources.terrain;
  const P = a.points;
  const end = Math.max(a.grid.endIdx + 1, TIMELINE_HOURS + 1);
  const labels = hourLabels(P, 0, end);
  const slice = (fn) => P.slice(0, end).map(fn);
  let hour = Math.min(a.grid.startIdx, TIMELINE_HOURS);
  const e = elev(a.terrain.elevationM);
  const minDte = Number(settings.minDteElevation);

  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    ${dataBanner(a)}
    <div class="page-head"><div><p class="sub">${esc(t('analysis.subtitle'))}</p></div></div>

    <div class="section-title"><span class="tag">A</span><h2>${esc(t('analysis.solar'))}</h2></div>
    <div class="grid g3">
      ${stat(t('metric.sunEl'), deg(m.sunEl), eph)}
      ${stat(t('metric.sunAz'), deg(m.sunAz, 1), eph)}
      ${stat(t('analysis.sunVis'), `${m.sunVisible ? esc(t('common.yes')) : esc(t('common.no'))} <small>(${pct(P[a.grid.startIdx].sunFrac)} ${esc(t('analysis.disk'))})</small>`, eph)}
      ${stat(t('metric.sunlight'), `${num(m.sunlightHours, 0)}<small>${esc(t('unit.h'))} / ${num(m.windowHours, 0)}${esc(t('unit.h'))}</small>`, eph)}
      ${stat(m.sunVisible ? t('analysis.untilSunset') : t('analysis.untilSunrise'), m.sunVisible ? (m.timeUntilSunsetH === null ? esc(t('common.beyondWindow')) : `${num(m.timeUntilSunsetH, 0)}<small>${esc(t('unit.h'))}</small>`) : (m.timeUntilSunriseH === null ? esc(t('common.beyondWindow')) : `${num(m.timeUntilSunriseH, 0)}<small>${esc(t('unit.h'))}</small>`), eph)}
      ${stat(t('metric.power'), `${num(m.avgPowerW, 0)}<small>W ${esc(t('analysis.avg'))}</small>`, 'ESTIMATED')}
    </div>
    <div class="grid g3 mt">
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.sunEl'))}</div><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="c-sunel"></canvas></div></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.sunAz'))}</div><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="c-sunaz"></canvas></div></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.sunlight'))}</div><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="c-sunlight"></canvas></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="section-title"><span class="tag">B</span><h2>${esc(t('analysis.earth'))}</h2></div>
    <div class="grid g3">
      ${stat(t('metric.earthEl'), deg(m.earthEl), eph)}
      ${stat(t('analysis.earthAz'), deg(m.earthAz, 1), eph)}
      ${stat(t('metric.earthVis'), `${m.earthVisible ? esc(t('common.yes')) : esc(t('common.no'))} <small>(${pct(m.earthVisibleFraction)} ${esc(t('analysis.ofWindow'))})</small>`, eph)}
      ${stat(t('analysis.earthset'), m.earthVisible ? (m.earthsetH === null ? esc(t('common.beyondWindow')) : `${num(m.earthsetH, 0)}<small>${esc(t('unit.h'))}</small>`) : esc(t('analysis.belowHorizon')), eph)}
      ${stat(t('metric.comm'), `${num(m.commLongestH, 0)}<small>${esc(t('unit.h'))} ${esc(t('analysis.longest'))}</small>`, eph)}
      ${stat(t('analysis.dte'), `${pct(m.dteFraction)} <small>${esc(t('analysis.minEl', { v: num(minDte, 1) }))}</small>`, eph)}
    </div>
    <div class="grid g3 mt">
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.earthEl'))}</div><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="c-earthel"></canvas></div></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.earthVis'))}</div><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="c-earthvis"></canvas></div></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.comm'))}</div><div class="chart-box"><canvas id="c-comm"></canvas></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="section-title"><span class="tag">C</span><h2>${esc(t('analysis.terrain'))}</h2></div>
    <div class="grid g4">
      ${stat(t('metric.elevation'), `${e.v}<small>${e.u}</small>`, a.sources.terrainFields.elevation)}
      ${stat(t('metric.slope'), deg(a.terrain.slopeDeg, 1), a.sources.terrainFields.slope)}
      ${stat(t('analysis.complexity'), `${num(a.terrain.roughness, 2)} <small>${levelChip(riskLevel(100 - a.terrain.roughness * 100))}</small>`, a.sources.terrainFields.roughness)}
      ${stat(t('analysis.horizonObs'), deg(a.terrain.horizonDeg, 1), a.sources.terrainFields.horizon)}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt"><div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.horizon'))}</span>${sourceBadge(terr === 'IMPORTED' ? 'IMPORTED' : 'DEMO')}</div>
      <div class="chart-box"><canvas id="c-horizon"></canvas></div></div>

    <div class="section-title"><span class="tag">D</span><h2>${esc(t('analysis.timeline'))}</h2></div>
    <div class="grid g3">
      <div class="card span2">
        <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('analysis.sky'))}</span><span class="mono small" id="tl-time"></span></div>
        <div class="sky" id="sky"></div>
        <div class="timeline mt">
          <div class="row">
            <button class="btn sm" id="tl-play" aria-label="${esc(t('analysis.play'))}">${icons.play}</button>
            <label class="sr-only" for="tl-range">${esc(t('analysis.timeline'))}</label>
            <input type="range" id="tl-range" min="0" max="${TIMELINE_HOURS}" step="1" value="${hour}" style="flex:1" aria-valuetext="${hm(hour)}"/>
          </div>
          <div class="ticks">${[0, 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24].map((h) => `<button class="chip" data-hour="${h}">${h === 24 ? '24:00' : hm(h)}</button>`).join('')}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('analysis.state'))}</span></div><dl class="kv" id="tl-state"></dl></div>
    </div>

    <div class="mt">${sourcesPanel(a)}</div>
  </div>`;

  const c1 = cssVar('--c1');
  const c2 = cssVar('--c2');
  const c3 = cssVar('--c3');
  const c4 = cssVar('--c4');
  const textMuted = cssVar('--text-3');
  const charts = [
    lineChart(root.querySelector('#c-sunel'), { labels, yTitle: '°', marker: hour, datasets: [
      { label: t('chart.sunEl'), data: slice((p) => p.sunEl), color: c2 },
      { label: t('chart.terrainMask'), data: slice((p) => p.sunMask), color: textMuted, dash: true },
    ] }),
    lineChart(root.querySelector('#c-sunaz'), { labels, yTitle: '°', yMin: 0, yMax: 360, marker: hour, datasets: [{ label: t('chart.sunAz'), data: slice((p) => p.sunAz), color: c2 }] }),
    lineChart(root.querySelector('#c-sunlight'), { labels, yTitle: '%', yMin: 0, yMax: 100, marker: hour, datasets: [
      { label: t('chart.sunlight'), data: slice((p) => p.sunFrac * 100), color: c2, fill: true, stepped: true },
    ] }),
    lineChart(root.querySelector('#c-earthel'), { labels, yTitle: '°', marker: hour, datasets: [
      { label: t('chart.earthEl'), data: slice((p) => p.earthEl), color: c1 },
      { label: t('chart.dteThreshold'), data: slice((p) => p.earthMask + minDte), color: textMuted, dash: true },
    ] }),
    lineChart(root.querySelector('#c-earthvis'), { labels, yTitle: '%', yMin: 0, yMax: 100, marker: hour, datasets: [
      { label: t('chart.earthVis'), data: slice((p) => p.earthFrac * 100), color: c1, fill: true },
    ] }),
    lineChart(root.querySelector('#c-comm'), { labels, yMin: 0, yMax: 1.05, marker: hour, datasets: [
      { label: t('chart.comm'), data: slice((p) => (p.dte ? 1 : 0)), color: c4, fill: true, stepped: true },
      { label: t('chart.power'), data: slice((p) => p.powerW / (m.idealW || 1)), color: c3, dash: true },
    ] }),
  ];
  lineChart(root.querySelector('#c-horizon'), {
    labels: a.terrain.horizonProfile.map((_, i) => `${i * 10}°`),
    yTitle: '°',
    yMin: 0,
    datasets: [{ label: t('chart.horizon'), data: a.terrain.horizonProfile, color: c3, fill: true }],
  });

  const update = () => {
    const p = P[hour];
    root.querySelector('#sky').innerHTML = skyPanorama(a, hour);
    root.querySelector('#tl-time').textContent = `${a.grid.start.slice(0, 10)} ${hour === 24 ? '24:00' : hm(hour)} UTC`;
    const range = root.querySelector('#tl-range');
    range.value = hour;
    range.setAttribute('aria-valuetext', hm(hour));
    root.querySelector('#tl-state').innerHTML = `
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.sunEl'))}</dt><dd>${deg(p.sunEl)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.sunAz'))}</dt><dd>${deg(p.sunAz, 1)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('analysis.sunVis'))}</dt><dd>${pct(p.sunFrac)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.power'))}</dt><dd>${num(p.powerW, 0)} W</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.earthEl'))}</dt><dd>${deg(p.earthEl)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('analysis.earthAz'))}</dt><dd>${deg(p.earthAz, 1)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.earthVis'))}</dt><dd>${esc(p.earthVisible ? t('common.yes') : t('common.no'))}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('analysis.dte'))}</dt><dd style="color:${p.dte ? 'var(--ok)' : 'var(--bad)'}">${esc(p.dte ? t('analysis.available') : t('analysis.unavailable'))}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('analysis.phase'))}</dt><dd>${esc(hour < a.grid.startIdx ? t('analysis.preMission') : t('analysis.inMission'))}</dd>`;
    charts.forEach((c) => setMarker(c, hour));
  };
  update();

  root.querySelector('#tl-range').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    hour = Number(e.target.value);
    update();
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-hour]').forEach((b) =>
    b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      hour = Number(b.dataset.hour);
      update();
    }),
  );
  let timer = null;
  const playBtn = root.querySelector('#tl-play');
  playBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (timer) {
      clearInterval(timer);
      timer = null;
      playBtn.innerHTML = icons.play;
      return;
    }
    playBtn.innerHTML = icons.pause;
    timer = setInterval(() => {
      hour = (hour + 1) % (TIMELINE_HOURS + 1);
      update();
    }, 450);
  });

  return () => {
    clearInterval(timer);
    destroyCharts();
  };
}

# FILE 14/38: public/src/pages/assistant.js

import { esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { chatHTML, engineLabel } from '../components/assistantPanel.js';
import { sourceBadge } from '../components/ui.js';
import { moonImage } from '../three/moonArt.js';
import { num, lat, lon } from '../utils/format.js';
import { siteName } from './dashboard.js';

/** Full-screen AI Assistant: same conversation as the floating panel, more room. */
export function render(root) {
  const { mission: m, analysis: a, comparison: c } = getState();
  const topics = ['sites', 'resources', 'illumination', 'terrain', 'planning', 'risk'];
  const rec = c?.result?.recommendedIndex;
  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    <div class="page-head"><div><p class="sub">${esc(t('aipage.subtitle'))}</p></div></div>
    <div class="ai-layout">
      <section class="glass ai-main">
        <header class="ai-head">
          <img alt="" src="${moonImage(64)}"/>
          <div><h2>${esc(t('ai.title'))}</h2><p class="tiny muted">${esc(engineLabel())}</p></div>
        </header>
        ${chatHTML('page')}
      </section>
      <aside class="ai-side">
        <div class="glass panel">
          <h3>${esc(t('aipage.context'))}</h3>
          ${a ? `<dl class="kv">
            <dt>${esc(t('planner.name'))}</dt><dd>${esc(m.name || t('dash.untitled'))}</dd>
            <dt>${esc(t('planner.site'))}</dt><dd>${esc(siteName(m))}</dd>
            <dt>${esc(t('planner.lat'))} / ${esc(t('planner.lon'))}</dt><dd>${lat(a.site.lat)}, ${lon(a.site.lon)}</dd>
            <dt>${esc(t('planner.date'))}</dt><dd>${esc(m.date)} ${esc(m.time)} UTC</dd>
            <dt>${esc(t('metric.overall'))}</dt><dd>${num(a.scores.overall, 0)} / 100</dd>
            ${rec !== null && rec !== undefined ? `<dt>${esc(t('compare.recommendedSite'))}</dt><dd>${esc(t('compare.site'))} ${esc(c.sites[rec].key)}</dd>` : ''}
          </dl>
          <div class="row mt-s">${sourceBadge(a.sources.ephemeris)} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.terrain === 'MIXED' ? 'MIXED' : a.sources.terrain)}</div>`
          : `<p class="small muted">${esc(t('ai.noAnalysis'))}</p><a class="btn sm primary" href="#planner">${esc(t('empty.action'))}</a>`}
        </div>
        <div class="glass panel">
          <h3>${esc(t('aipage.topics'))}</h3>
          <ul class="topic-list">${topics.map((k) => `<li><b>${esc(t(`aipage.topic.${k}`))}</b><span>${esc(t(`aipage.topic.${k}.d`))}</span></li>`).join('')}</ul>
        </div>
        <p class="tiny muted">${esc(t('aipage.note'))}</p>
      </aside>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  const msgs = root.querySelector('.chat-page .msgs');
  if (msgs) msgs.scrollTop = msgs.scrollHeight;
  return () => {};
}

# FILE 15/38: public/src/pages/auth.js

import { esc, icons, logoSvg } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState, setState, updateSettings } from '../state/store.js';
import * as api from '../services/api.js';
import { modal } from '../components/ui.js';
import { moonImage } from '../three/moonArt.js';
import { createMoonScene } from '../three/moonScene.js';
import { storageKind } from '../utils/storage.js';
import { refreshHistory } from '../services/missionService.js';

let tab = 'login';
let flash = null; // {type, text}
let prefillEmail = '';

const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

let disposeCurrent = null;
export function render(root) {
  const rerender = () => {
    disposeCurrent?.();
    disposeCurrent = mount(root, rerender);
  };
  rerender();
  return () => {
    disposeCurrent?.();
    disposeCurrent = null;
  };
}

function mount(root, rerender) {
  const { settings } = getState();
  root.innerHTML = `
  <main class="auth">
    <section class="auth-visual" aria-label="${esc(t('auth.visual'))}">
      <div class="moon-fallback" id="auth-moon-fallback"></div>
      <div id="auth-moon" style="position:absolute;inset:0"></div>
      <div class="caption">
        <div class="row" style="gap:12px"><svg class="crescent" viewBox="0 0 32 32" width="40" height="40" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M20.5 4.5A12 12 0 1 0 27.5 22 10 10 0 0 1 20.5 4.5z" fill="currentColor"/><path d="M24 6l.8 2 2 .8-2 .8-.8 2-.8-2-2-.8 2-.8z" fill="currentColor"/></svg><h1>Lunar AI</h1></div>
        <p>${esc(t('auth.tagline'))}</p>
      </div>
    </section>
    <section class="auth-side">
      <div class="auth-top">
        <div class="row"><span class="tiny muted upper">${esc(t('app.tagline'))}</span></div>
        <div class="row">
          <label class="sr-only" for="auth-lang">${esc(t('settings.language'))}</label>
          <select id="auth-lang" style="width:auto;min-height:36px;padding:4px 10px">
            <option value="en" ${settings.lang === 'en' ? 'selected' : ''}>English</option>
            <option value="ar" ${settings.lang === 'ar' ? 'selected' : ''}>العربية</option>
          </select>
          <button class="icon-btn" data-action="auth-theme" aria-label="${esc(t('settings.theme'))}">${settings.theme === 'dark' ? icons.sun : icons.moon}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="auth-card">
        <h2 style="margin-bottom:4px">${esc(t(tab === 'login' ? 'auth.welcome' : 'auth.create'))}</h2>
        <p class="muted small" style="margin-bottom:18px">${esc(t(tab === 'login' ? 'auth.welcomeSub' : 'auth.createSub'))}</p>
        <div class="tabs" role="tablist">
          <button role="tab" aria-selected="${tab === 'login'}" data-tab="login">${esc(t('auth.login'))}</button>
          <button role="tab" aria-selected="${tab === 'register'}" data-tab="register">${esc(t('auth.register'))}</button>
        </div>
        ${flash ? `<div class="form-msg ${flash.type}" role="alert">${esc(flash.text)}</div>` : ''}
        <form id="auth-form" novalidate class="stack">
          <label class="field">${esc(t('auth.email'))}
            <input type="email" name="email" autocomplete="email" required value="${esc(prefillEmail)}" />
            <span class="err" data-err="email"></span></label>
          <label class="field">${esc(t('auth.password'))}
            <input type="password" name="password" autocomplete="${tab === 'login' ? 'current-password' : 'new-password'}" required minlength="8" />
            <span class="err" data-err="password"></span>
            ${tab === 'register' ? `<span class="hint">${esc(t('auth.passwordHint'))}</span>` : ''}</label>
          ${tab === 'register' ? `<label class="field">${esc(t('auth.confirm'))}
            <input type="password" name="confirm" autocomplete="new-password" required />
            <span class="err" data-err="confirm"></span></label>` : ''}
          ${tab === 'login' ? `<div class="row between">
            <label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="remember" ${getState().remember !== false ? 'checked' : ''}/> ${esc(t('auth.remember'))}</label>
            <a href="#" data-action="forgot" class="small">${esc(t('auth.forgot'))}</a></div>` : ''}
          <button class="btn primary" type="submit" style="width:100%">${esc(t(tab === 'login' ? 'auth.loginBtn' : 'auth.registerBtn'))}</button>
        </form>
        <p class="tiny muted mt">${esc(t(getState().backend === 'remote' ? 'auth.storeRemote' : storageKind === 'memory' ? 'auth.storeMemory' : 'auth.storeLocal'))}</p>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>`;

  const cleanup = [];
  // Hero Moon: same Moon artwork as the rest of the app (no WebGL needed)
  const host = root.querySelector('#auth-moon');
  root.querySelector('#auth-moon-fallback')?.remove();
  host.innerHTML = `<div class="auth-globe"><img alt="" src="${moonImage(560)}"/></div>`;
  const globe = host.querySelector('.auth-globe');
  let heroScene = null;
  let alive = true;
  createMoonScene(globe, { mode: 'hero' }).then((sc) => {
    if (!sc) return;
    if (!alive || !globe.isConnected) return sc.dispose();
    heroScene = sc;
    globe.querySelector('img')?.remove();
  });
  cleanup.push(() => {
    alive = false;
    heroScene?.dispose();
  });
  const onClick = async (e) => {
    const tb = e.target.closest('[data-tab]');
    if (tb) {
      tab = tb.dataset.tab;
      flash = null;
      rerender();
      return;
    }
    const a = e.target.closest('[data-action]');
    if (!a) return;
    if (a.dataset.action === 'auth-theme') updateSettings({ theme: getState().settings.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark' });
    if (a.dataset.action === 'forgot') {
      e.preventDefault();
      const email = root.querySelector('input[name=email]').value;
      const r = await api.forgotPassword(email);
      await modal({
        title: t('auth.forgotTitle'),
        body: esc(r.ok ? t('auth.forgotSent') : t('auth.forgotUnavailable')),
        info: true,
      });
    }
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', onClick);
  cleanup.push(() => root.removeEventListener('click', onClick));

  const langSel = root.querySelector('#auth-lang');
  langSel.addEventListener('change', () => updateSettings({ lang: langSel.value }));

  root.querySelector('#auth-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const f = new FormData(e.target);
    const email = String(f.get('email') || '').trim();
    const password = String(f.get('password') || '');
    const errs = {};
    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) errs.email = t('auth.err.email');
    if (password.length < 8) errs.password = t('auth.err.password');
    if (tab === 'register' && password !== f.get('confirm')) errs.confirm = t('auth.err.confirm');
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-err]').forEach((el) => {
      el.textContent = errs[el.dataset.err] || '';
      const input = root.querySelector(`[name=${el.dataset.err}]`);
      input?.setAttribute('aria-invalid', errs[el.dataset.err] ? 'true' : 'false');
    });
    if (Object.keys(errs).length) return;

    const btn = e.target.querySelector('button[type=submit]');
    btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      if (tab === 'register') {
        const r = await api.register(email, password);
        if (!r.ok) {
          flash = { type: 'error', text: t(`auth.err.${r.error}`) };
        } else {
          flash = { type: 'success', text: t('auth.registered') };
          tab = 'login';
          prefillEmail = email;
        }
        rerender();
      } else {
        const r = await api.login(email, password);
        if (!r.ok) {
          flash = { type: 'error', text: t(`auth.err.${r.error}`) };
          prefillEmail = email;
          rerender();
          return;
        }
        flash = null;
        prefillEmail = '';
        setState({ user: r.user, token: r.token, remember: f.get('remember') === 'on' });
        await refreshHistory();
        location.hash = '#planner';
      }
    } finally {
      btn.disabled = false;
    }
  });

  return () => cleanup.forEach((fn) => fn());
}


######################################################################
# FILE 16/38: public/src/pages/compare.js
######################################################################

import { esc, cssVar } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { emptyState, sourceBadge, levelChip } from '../components/ui.js';
import { dataBanner } from '../components/sourcesPanel.js';
import { PRESET_SITES, presetById } from '../data/sites.js';
import { COMPARE_METRICS, RISK_KEYS, riskLevel } from '../services/analysisEngine.js';
import { runComparison, defaultComparisonSites, adoptRecommendedSite, siteLabel } from '../services/missionService.js';
import { barChart, radarChart, destroyCharts } from '../charts/charts.js';
import { num, lat, lon } from '../utils/format.js';

export function render(root) {
  const { analysis: a, comparison: c, mission } = getState();
  if (!a) {
    root.innerHTML = `<div class="page">${emptyState('empty.title', 'empty.text', '#planner', 'empty.action')}</div>`;
    return () => {};
  }
  const sites = (c?.sites?.length ? c.sites : defaultComparisonSites(mission)).map((s) => ({ ...s }));
  const computed = Boolean(c?.result && c.analyses?.length === sites.length);
  const res = computed ? c.result : null;
  const A = computed ? c.analyses : [];
  const recI = res?.recommendedIndex ?? null;

  const siteEditor = (s, i) => `
    <div class="card site-card ${recI === i ? 'rec-box' : ''}">
      <div class="card-head"><span class="site-letter">${esc(t('compare.site'))} ${esc(s.key)}${recI === i ? ' ★' : ''}</span>
        ${s.fromMission ? `<span class="badge EXTERNAL">${esc(t('compare.missionSite'))}</span>` : ''}</div>
      <div class="stack" data-site="${i}">
        <label class="field">${esc(t('compare.location'))}
          <select data-k="siteId">
            ${PRESET_SITES.map((p) => `<option value="${p.id}" ${s.siteId === p.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(t(p.nameKey))}</option>`).join('')}
            <option value="custom" ${!presetById(s.siteId) ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(t('site.custom'))}</option>
          </select></label>
        <div class="form-grid">
          <label class="field">${esc(t('planner.lat'))}<input type="number" step="0.001" min="-90" max="90" data-k="lat" value="${esc(s.lat)}"/></label>
          <label class="field">${esc(t('planner.lon'))}<input type="number" step="0.001" min="-180" max="360" data-k="lon" value="${esc(s.lon)}"/></label>
          <label class="field">${esc(t('planner.date'))}<input type="date" data-k="date" value="${esc(s.date)}"/></label>
          <label class="field">${esc(t('planner.time'))} (UTC)<input type="time" data-k="time" value="${esc(s.time)}"/></label>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  const metricLabel = (k) => t(`cmp.${k}`);
  const cell = (m, i) => {
    const v = m.path(A[i]);
    const best = res.best[m.key]?.includes(i);
    let txt;
    if (m.key === 'elevationM') txt = num(v, 0);
    else if (m.key === 'terrainRisk') txt = `${levelChip(riskLevel(A[i].scores.terrain))} <span class="mono">${num(v, 0)}</span>`;
    else txt = num(v, m.unit === '°' ? 2 : 0);
    return `<td class="num ${best ? 'best' : ''} ${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}">${txt}${m.key !== 'terrainRisk' && m.unit ? ` <span class="muted tiny">${esc(m.unit === 'h' ? t('unit.h') : m.unit)}</span>` : ''}</td>`;
  };

  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    ${dataBanner(a)}
    <div class="page-head">
      <div><p class="sub">${esc(t('compare.subtitle'))}</p></div>
      <div class="row"><button class="btn primary" id="run-compare">${esc(t('compare.run'))}</button></div>
    </div>
    <div class="grid g3">${sites.map(siteEditor).join('')}</div>

    ${computed ? `
    <div class="card mt">
      <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('compare.table'))}</span>
        <span class="row tiny muted">${esc(t('compare.bestNote'))}</span></div>
      <div class="table-wrap"><table>
        <thead><tr><th>${esc(t('compare.metric'))}</th>${sites.map((s, i) => `<th class="${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}">${esc(t('compare.site'))} ${esc(s.key)}${recI === i ? ' ★' : ''}<div class="tiny muted" style="text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0">${esc(siteLabel(s))}</div></th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr><td>${esc(t('compare.location'))}</td>${sites.map((s, i) => `<td class="num ${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}">${lat(s.lat)}<br>${lon(s.lon)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
          <tr><td>${esc(t('compare.datetime'))}</td>${sites.map((s, i) => `<td class="num ${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}">${esc(s.date)} ${esc(s.time)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
          ${COMPARE_METRICS.map((m) => `<tr><td>${esc(metricLabel(m.key))}</td>${sites.map((_, i) => cell(m, i)).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
          <tr><td>${esc(t('compare.dataSource'))}</td>${sites.map((_, i) => `<td class="${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}">${sourceBadge(A[i].sources.ephemeris)} ${sourceBadge(A[i].sources.terrain === 'MIXED' ? 'MIXED' : A[i].sources.terrain)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
        </tbody>
      </table></div>
    </div>

    <div class="grid g2 mt">
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.suitability'))}</div><div class="chart-box tall"><canvas id="c-cmp-bar"></canvas></div></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-title">${esc(t('chart.profile'))}</div><div class="chart-box tall"><canvas id="c-cmp-radar"></canvas></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card rec-box mt">
      <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('compare.aiRec'))}</span>
        <span class="row">${sourceBadge(A[recI].sources.ephemeris)}</span></div>
      <div class="grid g2">
        <div>
          <div class="tiny muted upper">${esc(t('compare.recommendedSite'))}</div>
          <h2 style="font-size:1.6rem;margin:4px 0">${esc(t('compare.site'))} ${esc(sites[recI].key)} — ${esc(siteLabel(sites[recI]))}</h2>
          <div class="mono">${lat(sites[recI].lat)}, ${lon(sites[recI].lon)} · ${esc(sites[recI].date)} ${esc(sites[recI].time)} UTC</div>
          <div class="mono mt-s">${esc(t('metric.overall'))}: <b>${num(A[recI].scores.overall, 0)} / 100</b></div>
          <div class="row mt">
            ${recI !== 0 ? `<button class="btn primary" id="adopt">${esc(t('compare.adopt'))}</button>` : `<span class="badge LIVE">${esc(t('compare.alreadyMission'))}</span>`}
            <a class="btn" href="#explorer">${esc(t('compare.view3d'))}</a>
            <a class="btn" href="#risk">${esc(t('compare.viewRisk'))}</a>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <div class="tiny muted upper">${esc(t('compare.why'))}</div>
          <ul class="list mt-s">${res.reasons.length ? res.reasons.map((r) => `<li>${esc(t(r.key, { v: num(r.v, 1) }))}</li>`).join('') : `<li>${esc(t('compare.noReasons'))}</li>`}</ul>
          <p class="tiny muted mt">${esc(t('compare.recNote'))}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>` : `<div class="card mt empty"><p>${esc(t('compare.notRun'))}</p></div>`}
  </div>`;

  if (computed) {
    const colors = [cssVar('--c1'), cssVar('--c2'), cssVar('--c3')];
    barChart(root.querySelector('#c-cmp-bar'), {
      labels: [t('metric.overall'), ...RISK_KEYS.map((k) => t(`cat.${k}`))],
      yMax: 100,
      datasets: sites.map((s, i) => ({ label: `${t('compare.site')} ${s.key}`, data: [A[i].scores.overall, ...RISK_KEYS.map((k) => A[i].scores[k])], color: colors[i] })),
    });
    radarChart(root.querySelector('#c-cmp-radar'), {
      labels: RISK_KEYS.map((k) => t(`cat.${k}`)),
      datasets: sites.map((s, i) => ({ label: `${t('compare.site')} ${s.key}`, data: RISK_KEYS.map((k) => A[i].scores[k]), color: colors[i] })),
    });
  }

  // editing
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-site]').forEach((box) => {
    const i = Number(box.dataset.site);
    box.addEventListener('change', (ev) => {
      const k = ev.target.dataset.k;
      if (!k) return;
      const s = sites[i];
      if (k === 'siteId') {
        const p = presetById(ev.target.value);
        s.siteId = ev.target.value;
        if (p) {
          s.lat = p.lat;
          s.lon = p.lon;
          box.querySelector('[data-k=lat]').value = p.lat;
          box.querySelector('[data-k=lon]').value = p.lon;
        }
      } else if (k === 'lat' || k === 'lon') {
        s[k] = Number(ev.target.value);
        const p = presetById(s.siteId);
        if (p && (p.lat !== s.lat || p.lon !== s.lon)) {
          s.siteId = 'custom';
          box.querySelector('[data-k=siteId]').value = 'custom';
        }
      } else s[k] = ev.target.value;
      if (s.fromMission && (s.lat !== Number(mission.latitude) || s.lon !== Number(mission.longitude) || s.date !== mission.date || s.time !== mission.time)) s.fromMission = false;
    });
  });

  root.querySelector('#run-compare').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const bad = sites.find((s) => !Number.isFinite(Number(s.lat)) || Math.abs(s.lat) > 90 || !Number.isFinite(Number(s.lon)) || !s.date || !s.time);
    if (bad) return;
    await runComparison(sites.map((s) => ({ ...s, lat: Number(s.lat), lon: Number(s.lon) })));
  });
  root.querySelector('#adopt')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const r = await adoptRecommendedSite();
    if (r?.ok) location.hash = '#dashboard';
  });

  return () => destroyCharts();
}

import { esc, cssVar } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';

import { sourcesPanel, dataBanner } from '../components/sourcesPanel.js';
import { generateMissionSummary } from '../services/insights.js';
import { riskLevel } from '../services/analysisEngine.js';
import { num, deg, pct, lat, lon } from '../utils/format.js';
import { presetById } from '../data/sites.js';
import { skyPanorama } from './analysis.js';
import { moonImage, landscapeImage, surfaceTile } from '../three/moonArt.js';
import { lineChart, destroyCharts, hourLabels } from '../charts/charts.js';
import { setState } from '../state/store.js';
import { PRESET_SITES } from '../data/sites.js';
import { loadMissionRecord } from '../services/missionService.js';
import { openAssistantWith } from '../components/assistantPanel.js';

const KIND = { sunEl: 'sun', sunAz: 'sun', sunlight: 'sun', power: 'sun', earthEl: 'earth', earthVis: 'earth', comm: 'earth', terrainRisk: 'terrain', overall: 'score' };

export function siteName(m) {
  const p = presetById(m.siteId);
  return p ? t(p.nameKey) : m.siteName || t('site.custom');
}

function metricCard({ key, label, value, unit = '', foot = '', bar = null, src }) {
  return `<div class="card metric-card" data-metric="${key}" data-kind="${KIND[key] || 'score'}">
    <div class="metric">
      <div class="label"><span>${esc(label)}</span>${src ? sourceBadge(src) : ''}</div>
      <div class="value">${value}${unit ? `<small>${esc(unit)}</small>` : ''}</div>
      ${foot ? `<div class="foot">${foot}</div>` : ''}
      ${bar !== null ? `<div class="bar"><i style="width:${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, bar))}%"></i></div>` : ''}
    </div></div>`;
}


const ic = {
  spark: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3l1.8 5.2L19 10l-5.2 1.8L12 17l-1.8-5.2L5 10l5.2-1.8z"/></svg>',
  cube: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3l8 4.5v9L12 21l-8-4.5v-9z"/><path d="M4 7.5l8 4.5 8-4.5M12 12v9"/></svg>',
  map: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 21s-7-6.2-7-12a7 7 0 0 1 14 0c0 5.8-7 12-7 12z"/><circle cx="12" cy="9" r="2.5"/></svg>',
  doc: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M6 3h9l4 4v14H6z"/><path d="M15 3v4h4M9 12h7M9 16h7"/></svg>',
  bars: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><rect x="4" y="13" width="3" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="10.5" y="9" width="3" height="11" rx="1"/><rect x="17" y="5" width="3" height="15" rx="1"/></svg>',
  target: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3" fill="currentColor"/></svg>',
  search: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"/><path d="M20 20l-4-4"/></svg>',
  chev: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M9 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg>',
  sun: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/><path d="M12 2v2M12 20v2M2 12h2M20 12h2M4.9 4.9l1.4 1.4M17.7 17.7l1.4 1.4M4.9 19.1l1.4-1.4M17.7 6.3l1.4-1.4"/></svg>',
  dish: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M5 19a9 9 0 0 1 0-12.7l12.7 12.7A9 9 0 0 1 5 19z"/><path d="M11.3 12.7L16 8M16 8a2 2 0 1 0 0 .01"/></svg>',
  slope: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M3 19h18L14 7z"/></svg>',
};

function sparkline(values, w = 120, h = 40) {
  if (!values.length) return '';
  const min = Math.min(...values);
  const max = Math.max(...values);
  const span = max - min || 1;
  const pts = values.map((v, i) => `${((i / (values.length - 1 || 1)) * w).toFixed(1)},${(h - 4 - ((v - min) / span) * (h - 8)).toFixed(1)}`);
  return `<svg class="spark" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true">
    <path d="M0,${h} L${pts.join(' L')} L${w},${h} Z" fill="currentColor" opacity=".14"/>
    <path d="M${pts.join(' L')}" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"/></svg>`;
}

  const opts = [...PRESET_SITES.map((p) => t(p.nameKey)), ...history.map((h) => h.name)];

  <section class="hero2">
    <div class="hero2-copy">
      <span class="status-pill ${online ? 'on' : 'local'}"><i></i>${esc(t(online ? 'home.statusOnline' : 'home.statusLocal'))}</span>
      <h1 class="hero2-title">${t('home.heroTitle')}</h1>
      <p class="hero2-sub">${esc(a ? t('home.subActive', { name: m.name || t('dash.untitled') }) : t('home.heroSub'))}</p>
      <div class="hero2-stats">
        <a href="#planner"><b class="c-blue">01</b><span>${esc(t('nav.planner'))}</span></a>
        <a href="#explorer"><b class="c-cyan">3D</b><span>${esc(t('home.lunarExplorer'))}</span></a>
        <a href="#assistant"><b class="c-green">AI</b><span>${esc(t('home.smartAnalysis'))}</span></a>
      </div>
      <form class="hero-search" id="hero-search" role="search">
        ${ic.search}
        <label class="sr-only" for="hero-q">${esc(t('home.searchPh'))}</label>
        <input id="hero-q" list="hero-list" placeholder="${esc(t('home.searchPh'))}" autocomplete="off"/>
        <datalist id="hero-list">${opts.map((o) => `<option value="${esc(o)}">`).join('')}</datalist>
      </form>
    </div>
    <div class="hero2-moon">
      <div class="hero2-globe" id="hero-globe"><img alt="" src="${moonImage(520)}"/></div>
      <span class="moon-cap">${esc(t('home.moonCaption'))}</span>
    </div>
  </section>`;
}

function stats(a, c, history) {
  const sun = a ? a.points.slice(a.grid.startIdx, a.grid.endIdx + 1).map((p) => p.sunEl) : [];
  return `
  <div class="home-stats">
    <a class="glass stat" href="#reports"><div><span>${esc(t('home.savedMissions'))}</span><b>${history.length}</b></div><span class="stat-art blue">${sparkline(history.length ? history.slice().reverse().map((h, i) => (h.score ?? 0) + i) : [0, 0])}</span></a>
    <a class="glass stat" href="#analysis"><div><span>${esc(t('dash.score'))}</span><b>${a ? num(a.scores.overall, 0) : '—'}<small>${a ? ' / 100' : ''}</small></b></div><span class="stat-art blue">${a ? sparkline(sun) : ic.bars}</span></a>
    <a class="glass stat" href="#compare"><div><span>${esc(t('home.sitesCompared'))}</span><b>${c?.result ? c.sites.length : 0}</b></div><span class="stat-art round">${ic.target}</span></a>
  </div>`;
}

function recent(history) {
  const items = history.slice(0, 3);
  return `<div class="glass panel">
    <h3>${esc(t('home.recent'))}</h3>
    ${items.length ? `<ul class="recent">${items.map((h) => `
      <li><button data-open="${esc(h.id)}"><img alt="" src="${moonImage(64)}"/><span><b>${esc(h.name)}</b><small>${esc(h.date)} · <em class="${h.status === 'analyzed' ? 'ok' : ''}">${esc(t(`status.${h.status === 'analyzed' ? 'analyzed' : 'draft'}`))}</em></small></span>${ic.chev}</button></li>`).join('')}</ul>`
      : `<p class="muted small">${esc(t('history.empty'))}</p><a class="btn sm" href="#planner">${esc(t('empty.action'))}</a>`}
  </div>`;
}

    </a>
    <p class="tiny muted">${esc(t('home.mapNote'))}</p>
  </div>`;
}
