  return `<div class="glass panel">
    <h3>${esc(t('home.data'))}</h3>
    ${a ? `<div class="tabs-pill" role="tablist">
      <button role="tab" data-series="sun" aria-selected="true">${esc(t('metric.sunEl'))}</button>
      <button role="tab" data-series="earth" aria-selected="false">${esc(t('metric.earthEl'))}</button>
      <button role="tab" data-series="power" aria-selected="false">${esc(t('metric.power'))}</button>
    </div>
    <div class="chart-box mini"><canvas id="home-chart"></canvas></div>` : `<p class="muted small">${esc(t('empty.text'))}</p>`}
  </div>`;
}

function quick(a) {
  const btn = (act, icon, key, href) => href ? `<a class="qa" href="${href}"><span>${icon}</span>${esc(t(key))}</a>` : `<button class="qa" data-qa="${act}"><span>${icon}</span>${esc(t(key))}</button>`;
  return `<div class="glass panel">
    <h3>${esc(t('home.quick'))}</h3>
    <div class="qa-list">
      ${btn('ask', ic.spark, 'home.askAi')}
      ${btn('', ic.cube, 'home.view3d', '#explorer')}
      ${btn('', ic.map, 'nav.compare', '#compare')}
      ${btn('', ic.doc, 'home.report', a ? '#reports' : '#planner')}
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

function progress(a) {
  return `<div class="glass panel">
    <h3>${esc(t('home.progress'))}</h3>
    <div class="prog">
      ${scoreRing(a.scores.overall, { size: 96, label: t('dash.score') })}
      <ul class="legend-list">${['power', 'communication', 'terrain'].map((k) => `<li><i class="${a.scores[k] >= 70 ? 'ok' : a.scores[k] >= 40 ? 'warn' : 'bad'}"></i><span>${esc(t(`cat.${k}`))}</span><b>${num(a.scores[k], 0)}</b></li>`).join('')}</ul>
    </div>
  </div>`;
}

function latest(a) {
  const mm = a.metrics;
  return `<div class="glass panel">
    <h3>${esc(t('home.latest'))}</h3>
    <div class="latest"><img alt="" src="${surfaceTile(120, a.site.lon + 40)}"/>
      <dl>
        <dt>${esc(t('metric.sunlight'))}</dt><dd>${num(mm.sunlightHours, 0)} ${esc(t('unit.h'))} · ${pct(mm.sunlightFraction)}</dd>
        <dt>${esc(t('metric.power'))}</dt><dd>${num(mm.avgPowerW, 0)} W</dd>
        <dt>${esc(t('metric.comm'))}</dt><dd>${num(mm.commLongestH, 0)} ${esc(t('unit.h'))}</dd>
      </dl></div>
    <p class="tiny muted">${sourceBadge(a.sources.ephemeris)} ${esc(t('home.computed'))} ${esc(a.computedAt.slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' '))} UTC</p>
  </div>`;
}

function conditions(a) {
  const p0 = a.points[a.grid.startIdx];
  return `<div class="glass panel">
    <h3>${esc(t('home.conditions'))}</h3>
    <div class="cond"><img alt="" src="${moonImage(96)}"/>
      <ul>
        <li class="sun">${ic.sun}<span>${esc(t('metric.sunEl'))} ${deg(p0.sunEl)} · ${esc(a.metrics.sunVisible ? t('home.lit') : t('home.dark'))}</span></li>
        <li class="earth">${ic.dish}<span>${esc(a.metrics.dteNow ? t('home.dteOn') : t('home.dteOff'))} · ${deg(p0.earthEl)}</span></li>
        <li>${ic.slope}<span>${esc(t('metric.slope'))} ${deg(a.terrain.slopeDeg, 1)} · ${esc(t(`src.${a.sources.terrainFields.slope}`))}</span></li>
      </ul></div>
  </div>`;
}

function riskBars(a) {
  return `<div class="glass panel">
    <h3>${esc(t('home.riskOverview'))}</h3>
    <div class="riskbars">${RISK_KEYS.map((k) => `<div class="rb" title="${esc(t(`cat.${k}`))}: ${num(a.scores[k], 0)}"><span style="height:${Math.max(6, a.scores[k])}%" class="${a.scores[k] >= 70 ? 'ok' : a.scores[k] >= 40 ? 'warn' : 'bad'}"></span><small>${esc(t(`cat.${k}`)).slice(0, 4)}</small></div>`).join('')}</div>
    <a class="tiny" href="#risk">${esc(t('nav.risk'))}</a>
  </div>`;
}

export function render(root) {
  const { mission: m, analysis: a, comparison: c, settings, history } = getState();
  const hist = history || [];

  let rest = '';
  if (a) {
    const mm = a.metrics;
    const eph = a.sources.ephemeris;
    const terr = a.sources.terrain === 'MIXED' ? 'MIXED' : a.sources.terrain;
    const sum = generateMissionSummary(a, c);
    const cards = settings.dashboardCards;
    const stale = a.site.lat !== Number(m.latitude) || a.site.lon !== Number(m.longitude) || !a.grid.start.startsWith(m.date);
    const list = [
      cards.sunEl && metricCard({ key: 'sunEl', label: t('metric.sunEl'), value: num(mm.sunEl), unit: '°', foot: `${esc(t('metric.range'))}: ${deg(mm.minSunEl)} … ${deg(mm.maxSunEl)}`, src: eph }),
      cards.sunAz && metricCard({ key: 'sunAz', label: t('metric.sunAz'), value: num(mm.sunAz, 1), unit: '°', foot: esc(t('metric.azNote')), src: eph }),
      cards.sunlight && metricCard({ key: 'sunlight', label: t('metric.sunlight'), value: num(mm.sunlightHours, 0), unit: t('unit.h'), foot: `${pct(mm.sunlightFraction)} ${esc(t('metric.ofWindow', { h: num(mm.windowHours, 0) }))}`, bar: mm.sunlightFraction * 100, src: eph }),
      cards.earthEl && metricCard({ key: 'earthEl', label: t('metric.earthEl'), value: num(mm.earthEl), unit: '°', foot: `${esc(t('metric.max'))}: ${deg(mm.maxEarthEl)}`, src: eph }),
      cards.earthVis && metricCard({ key: 'earthVis', label: t('metric.earthVis'), value: num(mm.earthVisibleFraction * 100, 0), unit: '%', foot: esc(mm.earthVisible ? t('metric.visibleNow') : t('metric.notVisibleNow')), bar: mm.earthVisibleFraction * 100, src: eph }),
      cards.comm && metricCard({ key: 'comm', label: t('metric.comm'), value: num(mm.commLongestH, 0), unit: t('unit.h'), foot: `${esc(t('metric.dte'))}: ${pct(mm.dteFraction)}`, bar: mm.dteFraction * 100, src: eph }),
      cards.power && metricCard({ key: 'power', label: t('metric.power'), value: num(mm.avgPowerW, 0), unit: 'W', foot: `${esc(t('metric.peak'))} ${num(mm.peakPowerW, 0)} W · ${num(mm.energyKWh, 1)} kWh`, bar: a.scores.power, src: 'ESTIMATED' }),
      cards.terrainRisk && metricCard({ key: 'terrainRisk', label: t('metric.terrainRisk'), value: levelChip(riskLevel(a.scores.terrain)), foot: `${esc(t('metric.slope'))} ${deg(a.terrain.slopeDeg, 1)}`, src: terr }),
      cards.overall && metricCard({ key: 'overall', label: t('metric.overall'), value: num(a.scores.overall, 0), unit: '/100', foot: levelChip(riskLevel(a.scores.overall)), bar: a.scores.overall }),
    ].filter(Boolean);

    rest = `
    <div class="home-row">${progress(a)}${latest(a)}${conditions(a)}${riskBars(a)}</div>

    <section class="home-split">
      <div class="glass panel">
        <h3>${esc(t('dash.horizonTitle'))}</h3>
        <p class="small muted">${esc(t('dash.horizonSub'))}</p>
        <div class="sky">${skyPanorama(a, a.grid.startIdx)}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="glass panel brief-panel">
        <h3>${esc(t('dash.aiSummary'))}</h3>
        ${stale ? `<p class="small" style="color:var(--warn)">${esc(t('dash.stale'))} <a href="#planner">${esc(t('dash.reanalyze'))}</a></p>` : ''}
        <div class="brief-item good"><b>${esc(t('sum.h.strength'))}</b><p>${esc(sum.strength)}</p></div>
        <div class="brief-item bad"><b>${esc(t('sum.h.risk'))}</b><p>${esc(sum.risk)}</p></div>
        <div class="brief-item"><b>${esc(t('sum.h.comm'))}</b><p>${esc(sum.comm)}</p></div>
        <div class="brief-item"><b>${esc(t('sum.h.power'))}</b><p>${esc(sum.power)}</p></div>
        <div class="brief-item"><b>${esc(t('sum.h.terrain'))}</b><p>${esc(sum.terrain)}</p></div>
        <div class="brief-item rec"><b>${esc(t('sum.h.recommendation'))}</b><p>${esc(sum.recommendation)}</p></div>
        ${sum.caveat ? `<p class="tiny muted">${esc(sum.caveat)}</p>` : ''}
      </div>
    </section>

    <h3 class="block-title">${esc(t('dash.metrics'))}</h3>
    <div class="metrics-grid">${list.join('')}</div>
    <div class="mt">${sourcesPanel(a)}</div>`;
  }

  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page home">
    ${a ? dataBanner(a) : ''}
    ${hero(a, m, hist)}
    ${stats(a, c, hist)}
    <div class="home-row">${recent(hist)}${mapCard(a, m)}${dataOverview(a)}${quick(a)}</div>
    ${rest}
  </div>`;

  // interactive 3D Moon in the hero (keeps the image if WebGL / CDN is unavailable)
  let heroScene = null;
  let alive = true;
  const globe = root.querySelector('#hero-globe');
  createMoonScene(globe, { mode: 'hero' }).then((sc) => {
    if (!sc) return;
    if (!alive || !globe.isConnected) return sc.dispose();
    heroScene = sc;
    globe.querySelector('img')?.remove();
  });

  // data overview chart with tabs
  let chart = null;
  const drawChart = (series) => {
    if (!a) return;
    destroyCharts();
    const P = a.points.slice(a.grid.startIdx, a.grid.endIdx + 1);
    const labels = hourLabels(a.points, a.grid.startIdx, a.grid.endIdx + 1);
    const cfg = {
      sun: { label: t('metric.sunEl'), data: P.map((p) => p.sunEl), color: cssVar('--sun'), y: '°' },
      earth: { label: t('metric.earthEl'), data: P.map((p) => p.earthEl), color: cssVar('--earth'), y: '°' },
      power: { label: t('metric.power'), data: P.map((p) => p.powerW), color: cssVar('--accent'), y: 'W' },
    }[series];
    chart = lineChart(root.querySelector('#home-chart'), { labels, yTitle: cfg.y, datasets: [{ label: cfg.label, data: cfg.data, color: cfg.color, fill: true }] });
  };
  drawChart('sun');
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-series]').forEach((b) =>
    b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      root.querySelectorAll('[data-series]').forEach((x) => x.setAttribute('aria-selected', x === b));
      if (!root.querySelector('#home-chart')) {
        root.querySelector('.chart-box.mini').innerHTML = '<canvas id="home-chart"></canvas>';
      }
      drawChart(b.dataset.series);
    }),
  );

  // hero search: presets → planner, saved missions → open
  root.querySelector('#hero-search').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const q = root.querySelector('#hero-q').value.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return;
    const rec = hist.find((h) => h.name.toLowerCase() === q) || hist.find((h) => h.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if (rec) {
      loadMissionRecord(rec);
      location.hash = rec.analysis ? '#dashboard' : '#planner';
      return;
    }
    const site = PRESET_SITES.find((p) => t(p.nameKey).toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.id.includes(q));
    if (site) {
      setState({ mission: { ...getState().mission, siteId: site.id, region: site.region, latitude: site.lat, longitude: site.lon, siteName: '' } });
      location.hash = '#planner';
      return;
    }
    toast(t('home.noMatch'), 'error', { force: true });
  });

  root.querySelectorAll('[data-open]').forEach((b) =>
    b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const rec = hist.find((h) => h.id === b.dataset.open);
      if (!rec) return;
      loadMissionRecord(rec);
      location.hash = rec.analysis ? '#dashboard' : '#planner';
    }),
  );
  root.querySelector('[data-qa="ask"]')?.addEventListener('click', () => openAssistantWith(t('ai.q.best')));

  return () => {
    alive = false;
    heroScene?.dispose();
    destroyCharts();
  };
}


######################################################################
# FILE 18/38: public/src/pages/explorer.js
######################################################################

import { esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState, setState } from '../state/store.js';
import { emptyState, sourceBadge } from '../components/ui.js';
import { dataBanner } from '../components/sourcesPanel.js';
import { createMoonScene } from '../three/moonScene.js';
import { siteLabel } from '../services/missionService.js';
import { deg, lat, lon, elev, utc, num } from '../utils/format.js';

const layerState = { illumination: true, terrain: true, site: true, trajectory: true, sun: true, earth: true, base: 'color' };

export function sceneSites() {
  const { analysis: a, comparison: c } = getState();
  if (c?.sites?.length) return { sites: c.sites.map((s) => ({ key: s.key, lat: s.lat, lon: s.lon })), rec: c.result?.recommendedIndex ?? null };
  return { sites: [{ key: 'A', lat: a.site.lat, lon: a.site.lon }], rec: null };
}

export function render(root) {
  const { analysis: a, comparison: c } = getState();
  if (!a) {
    root.innerHTML = `<div class="page">${emptyState('empty.title', 'empty.text', '#planner', 'empty.action')}</div>`;
    return () => {};
  }
  const { sites, rec } = sceneSites();
  let idx = a.grid.startIdx;
  const maxIdx = a.grid.endIdx;
  const e = elev(a.terrain.elevationM);
  const recSite = rec !== null ? c.sites[rec] : null;

  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    ${dataBanner(a)}
    <div class="page-head"><div><p class="sub">${esc(t('explorer.subtitle'))}</p></div>
      ${recSite ? `<div class="banner live" style="margin:0">★ ${esc(t('explorer.recommended'))}: ${esc(t('compare.site'))} ${esc(recSite.key)} — ${esc(siteLabel(recSite))} (${lat(recSite.lat)}, ${lon(recSite.lon)})</div>` : ''}
    </div>
    <div class="explorer">
      <div class="viewport" id="viewport">
        <div class="loading" id="vp-loading"><div class="stack" style="align-items:center"><div class="spinner"></div>${esc(t('explorer.loading'))}</div></div>
        <div class="overlay" id="vp-overlay"></div>
        <div class="controls no-print">
          <button class="btn sm" data-cmd="reset">${esc(t('explorer.reset'))}</button>
          <button class="btn sm" data-cmd="focus">${esc(t('explorer.focus'))}</button>
          <button class="btn sm" data-cmd="terrain" aria-pressed="${layerState.terrain}">${esc(t('explorer.toggleTerrain'))}</button>
          <button class="btn sm" data-cmd="sun" aria-pressed="${layerState.sun}">${esc(t('explorer.toggleSun'))}</button>
          <button class="btn sm" data-cmd="earth" aria-pressed="${layerState.earth}">${esc(t('explorer.toggleEarth'))}</button>
        </div>
        <div class="legend">
          <div><i style="background:#6ea2ff"></i>${esc(t('explorer.legend.mission'))}</div>
          <div><i style="background:#f2c14e"></i>${esc(t('explorer.legend.recommended'))}</div>
          <div><i style="background:#e7edf6"></i>${esc(t('explorer.legend.alt'))}</div>
          <div><i style="background:#f2c14e;border-radius:0;height:2px"></i>${esc(t('explorer.legend.sun'))} · <i style="background:#6ea2ff;border-radius:0;height:2px"></i>${esc(t('explorer.legend.earth'))}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <aside class="stack">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('explorer.panel'))}</span></div>
          <dl class="kv" id="site-panel"></dl>
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('explorer.time'))}</span><span class="mono tiny" id="ex-time"></span></div>
          <label class="sr-only" for="ex-range">${esc(t('explorer.time'))}</label>
          <input type="range" id="ex-range" min="0" max="${maxIdx}" value="${idx}" style="width:100%" />
          <p class="tiny muted">${esc(t('explorer.timeHint'))}</p>
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('explorer.layers'))}</span></div>
          <div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="${esc(t('explorer.basemap'))}" id="basemap">
            ${['color', 'elevation', 'slope'].map((k) => `<button type="button" data-base="${k}" aria-pressed="${layerState.base === k}">${esc(t(`explorer.base.${k}`))}</button>`).join('')}
          </div>
          <div class="layer-list mt-s">
            ${['illumination', 'terrain', 'site', 'trajectory', 'sun', 'earth'].map((k) => `<label><span>${esc(t(`explorer.layer.${k}`))}</span><input type="checkbox" data-layer="${k}" ${layerState[k] ? 'checked' : ''}/></label>`).join('')}
          </div>
          <p class="tiny muted mt-s" id="layer-note"></p>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
    <p class="tiny muted mt">${esc(t('explorer.scaleNote'))}</p>
  </div>`;

  const panel = () => {
    const p = a.points[idx];
    root.querySelector('#ex-time').textContent = utc(p.t);
    root.querySelector('#site-panel').innerHTML = `
      <dt>${esc(t('planner.lat'))}</dt><dd>${lat(a.site.lat)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('planner.lon'))}</dt><dd>${lon(a.site.lon)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.elevation'))}</dt><dd>${e.v} ${e.u} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.terrainFields.elevation)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.slope'))}</dt><dd>${deg(a.terrain.slopeDeg, 1)} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.terrainFields.slope)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.sunEl'))}</dt><dd>${deg(p.sunEl)} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.ephemeris)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.earthEl'))}</dt><dd>${deg(p.earthEl)} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.ephemeris)}</dd>
      <dt>${esc(t('metric.power'))}</dt><dd>${num(p.powerW, 0)} W</dd>`;
  };
  panel();

  const vp = root.querySelector('#viewport');
  let scene = null;
  let disposed = false;
  createMoonScene(vp, { mode: 'explorer' }).then((s) => {
    root.querySelector('#vp-loading')?.remove();
    if (!s) {
      vp.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<div class="loading">${esc(t('explorer.unavailable'))}</div>`);
      return;
    }
    if (disposed) return s.dispose();
    scene = s;
    vp.querySelector('#vp-overlay').innerHTML = `<span class="badge ${s.textureKind === 'IMPORTED' ? 'IMPORTED' : 'EXTERNAL'}" style="background:rgba(0,0,0,.55)">${esc(t('explorer.texture'))}: ${esc(s.textureLabel)}</span>`;
    if (!s.hasLayers) root.querySelector('#layer-note').textContent = t('explorer.noLayers');
    s.setSites(sites, 0, rec);
    const p = a.points[idx];
    s.setSunEarth(p.sunSub, p.earthSub);
    Object.entries(layerState).forEach(([k, v]) => (k === 'base' ? s.setBaseMap(v) : s.setLayer(k, v)));
    s.focusSite();
  });

  root.querySelector('#ex-range').addEventListener('input', (ev) => {
    idx = Number(ev.target.value);
    panel();
    const p = a.points[idx];
    scene?.setSunEarth(p.sunSub, p.earthSub);
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-layer]').forEach((cb) =>
    cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
      layerState[cb.dataset.layer] = cb.checked;
      scene?.setLayer(cb.dataset.layer, cb.checked);
      const btn = root.querySelector(`[data-cmd="${cb.dataset.layer}"]`);
      btn?.setAttribute('aria-pressed', cb.checked);
    }),
  );
  root.querySelector('#basemap').addEventListener('click', (ev) => {
    const b = ev.target.closest('[data-base]');
    if (!b) return;
    layerState.base = b.dataset.base;
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-base]').forEach((x) => x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', x === b));
    scene?.setBaseMap(layerState.base);
  });
  root.querySelector('.controls').addEventListener('click', (ev) => {
    const b = ev.target.closest('[data-cmd]');
    if (!b || !scene) return;
    const cmd = b.dataset.cmd;
    if (cmd === 'reset') scene.resetView();
    else if (cmd === 'focus') scene.focusSite();
    else {
      layerState[cmd] = !layerState[cmd];
      scene.setLayer(cmd, layerState[cmd]);
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', layerState[cmd]);
      const cb = root.querySelector(`[data-layer="${cmd}"]`);
      if (cb) cb.checked = layerState[cmd];
    }
  });

  return () => {
    disposed = true;
    if (scene) {
      try {
        setState({ snapshot: scene.snapshot() }, { silent: true });
      } catch {
        /* ignore */
      }
      scene.dispose();
    }
  };
}


######################################################################
# FILE 19/38: public/src/pages/planner.js
######################################################################

import { esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState, setState, emptyMission } from '../state/store.js';
import { PRESET_SITES, presetById, DEMO_MISSIONS } from '../data/sites.js';
import { validateMission, analyzeCurrentMission, saveCurrentMission } from '../services/missionService.js';
import { modal, sourceBadge } from '../components/ui.js';

const OBJECTIVES = ['science', 'exploration', 'resource', 'technology', 'custom'];
const VEHICLE_SUGGESTIONS = ['Robotic lander', 'Crewed lander (HLS-class)', 'Pressurized rover', 'Unpressurized rover', 'CubeSat lander', 'Sample-return lander'];

let errors = {};

export function render(root) {
  let dispose = null;
  const rerender = () => {
    dispose?.();
    dispose = mount(root, rerender);
  };
  rerender();
  return () => dispose?.();
}

function field(name, label, inner, { full = false, hint = '' } = {}) {
  return `<label class="field ${full ? 'full' : ''}">${esc(label)}${inner}
    ${hint ? `<span class="hint">${hint}</span>` : ''}
    <span class="err" id="err-${name}">${errors[name] ? esc(t(errors[name])) : ''}</span></label>`;
}

function mount(root, rerender) {
  const { mission: m, settings, analysis } = getState();
  const sites = PRESET_SITES.filter((s) => m.region === 'custom' || s.region === m.region);
  const inv = (n) => (errors[n] ? 'aria-invalid="true"' : '');
  const tr = m.terrain || {};

  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    <div class="page-head">
      <div>
        <p class="sub">${esc(t('planner.subtitle'))}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="row">
        ${m.id ? `<span class="badge EXTERNAL">${esc(t('planner.editing'))}: ${esc(m.name)}</span>` : `<span class="badge EXTERNAL">${esc(t('planner.newMission'))}</span>`}
        ${analysis ? `<a class="btn sm ghost" href="#dashboard">${esc(t('planner.viewDashboard'))}</a>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>

    ${settings.demoMode ? `<div class="banner demo"><span>${esc(t('planner.demoLoad'))}</span>
      <select id="demo-pick" style="width:auto;min-height:34px;padding:4px 10px">
        <option value="">—</option>${DEMO_MISSIONS.map((d) => `<option value="${d.id}">${esc(d.name)}</option>`).join('')}
      </select></div>` : ''}

    <form id="planner-form" class="grid g3" novalidate>
      <div class="card span2">
        <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('planner.section.mission'))}</span></div>
        <div class="form-grid">
          ${field('name', t('planner.name'), `<input name="name" value="${esc(m.name)}" ${inv('name')} required placeholder="${esc(t('planner.namePh'))}"/>`, { full: true })}
          ${field('objective', t('planner.objective'), `<select name="objective">${OBJECTIVES.map((o) => `<option value="${o}" ${m.objective === o ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(t(`objective.${o}`))}</option>`).join('')}</select>`)}
          ${m.objective === 'custom'
            ? field('objectiveCustom', t('planner.objectiveCustom'), `<input name="objectiveCustom" value="${esc(m.objectiveCustom)}" ${inv('objectiveCustom')} />`)
            : field('objectiveNote', t('planner.objectiveNote'), `<input name="objectiveCustom" value="${esc(m.objectiveCustom)}" placeholder="${esc(t('planner.optional'))}"/>`)}
          ${field('vehicleType', t('planner.vehicle'), `<input name="vehicleType" list="vehicle-list" value="${esc(m.vehicleType)}" ${inv('vehicleType')} placeholder="${esc(t('planner.vehiclePh'))}"/>
            <datalist id="vehicle-list">${VEHICLE_SUGGESTIONS.map((v) => `<option value="${esc(v)}">`).join('')}</datalist>`, { hint: esc(t('planner.vehicleHint')) })}
          ${field('duration', t('planner.duration'), `<input name="duration" type="number" min="0.25" max="28" step="0.25" value="${esc(m.duration)}" ${inv('duration')} />`, { hint: esc(t('planner.durationHint')) })}
          ${field('date', t('planner.date'), `<input name="date" type="date" value="${esc(m.date)}" ${inv('date')} />`)}
          ${field('time', t('planner.time'), `<input name="time" type="time" value="${esc(m.time)}" ${inv('time')} />`, { hint: 'UTC' })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('planner.section.site'))}</span></div>
        <div class="stack">
          ${field('region', t('planner.region'), `<select name="region">
            ${['south', 'north', 'custom'].map((r) => `<option value="${r}" ${m.region === r ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(t(`region.${r}`))}</option>`).join('')}</select>`)}
          ${field('siteId', t('planner.site'), `<select name="siteId">
            ${sites.map((s) => `<option value="${s.id}" ${m.siteId === s.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(t(s.nameKey))}</option>`).join('')}
            <option value="custom" ${m.siteId === 'custom' ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(t('site.custom'))}</option></select>`, { hint: esc(t('planner.siteHint')) })}
          ${m.siteId === 'custom' ? field('siteName', t('planner.siteName'), `<input name="siteName" value="${esc(m.siteName)}" />`) : ''}
          <div class="form-grid">
            ${field('latitude', t('planner.lat'), `<input name="latitude" type="number" step="0.001" min="-90" max="90" value="${esc(m.latitude)}" ${inv('latitude')} />`, { hint: esc(t('planner.latHint')) })}
            ${field('longitude', t('planner.lon'), `<input name="longitude" type="number" step="0.001" min="-180" max="360" value="${esc(m.longitude)}" ${inv('longitude')} />`, { hint: esc(t('planner.lonHint')) })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card span3">
        <div class="card-head">
          <span class="card-title">${esc(t('planner.section.terrain'))}</span>
          <span class="row">${sourceBadge('IMPORTED')} <span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('planner.terrainOptional'))}</span></span>
        </div>
        <p class="small muted" style="margin-bottom:12px">${t('planner.terrainHelp')}</p>
        <div class="grid g3">
          ${field('elevationM', t('planner.elev'), `<input name="terrain.elevationM" type="number" step="1" value="${esc(tr.elevationM ?? '')}" placeholder="${esc(t('planner.demoIfEmpty'))}"/>`)}
          ${field('slopeDeg', t('planner.slope'), `<input name="terrain.slopeDeg" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" max="60" value="${esc(tr.slopeDeg ?? '')}" ${inv('slopeDeg')} placeholder="${esc(t('planner.demoIfEmpty'))}"/>`)}
          ${field('horizonDeg', t('planner.horizon'), `<input name="terrain.horizonDeg" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" max="30" value="${esc(tr.horizonDeg ?? '')}" ${inv('horizonDeg')} placeholder="${esc(t('planner.demoIfEmpty'))}"/>`)}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="span3 row" style="justify-content:flex-end">
        <button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-action="reset">${esc(t('planner.reset'))}</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn" data-action="save">${esc(t('planner.save'))}</button>
        <button type="submit" class="btn primary">${esc(t('planner.analyze'))}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  </div>`;

  const form = root.querySelector('#planner-form');

  const readForm = () => {
    const cur = getState().mission;
    const fd = new FormData(form);
    const next = { ...cur, terrain: { ...(cur.terrain || {}) } };
    for (const [k, v] of fd.entries()) {
      if (k.startsWith('terrain.')) next.terrain[k.slice(8)] = v;
      else next[k] = v;
    }
    next.latitude = next.latitude === '' ? '' : Number(next.latitude);
    next.longitude = next.longitude === '' ? '' : Number(next.longitude);
    next.duration = Number(next.duration);
    return next;
  };

  const onInput = (e) => {
    const next = readForm();
    setState({ mission: next }, { silent: true });
    const name = e.target.name;
    if (name === 'region') {
      const first = PRESET_SITES.find((s) => s.region === next.region);
      if (next.region !== 'custom' && first) Object.assign(next, { siteId: first.id, latitude: first.lat, longitude: first.lon });
      else next.siteId = 'custom';
      setState({ mission: next }, { silent: true });
      rerender();
    } else if (name === 'siteId') {
      const p = presetById(next.siteId);
      if (p) Object.assign(next, { latitude: p.lat, longitude: p.lon, siteName: '' });
      setState({ mission: next }, { silent: true });
      rerender();
    } else if (name === 'objective') {
      rerender();
    } else if (name === 'latitude' || name === 'longitude') {
      // manual coordinates → custom site unless they still match the preset
      const p = presetById(next.siteId);
      if (p && (p.lat !== next.latitude || p.lon !== next.longitude)) {
        next.siteId = 'custom';
        next.region = 'custom';
        setState({ mission: next }, { silent: true });
      }
    }
  };
  form.addEventListener('change', onInput);
  form.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT') setState({ mission: readForm() }, { silent: true });
  });

  const showErrors = (errs) => {
    errors = errs;
    rerender();
    const first = root.querySelector('[aria-invalid="true"]');
    first?.focus();
  };

  form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setState({ mission: readForm() }, { silent: true });
    const errs = validateMission(getState().mission);
    if (Object.keys(errs).length) return showErrors(errs);
    errors = {};
    const r = await analyzeCurrentMission();
    if (r.ok) location.hash = '#dashboard';
    else if (Object.keys(r.errors || {}).length) showErrors(r.errors);
  });

  root.querySelector('[data-action="save"]').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    setState({ mission: readForm() }, { silent: true });
    const errs = validateMission(getState().mission);
    if (errs.name) return showErrors({ name: errs.name });
    errors = {};
    await saveCurrentMission();
    rerender();
  });

  root.querySelector('[data-action="reset"]').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const ok = await modal({ title: t('planner.resetTitle'), body: esc(t('planner.resetBody')), confirmText: t('planner.reset'), danger: true });
    if (!ok) return;
    errors = {};
    setState({ mission: emptyMission(), analysis: null, comparison: { sites: null, analyses: [], result: null, computedAt: null }, snapshot: null });
  });

  root.querySelector('#demo-pick')?.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const d = DEMO_MISSIONS.find((x) => x.id === e.target.value);
    if (!d) return;
    errors = {};
    setState({ mission: { ...emptyMission(), ...d, id: null }, analysis: null, comparison: { sites: null, analyses: [], result: null, computedAt: null } });
  });

  return () => {};
}


######################################################################
# FILE 20/38: public/src/pages/reports.js
######################################################################

import { esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { modal, toast, showLoader, hideLoader } from '../components/ui.js';
import { buildReportHTML, buildReportCharts, captureSnapshot, downloadPDF, exportCSV } from '../services/report.js';
import { saveCurrentMission, refreshHistory, loadMissionRecord, duplicateMissionRecord, deleteMissionRecord } from '../services/missionService.js';
import { presetById } from '../data/sites.js';
import { num, lat, lon } from '../utils/format.js';

function historyTable(history, currentId) {
  if (!history.length) return `<p class="muted small">${esc(t('history.empty'))}</p>`;
  return `<div class="table-wrap"><table>
    <thead><tr><th>${esc(t('history.name'))}</th><th>${esc(t('history.date'))}</th><th>${esc(t('history.site'))}</th><th>${esc(t('history.score'))}</th><th>${esc(t('history.status'))}</th><th>${esc(t('history.actions'))}</th></tr></thead>
    <tbody>${history.map((h) => {
      const site = presetById(h.siteId) ? t(presetById(h.siteId).nameKey) : h.siteName || t('site.custom');
      return `<tr ${h.id === currentId ? 'style="background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--accent) 7%,transparent)"' : ''}>
        <td><b>${esc(h.name)}</b>${h.demo ? ' <span class="badge DEMO">DEMO</span>' : ''}</td>
        <td class="num">${esc(h.date)} ${esc(h.time)}</td>
        <td>${esc(site)}<div class="tiny muted mono">${lat(h.latitude)}, ${lon(h.longitude)}</div></td>
        <td class="num">${h.score === null || h.score === undefined ? '—' : `${num(h.score, 0)}/100`}</td>
        <td>${esc(t(`status.${h.status === 'analyzed' ? 'analyzed' : 'draft'}`))}</td>
        <td><div class="row" style="flex-wrap:nowrap">
          <button class="btn sm" data-h="view" data-id="${esc(h.id)}">${esc(t('history.view'))}</button>
          <button class="btn sm" data-h="edit" data-id="${esc(h.id)}">${esc(t('history.edit'))}</button>
          <button class="btn sm" data-h="dup" data-id="${esc(h.id)}">${esc(t('history.duplicate'))}</button>
          <button class="btn sm danger" data-h="del" data-id="${esc(h.id)}">${esc(t('history.delete'))}</button>
        </div></td></tr>`;
    }).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

export function render(root) {
  const { analysis: a, mission: m, history } = getState();
  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    <div class="page-head"><div><p class="sub">${esc(t('report.subtitle'))}</p></div>
      <div class="row no-print">
        <button class="btn" data-act="save">${esc(t('planner.save'))}</button>
        <button class="btn" data-act="csv" ${a ? '' : 'disabled'}>${esc(t('report.csv'))}</button>
        <button class="btn primary" data-act="pdf" ${a ? '' : 'disabled'}>${esc(t('report.pdf'))}</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="card no-print" id="history-card">
      <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('history.title'))}</span><span class="tiny muted">${esc(t(getState().backend === 'remote' ? 'history.remote' : 'history.local'))}</span></div>
      <div id="history-body">${historyTable(history, m.id)}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="mt" id="report-host">${a ? `<div class="card"><div class="row"><div class="spinner" style="width:22px;height:22px"></div><span class="muted">${esc(t('report.building'))}</span></div></div>` : `<div class="card empty"><h2>${esc(t('empty.title'))}</h2><p>${esc(t('report.noAnalysis'))}</p><a class="btn primary" href="#planner">${esc(t('empty.action'))}</a></div>`}</div>
  </div>`;

  let alive = true;
  if (a) {
    (async () => {
      const charts = buildReportCharts(a, getState().comparison);
      const snapshot = await captureSnapshot();
      if (!alive) return;
      root.querySelector('#report-host').innerHTML = buildReportHTML({ charts, snapshot });
    })();
  }
  refreshHistory().then(() => {
    if (!alive) return;
    const body = root.querySelector('#history-body');
    if (body) body.innerHTML = historyTable(getState().history, getState().mission.id);
  });

  const onClick = async (e) => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-act],[data-h]');
    if (!b || b.disabled) return;
    if (b.dataset.act === 'save') await saveCurrentMission();
    if (b.dataset.act === 'csv') {
      exportCSV();
      toast(t('toast.csv'), 'success');
    }
    if (b.dataset.act === 'pdf') {
      const doc = root.querySelector('#report-doc');
      if (!doc) return;
      showLoader(t('report.pdfBuilding'));
      try {
        const mode = await downloadPDF(doc, `lunar-ai_${(m.name || 'mission').replace(/[^\w؀-ۿ-]+/g, '_')}_${m.date}.pdf`);
        if (mode === 'pdf') toast(t('toast.pdf'), 'success');
      } catch (err) {
        toast(`${t('toast.pdfFailed')}: ${err.message}`, 'error', { force: true });
        window.print();
      } finally {
        hideLoader();
      }
    }
    const id = b.dataset.id;
    const rec = id && getState().history.find((h) => h.id === id);
    if (!rec) return;
    if (b.dataset.h === 'view') {
      loadMissionRecord(rec);
      location.hash = rec.analysis ? '#dashboard' : '#planner';
    } else if (b.dataset.h === 'edit') {
      loadMissionRecord(rec);
      location.hash = '#planner';
    } else if (b.dataset.h === 'dup') {
      await duplicateMissionRecord(rec);
    } else if (b.dataset.h === 'del') {
      const ok = await modal({ title: t('history.deleteTitle'), body: esc(t('history.deleteBody', { name: rec.name })), confirmText: t('history.delete'), danger: true });
      if (ok) await deleteMissionRecord(rec);
    }
    const body = root.querySelector('#history-body');
    if (body) body.innerHTML = historyTable(getState().history, getState().mission.id);
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', onClick);
  return () => {
    alive = false;
    root.removeEventListener('click', onClick);
  };
}

import { esc, icons } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { sourceBadge } from '../components/ui.js';

/**
 * Official NASA / JPL sources (all five from the challenge brief) and how each is integrated.
 * status: LIVE = called through our backend, IMPORTED = dataset bundled/proxied, EXTERNAL = reference tool.
 */
export const RESOURCES = [
  { id: 'trek', name: 'NASA Moon Trek', url: 'https://trek.nasa.gov/moon/', status: 'EXTERNAL', purposes: 6, screens: ['planner', 'explorer', 'analysis'] },
  { id: 'quickmap', name: 'NASA QuickMap', url: 'https://quickmap.lroc.asu.edu/', status: 'EXTERNAL', purposes: 5, screens: ['planner', 'compare', 'explorer'] },
  { id: 'horizons', name: 'JPL Horizons', url: 'https://ssd.jpl.nasa.gov/horizons/', api: 'https://ssd-api.jpl.nasa.gov/doc/horizons.html', status: 'LIVE', purposes: 6, screens: ['dashboard', 'analysis', 'explorer', 'compare', 'risk', 'reports'] },
  { id: 'lro', name: 'NASA LRO Data Products', url: 'https://science.nasa.gov/mission/lro/data-products/', extra: 'https://svs.gsfc.nasa.gov/4720', status: 'IMPORTED', purposes: 4, screens: ['explorer', 'reports'] },
  { id: 'moonres', name: 'NASA Moon Resources', url: 'https://science.nasa.gov/solar-system/resources/resource-packages/earths-moon-resources/', status: 'EXTERNAL', purposes: 2, screens: ['resources', 'reports'] },
];

const SCREEN_KEYS = { planner: 'nav.planner', dashboard: 'nav.dashboard', analysis: 'nav.analysis', explorer: 'nav.explorer', compare: 'nav.compare', risk: 'nav.risk', reports: 'nav.reports', resources: 'nav.resources' };

function card(r) {
  const purposes = Array.from({ length: r.purposes }, (_, i) => t(`res.${r.id}.p${i + 1}`));
  return `
  <article class="card resource-card" id="src-${r.id}">
    <div class="res-head">
      <div>
        <h3>${esc(r.name)}</h3>
        <a class="res-url mono" href="${r.url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(r.url.replace(/^https:\/\//, ''))}</a>
      </div>
      ${sourceBadge(r.status)}
    </div>
    <dl>
      <div><dt>${esc(t('res.provides'))}</dt><dd>${esc(t(`res.${r.id}.provides`))}</dd></div>
      <div><dt>${esc(t('res.purpose'))}</dt><dd><ul class="purpose">${purposes.map((p) => `<li>${esc(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></dd></div>
      <div><dt>${esc(t('res.uses'))}</dt><dd>${esc(t(`res.${r.id}.uses`))}</dd></div>
      <div><dt>${esc(t('res.usedIn'))}</dt><dd class="row" style="gap:6px">${r.screens.map((s) => `<a class="chip" href="#${s}">${esc(t(SCREEN_KEYS[s]))}</a>`).join('')}</dd></div>
    </dl>
    <div class="row res-actions">
      <a class="btn sm primary" href="${r.url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(t('res.open'))} ${icons.ext}<span class="sr-only">(${esc(t('res.newTab'))})</span></a>
      ${r.api ? `<a class="btn sm" href="${r.api}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(t('res.apiDocs'))} ${icons.ext}</a>` : ''}
      ${r.extra ? `<a class="btn sm" href="${r.extra}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(t('res.svs'))} ${icons.ext}</a>` : ''}
    </div>
  </article>`;
}

export function render(root) {
  const screens = ['planner', 'dashboard', 'analysis', 'explorer', 'compare', 'risk', 'reports'];
  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    <div class="page-head"><div><p class="sub">${esc(t('res.subtitle'))}</p></div>
      <div class="row">
        <span class="row">${sourceBadge('LIVE')}<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('res.legend.live'))}</span></span>
        <span class="row">${sourceBadge('IMPORTED')}<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('res.legend.imported'))}</span></span>
        <span class="row">${sourceBadge('EXTERNAL')}<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('res.legend.external'))}</span></span>
        <span class="row">${sourceBadge('ESTIMATED')}<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('res.legend.estimated'))}</span></span>
        <span class="row">${sourceBadge('DEMO')}<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('res.legend.demo'))}</span></span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="grid g3">${RESOURCES.map(card).join('')}
      <article class="card resource-card">
        <div class="res-head"><h3>${esc(t('res.arch.title'))}</h3>${sourceBadge('LIVE')}</div>
        <div class="arch">
          <div class="arch-node">${esc(t('res.arch.browser'))}</div><div class="arch-arrow">↓ <span class="mono tiny">GET /api/horizons</span></div>
          <div class="arch-node accent">${esc(t('res.arch.backend'))}<span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('res.arch.backendNote'))}</span></div><div class="arch-arrow">↓ <span class="mono tiny">horizons.api · CENTER=coord@301</span></div>
          <div class="arch-node">JPL Horizons API<span class="tiny muted">ssd.jpl.nasa.gov</span></div>
        </div>
        <p class="tiny muted">${esc(t('res.arch.fallback'))}</p>
      </article>
    </div>

    <div class="card mt">
      <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('res.matrix'))}</span></div>
      <div class="table-wrap"><table>
        <thead><tr><th>${esc(t('res.source'))}</th><th>${esc(t('res.integration'))}</th>${screens.map((s) => `<th>${esc(t(SCREEN_KEYS[s]))}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
        <tbody>${RESOURCES.map((r) => `<tr><td><a href="#src-${r.id}" data-scroll="src-${r.id}"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b></a></td><td>${sourceBadge(r.status)}</td>
          ${screens.map((s) => `<td style="text-align:center">${r.screens.includes(s) ? '<span style="color:var(--ok)">●</span>' : '<span class="muted">—</span>'}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
          <tr><td><b>${esc(t('res.method.title'))}</b></td><td>${sourceBadge('ESTIMATED')}</td>${screens.map((s) => `<td style="text-align:center">${['dashboard', 'analysis', 'explorer', 'compare', 'risk', 'reports'].includes(s) ? '<span style="color:var(--est)">○</span>' : '<span class="muted">—</span>'}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
        </tbody></table></div>
      <p class="tiny muted mt-s">${esc(t('res.matrixNote'))}</p>
    </div>

    <div class="card mt">
      <div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('res.method.title'))}</span>${sourceBadge('ESTIMATED')}</div>
      <p class="small">${esc(t('res.method.body'))}</p>
    </div>
  </div>`;
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-scroll]').forEach((a) =>
    a.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      root.querySelector(`#${a.dataset.scroll}`)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    }),
  );
  return () => {};
}

# FILE 22/38: public/src/pages/risk.js

import { esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { emptyState, scoreRing, levelChip, sourceBadge } from '../components/ui.js';
import { dataBanner, sourcesPanel } from '../components/sourcesPanel.js';
import { RISK_KEYS, sensitivityStudy, riskLevel } from '../services/analysisEngine.js';
import { riskExplanation, whyThisSite } from '../services/insights.js';
import { siteLabel } from '../services/missionService.js';
import { num } from '../utils/format.js';

export function render(root) {
  const { analysis: a, mission, settings, comparison: c } = getState();
  if (!a) {
    root.innerHTML = `<div class="page">${emptyState('empty.title', 'empty.text', '#planner', 'empty.action')}</div>`;
    return () => {};
  }
  const sens = sensitivityStudy({ ...mission, latitude: a.site.lat, longitude: a.site.lon }, settings);
  const color = (s) => (s >= 70 ? 'var(--ok)' : s >= 40 ? 'var(--warn)' : 'var(--bad)');
  const recI = c?.result?.recommendedIndex ?? null;

  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    ${dataBanner(a)}
    <div class="page-head"><div><p class="sub">${esc(t('risk.subtitle'))}</p></div></div>

    <div class="grid g2">
      <div class="card"><div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('risk.overallRisk'))}</span>${levelChip(riskLevel(100 - a.overallRisk))}</div>
        <div class="score-wrap">${scoreRing(100 - a.overallRisk, { size: 150, label: t('risk.overallRisk') }).replace(`>${Math.round(100 - a.overallRisk)}<`, `>${Math.round(a.overallRisk)}<`)}
        <p class="small muted" style="flex:1;min-width:200px">${esc(t('risk.overallRiskNote'))}</p></div></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('risk.overallSuit'))}</span>${levelChip(riskLevel(a.scores.overall))}</div>
        <div class="score-wrap">${scoreRing(a.scores.overall, { size: 150, label: t('risk.overallSuit') })}
        <p class="small muted" style="flex:1;min-width:200px">${esc(t('risk.overallSuitNote'))}</p></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="grid g3 mt">
      ${RISK_KEYS.map((k) => {
        const r = a.risks[k];
        return `<div class="card risk-card" data-risk="${k}">
          <div class="head"><h3>${esc(t(`risk.${k}`))}</h3>${levelChip(r.level)}</div>
          <div class="meter"><i style="width:${100 - r.score}%;background:${color(r.score)}"></i></div>
          <div class="row between tiny muted mt-s"><span>${esc(t('risk.riskIndex'))}: <span class="mono">${num(100 - r.score, 0)}</span></span><span>${esc(t('risk.weight'))} ${Math.round(a.weights[k] * 100)}%</span></div>
          <p class="small mt-s">${esc(riskExplanation(k, a))}</p>
        </div>`;
      }).join('')}
    </div>

    <div class="grid g2 mt">
      <div class="card"><div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('risk.why'))}</span></div>
        <ul class="list small">${whyThisSite(a).map((w) => `<li>${esc(w)}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('risk.change'))}</span>${sourceBadge('ESTIMATED')}</div>
        <div class="table-wrap"><table>
          <thead><tr><th>${esc(t('risk.scenario'))}</th><th>${esc(t('metric.overall'))}</th><th>Δ</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody><tr><td>${esc(t('risk.baseline'))}</td><td class="num">${num(sens.base, 0)}</td><td class="num">—</td></tr>
          ${sens.cases.map((x) => `<tr><td>${esc(t(x.key))}</td><td class="num">${num(x.score, 0)}</td><td class="num" style="color:${x.delta >= 0.5 ? 'var(--ok)' : x.delta <= -0.5 ? 'var(--bad)' : 'var(--text-2)'}">${x.delta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${num(x.delta, 0)}</td></tr>`).join('')}
          </tbody></table></div>
        <p class="tiny muted mt-s">${esc(t('risk.changeNote'))}</p>
      </div>
    </div>

    ${c?.result ? `<div class="card mt"><div class="card-head"><span class="card-title">${esc(t('risk.bySite'))}</span></div>
      <div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>${esc(t('compare.site'))}</th>${RISK_KEYS.map((k) => `<th>${esc(t(`risk.${k}`))}</th>`).join('')}<th>${esc(t('metric.overall'))}</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>${c.sites.map((s, i) => `<tr><td class="${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}"><b>${esc(s.key)}${recI === i ? ' ★' : ''}</b> ${esc(siteLabel(s))}</td>
        ${RISK_KEYS.map((k) => `<td class="${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}">${levelChip(c.analyses[i].risks[k].level)}</td>`).join('')}
        <td class="num ${recI === i ? 'rec' : ''}">${num(c.analyses[i].scores.overall, 0)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></div>` : ''}

    <div class="mt">${sourcesPanel(a)}</div>
  </div>`;
  return () => {};
}

# FILE 23/38: public/src/pages/settings.js

import { esc } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState, updateSettings, DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../state/store.js';
import { WEIGHT_PRESETS } from '../services/analysisEngine.js';
import { toast } from '../components/ui.js';

const seg = (name, options, value) => `<div class="seg" role="group" data-seg="${name}">
  ${options.map(([v, label]) => `<button type="button" data-v="${v}" aria-pressed="${String(value) === String(v)}">${esc(label)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;

export function render(root) {
  const s = getState().settings;
  const cards = Object.keys(DEFAULT_SETTINGS.dashboardCards);
  root.innerHTML = `
  <div class="page">
    <div class="page-head"><div><p class="sub">${esc(t('settings.subtitle'))}</p></div></div>
    <div class="grid g2">
      <div class="card stack">
        <div class="card-title">${esc(t('settings.appearance'))}</div>
        <div class="row between"><span>${esc(t('settings.language'))}</span>${seg('lang', [['en', 'English'], ['ar', 'العربية']], s.lang)}</div>
        <div class="row between"><span>${esc(t('settings.theme'))}</span>${seg('theme', [['dark', t('settings.dark')], ['light', t('settings.light')]], s.theme)}</div>
        <div class="row between"><span>${esc(t('settings.units'))}</span>${seg('units', [['metric', t('settings.metric')], ['imperial', t('settings.imperial')]], s.units)}</div>
        <div class="row between"><span>${esc(t('settings.decimals'))}</span>${seg('decimals', [[1, '1'], [2, '2'], [3, '3']], s.decimals)}</div>
        <label class="row between check"><span>${esc(t('settings.notifications'))}</span><input type="checkbox" data-bool="notifications" ${s.notifications ? 'checked' : ''}/></label>
      </div>

      <div class="card stack">
        <div class="card-title">${esc(t('settings.data'))}</div>
        <label class="row between check"><span><b>${esc(t('settings.demo'))}</b><br><span class="tiny muted">${esc(t('settings.demoHint'))}</span></span><input type="checkbox" data-bool="demoMode" ${s.demoMode ? 'checked' : ''}/></label>
        <label class="field">${esc(t('settings.minDte'))}<input type="number" data-num="minDteElevation" min="0" max="20" step="0.5" value="${s.minDteElevation}"/><span class="hint">${esc(t('settings.minDteHint'))}</span></label>
        <div class="row between"><span>${esc(t('settings.panel'))}</span>${seg('panelType', [['vertical', t('settings.panel.vertical')], ['horizontal', t('settings.panel.horizontal')]], s.panelType)}</div>
        <div class="form-grid">
          <label class="field">${esc(t('settings.eff'))}<input type="number" data-num="panelEfficiency" min="0.05" max="0.5" step="0.01" value="${s.panelEfficiency}"/></label>
          <label class="field">${esc(t('settings.area'))}<input type="number" data-num="panelAreaM2" min="0.1" max="500" step="0.1" value="${s.panelAreaM2}"/></label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card stack">
        <div class="card-title">${esc(t('settings.dashboard'))}</div>
        <label class="field">${esc(t('settings.weights'))}
          <select data-sel="weightsPreset">${Object.keys(WEIGHT_PRESETS).map((k) => `<option value="${k}" ${s.weightsPreset === k ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(t(`weights.${k}`))}</option>`).join('')}</select>
          <span class="hint">${esc(Object.entries(WEIGHT_PRESETS[s.weightsPreset]).map(([k, w]) => `${t(`cat.${k}`)} ${Math.round(w * 100)}%`).join(' · '))}</span></label>
        <div class="small muted">${esc(t('settings.cards'))}</div>
        <div class="grid g2" style="gap:8px">${cards.map((k) => `<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-card="${k}" ${s.dashboardCards[k] ? 'checked' : ''}/> ${esc(t(`card.${k}`))}</label>`).join('')}</div>
      </div>

      <div class="card stack">
        <div class="card-title">${esc(t('settings.account'))}</div>
        <div class="small">${esc(t('nav.signedInAs'))}: <b>${esc(getState().user?.email || '')}</b></div>
        <div class="small muted">${esc(t(getState().backend === 'remote' ? 'auth.storeRemote' : 'auth.storeLocal'))}</div>
        <div class="small muted">${esc(t('settings.reanalyzeNote'))}</div>
        <div class="row"><button class="btn danger" data-action="logout">${esc(t('nav.logout'))}</button></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`;

  root.querySelectorAll('[data-seg]').forEach((g) =>
    g.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const b = e.target.closest('[data-v]');
      if (!b) return;
      const key = g.dataset.seg;
      const v = key === 'decimals' ? Number(b.dataset.v) : b.dataset.v;
      updateSettings({ [key]: v });
    }),
  );
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-bool]').forEach((cb) =>
    cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
      updateSettings({ [cb.dataset.bool]: cb.checked });
      if (cb.dataset.bool === 'demoMode') toast(cb.checked ? t('toast.demoOn') : t('toast.demoOff'), 'info', { force: true });
    }),
  );
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-num]').forEach((inp) =>
    inp.addEventListener('change', () => {
      const v = Number(inp.value);
      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v >= Number(inp.min) && v <= Number(inp.max)) updateSettings({ [inp.dataset.num]: v });
      else inp.value = getState().settings[inp.dataset.num];
    }),
  );
  root.querySelector('[data-sel="weightsPreset"]').addEventListener('change', (e) => updateSettings({ weightsPreset: e.target.value }));
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-card]').forEach((cb) =>
    cb.addEventListener('change', () => updateSettings({ dashboardCards: { ...getState().settings.dashboardCards, [cb.dataset.card]: cb.checked } })),
  );
  return () => {};
}


######################################################################
# FILE 24/38: public/src/services/analysisEngine.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Analysis Engine.
 *
 * Turns (mission + ephemeris + terrain) into time series, metrics, scores and risks.
 * Every number produced here is derived from the Scientific Data Layer; nothing is hard-coded.
 *
 * Scoring is a TRANSPARENT HEURISTIC for decision support (weights visible in Settings);
 * it is not a validated NASA suitability model.
 */
import { SOLAR_CONSTANT, SUN_RADIUS_KM, EARTH_RADIUS_KM, angularRadius, diskVisibleFraction } from './ephemeris.js';
import { getEphemerisSeries, getSunPosition, getEarthPosition } from './scienceData.js';
import { resolveTerrain } from './terrain.js';

export const WEIGHT_PRESETS = {
  balanced: { power: 0.25, communication: 0.2, terrain: 0.2, illumination: 0.15, landing: 0.1, schedule: 0.1 },
  power: { power: 0.35, communication: 0.15, terrain: 0.15, illumination: 0.2, landing: 0.075, schedule: 0.075 },
  communication: { power: 0.2, communication: 0.35, terrain: 0.15, illumination: 0.1, landing: 0.1, schedule: 0.1 },
  safety: { power: 0.15, communication: 0.15, terrain: 0.3, illumination: 0.1, landing: 0.2, schedule: 0.1 },
};

export const RISK_KEYS = ['power', 'communication', 'terrain', 'illumination', 'landing', 'schedule'];

const clamp = (v, a = 0, b = 100) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, v));

export function riskLevel(score) {
  if (score >= 70) return 'LOW';
  if (score >= 40) return 'MODERATE';
  return 'HIGH';
}

function missionStart(mission) {
  const [hh = '0', mm = '0'] = String(mission.time || '00:00').split(':');
  const day0 = new Date(`${mission.date}T00:00:00Z`);
  return { day0, startHour: Number(hh) + Number(mm) / 60 };
}

export function buildTimeGrid(mission) {
  const { day0, startHour } = missionStart(mission);
  const durationH = clamp(Number(mission.duration || 1) * 24, 1, 28 * 24);
  const startIdx = Math.floor(startHour);
  const gridHours = Math.min(720, Math.max(24, startIdx + Math.ceil(durationH) + 72));
  const times = [];
  for (let h = 0; h <= gridHours; h++) times.push(new Date(day0.getTime() + h * 3600000));
  return { times, startIdx, durationH, endIdx: Math.min(gridHours, startIdx + Math.ceil(durationH)) };
}

/* ---------- reusable scientific calculations ---------- */

export function calculateSolarElevation(sun) {
  return sun.el;
}

/** Fraction of the solar disk above the local terrain horizon (0..1). */
export function calculateSunVisibility(sun, terrain) {
  const r = angularRadius(SUN_RADIUS_KM, sun.distKm);
  return diskVisibleFraction(sun.el, terrain.horizonAt(sun.az), r);
}

export function calculateEarthVisibility(earth, terrain) {
  const r = angularRadius(EARTH_RADIUS_KM, earth.distKm);
  return diskVisibleFraction(earth.el, terrain.horizonAt(earth.az), r);
}

/**
 * Electrical power (W) for a solar array.
 * vertical: sun-tracking vertical array (typical for polar landers) — incidence factor cos(el)
 * horizontal: flat deck array — incidence factor sin(el)
 */
export function calculatePowerPotential(sun, sunFrac, settings) {
  const S = SOLAR_CONSTANT / (sun.distAU * sun.distAU);
  const el = (Math.max(0, sun.el) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const incidence = settings.panelType === 'horizontal' ? Math.sin(el) : Math.cos(el);
  return S * settings.panelEfficiency * settings.panelAreaM2 * sunFrac * Math.max(0, incidence);
}

export function calculateSunlightDuration(points, from, to) {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) h += points[i].sunFrac;
  return h;
}

function findTransition(points, from, predicate) {
  for (let i = from + 1; i < points.length; i++) if (predicate(points[i])) return i - from;
  return null;
}

function longestRun(points, from, to, pred) {
  let best = 0;
  let cur = 0;
  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
    if (pred(points[i])) {
      cur++;
      best = Math.max(best, cur);
    } else cur = 0;
  }
  return best;
}

export function calculateCommunicationWindow(points, from, to) {
  const visibleH = points.slice(from, to).filter((p) => p.earthVisible).length;
  const dteH = points.slice(from, to).filter((p) => p.dte).length;
  const longestH = longestRun(points, from, to, (p) => p.dte);
  let currentH = 0;
  for (let i = from; i < to && points[i].dte; i++) currentH++;
  const n = Math.max(1, to - from);
  return { visibleFraction: visibleH / n, dteFraction: dteH / n, longestH, currentH };
}

export function calculateTerrainRisk(terrain) {
  const s = terrain.slopeDeg;
  let slopeScore;
  if (s <= 5) slopeScore = 100;
  else if (s <= 10) slopeScore = 100 - (s - 5) * 6; // 100 -> 70
  else if (s <= 15) slopeScore = 70 - (s - 10) * 7; // 70 -> 35
  else slopeScore = Math.max(0, 35 - (s - 15) * 3.5); // 35 -> 0 at 25°
  const score = clamp(slopeScore * (1 - 0.35 * terrain.roughness));
  return { score, level: riskLevel(score), slopeScore };
}

export function calculateSuitabilityScore(scores, weights) {
  let sum = 0;
  let wsum = 0;
  for (const k of RISK_KEYS) {
    sum += (scores[k] ?? 0) * weights[k];
    wsum += weights[k];
  }
  return clamp(sum / wsum);
}

/* ---------- main pipeline ---------- */

export async function analyzeMission(mission, settings, { allowLive = !settings.demoMode } = {}) {
  const grid = buildTimeGrid(mission);
  const site = { lat: Number(mission.latitude), lon: Number(mission.longitude), altKm: 0 };
  const eph = await getEphemerisSeries(site, grid.times, { allowLive });
  return computeFromEphemeris(mission, settings, grid, eph);
}

/** Fast estimate-only analysis (used for sensitivity studies; never calls the network). */
export function analyzeMissionEstimated(mission, settings) {
  const grid = buildTimeGrid(mission);
  const site = { lat: Number(mission.latitude), lon: Number(mission.longitude), altKm: 0 };
  const eph = {
    sun: grid.times.map((t) => getSunPosition(site, t)),
    earth: grid.times.map((t) => getEarthPosition(site, t)),
    source: 'ESTIMATED',
    reason: 'SENSITIVITY',
  };
  return computeFromEphemeris(mission, settings, grid, eph);
}

function computeFromEphemeris(mission, settings, grid, eph) {
  const terrain = resolveTerrain(mission);
  const minDte = Number(settings.minDteElevation ?? 1);

  const points = grid.times.map((t, i) => {
    const sun = eph.sun[i];
    const earth = eph.earth[i];
    const sunFrac = calculateSunVisibility(sun, terrain);
    const earthFrac = calculateEarthVisibility(earth, terrain);
    const earthMask = terrain.horizonAt(earth.az);
    return {
      t: t.toISOString(),
      h: i,
      sunEl: sun.el,
      sunAz: sun.az,
      sunFrac,
      sunMask: terrain.horizonAt(sun.az),
      earthEl: earth.el,
      earthAz: earth.az,
      earthMask,
      earthFrac,
      earthVisible: earthFrac > 0.5,
      dte: earth.el >= earthMask + minDte,
      powerW: calculatePowerPotential(sun, sunFrac, settings),
      sunDistAU: sun.distAU,
      earthDistKm: earth.distKm,
      sunSub: [sun.subLat, sun.subLon],
      earthSub: [earth.subLat, earth.subLon],
    };
  });

  const { startIdx, endIdx, durationH } = grid;
  const p0 = points[startIdx];
  const winN = Math.max(1, endIdx - startIdx);
  const lit0 = p0.sunFrac >= 0.5;

  const sunlightHours = calculateSunlightDuration(points, startIdx, endIdx);
  const sunlightFraction = sunlightHours / winN;
  const timeUntilSunsetH = lit0 ? findTransition(points, startIdx, (p) => p.sunFrac < 0.5) : 0;
  const timeUntilSunriseH = lit0 ? 0 : findTransition(points, startIdx, (p) => p.sunFrac >= 0.5);
  const earthsetH = p0.earthVisible ? findTransition(points, startIdx, (p) => !p.earthVisible) : 0;
  const comm = calculateCommunicationWindow(points, startIdx, endIdx);
  const win = points.slice(startIdx, endIdx);
  const avgPowerW = win.reduce((s, p) => s + p.powerW, 0) / win.length;
  const peakPowerW = Math.max(...win.map((p) => p.powerW));
  const energyKWh = win.reduce((s, p) => s + p.powerW, 0) / 1000;
  const idealW = SOLAR_CONSTANT * settings.panelEfficiency * settings.panelAreaM2;
  const longestDarkH = longestRun(points, startIdx, endIdx, (p) => p.sunFrac < 0.5);
  const minSunEl = Math.min(...win.map((p) => p.sunEl));
  const maxSunEl = Math.max(...win.map((p) => p.sunEl));
  const maxEarthEl = Math.max(...win.map((p) => p.earthEl));
  const terrainRisk = calculateTerrainRisk(terrain);

  // ---- scores (0..100, higher = better) ----
  const illumination = clamp(sunlightFraction * 100 - Math.min(30, longestDarkH / 4));
  const power = clamp((avgPowerW / idealW) * 100);
  const communication = clamp((0.5 * comm.visibleFraction + 0.5 * comm.dteFraction) * 100);
  const terrainScore = terrainRisk.score;
  const slopeLanding = terrain.slopeDeg <= 8 ? 100 : clamp(100 - (terrain.slopeDeg - 8) * 8.3);
  const lightingAtLanding = p0.sunFrac < 0.5 ? 0.55 : p0.sunEl < 3 ? 0.85 : 1; // low sun = long shadows
  const landing = clamp(slopeLanding * lightingAtLanding * (p0.dte ? 1 : 0.85));
  let schedule;
  if (lit0) schedule = timeUntilSunsetH === null ? 100 : clamp((timeUntilSunsetH / durationH) * 100);
  else schedule = timeUntilSunriseH === null ? 0 : clamp(30 * (1 - timeUntilSunriseH / durationH));

  const scores = { power, communication, terrain: terrainScore, illumination, landing, schedule };
  const weights = WEIGHT_PRESETS[settings.weightsPreset] || WEIGHT_PRESETS.balanced;
  const overall = calculateSuitabilityScore(scores, weights);
  const risks = Object.fromEntries(RISK_KEYS.map((k) => [k, { score: scores[k], level: riskLevel(scores[k]) }]));
  const overallRisk = clamp(100 - overall);

  return {
    missionId: mission.id,
    computedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    site: { lat: Number(mission.latitude), lon: Number(mission.longitude) },
    sources: {
      ephemeris: eph.source, // LIVE | ESTIMATED
      ephemerisReason: eph.reason || null,
      liveError: eph.liveError || null,
      ephemerisMeta: eph.meta || null,
      terrain: terrain.source, // IMPORTED | MIXED | DEMO
      terrainFields: terrain.fieldSources,
      demoMode: Boolean(settings.demoMode),
    },
    grid: { startIdx, endIdx, durationH, start: points[0].t },
    points,
    terrain: {
      elevationM: terrain.elevationM,
      slopeDeg: terrain.slopeDeg,
      roughness: terrain.roughness,
      horizonDeg: terrain.horizonBase,
      horizonProfile: Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, i) => +terrain.horizonAt(i * 10).toFixed(2)),
    },
    metrics: {
      sunEl: p0.sunEl,
      sunAz: p0.sunAz,
      sunVisible: lit0,
      sunlightHours,
      sunlightFraction,
      timeUntilSunsetH,
      timeUntilSunriseH,
      longestDarkH,
      minSunEl,
      maxSunEl,
      earthEl: p0.earthEl,
      earthAz: p0.earthAz,
      earthVisible: p0.earthVisible,
      earthVisibleFraction: comm.visibleFraction,
      dteNow: p0.dte,
      dteFraction: comm.dteFraction,
      earthsetH,
      commLongestH: comm.longestH,
      commCurrentH: comm.currentH,
      maxEarthEl,
      avgPowerW,
      peakPowerW,
      energyKWh,
      idealW,
      windowHours: winN,
    },
    scores: { ...scores, overall },
    weights,
    risks,
    overallRisk,
  };
}

/** Compare analysed sites: best value per metric + recommendation (by overall score). */
export const COMPARE_METRICS = [
  { key: 'sunEl', path: (a) => a.metrics.sunEl, better: 'max', unit: '°' },
  { key: 'sunlightHours', path: (a) => a.metrics.sunlightHours, better: 'max', unit: 'h' },
  { key: 'avgPowerW', path: (a) => a.metrics.avgPowerW, better: 'max', unit: 'W' },
  { key: 'earthVisibleFraction', path: (a) => a.metrics.earthVisibleFraction * 100, better: 'max', unit: '%' },
  { key: 'commLongestH', path: (a) => a.metrics.commLongestH, better: 'max', unit: 'h' },
  { key: 'elevationM', path: (a) => a.terrain.elevationM, better: 'none', unit: 'm' },
  { key: 'slopeDeg', path: (a) => a.terrain.slopeDeg, better: 'min', unit: '°' },
  { key: 'terrainRisk', path: (a) => 100 - a.scores.terrain, better: 'min', unit: '' },
  { key: 'overall', path: (a) => a.scores.overall, better: 'max', unit: '/100' },
];

export function compareSites(analyses) {
  const valid = analyses.map((a, i) => ({ a, i })).filter((x) => x.a);
  const best = {};
  for (const m of COMPARE_METRICS) {
    if (m.better === 'none' || valid.length < 2) continue;
    const vals = valid.map(({ a, i }) => ({ i, v: m.path(a) }));
    if (Math.max(...vals.map((x) => x.v)) - Math.min(...vals.map((x) => x.v)) < 1e-9) continue; // all equal: no winner
    const target = m.better === 'max' ? Math.max(...vals.map((x) => x.v)) : Math.min(...vals.map((x) => x.v));
    best[m.key] = vals.filter((x) => Math.abs(x.v - target) < 1e-9).map((x) => x.i);
  }
  if (!valid.length) return { best, recommendedIndex: null, reasons: [] };
  const rec = valid.reduce((p, c) => (c.a.scores.overall > p.a.scores.overall ? c : p));
  const others = valid.filter((x) => x.i !== rec.i);
  const reasons = [];
  const avg = (fn) => others.reduce((s, x) => s + fn(x.a), 0) / Math.max(1, others.length);
  if (others.length) {
    const r = rec.a;
    if (r.metrics.sunEl > avg((a) => a.metrics.sunEl)) reasons.push({ key: 'reason.solar', v: r.metrics.sunEl });
    if (r.metrics.sunlightHours > avg((a) => a.metrics.sunlightHours)) reasons.push({ key: 'reason.sunlight', v: r.metrics.sunlightHours });
    if (r.metrics.avgPowerW > avg((a) => a.metrics.avgPowerW)) reasons.push({ key: 'reason.power', v: r.metrics.avgPowerW });
    if (r.metrics.earthVisibleFraction > avg((a) => a.metrics.earthVisibleFraction)) reasons.push({ key: 'reason.earth', v: r.metrics.earthVisibleFraction * 100 });
    if (r.scores.terrain > avg((a) => a.scores.terrain)) reasons.push({ key: 'reason.terrain', v: r.terrain.slopeDeg });
  }
  return { best, recommendedIndex: rec.i, reasons };
}

/**
 * Sensitivity study: re-run the (estimated) analysis with perturbed inputs to show
 * what could change the recommendation. All results are ESTIMATED.
 */
export function sensitivityStudy(mission, settings) {
  const base = analyzeMissionEstimated(mission, settings).scores.overall;
  const addDays = (d, n) => {
    const x = new Date(`${d}T00:00:00Z`);
    x.setUTCDate(x.getUTCDate() + n);
    return x.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  };
  const towardEquator = Math.sign(mission.latitude || -1) * -0.5;
  const t = mission.terrain || {};
  const cases = [
    { key: 'sens.date7', m: { ...mission, date: addDays(mission.date, 7) } },
    { key: 'sens.date14', m: { ...mission, date: addDays(mission.date, 14) } },
    { key: 'sens.coord', m: { ...mission, latitude: Number(mission.latitude) + towardEquator } },
    { key: 'sens.lon', m: { ...mission, longitude: Number(mission.longitude) + 90 } },
    { key: 'sens.steep', m: { ...mission, terrain: { ...t, slopeDeg: (Number(t.slopeDeg) || resolveTerrain(mission).slopeDeg) + 6 } } },
    { key: 'sens.dte', m: mission, s: { ...settings, minDteElevation: Number(settings.minDteElevation || 1) + 3 } },
  ];
  return {
    base,
    cases: cases.map((c) => {
      const score = analyzeMissionEstimated(c.m, c.s || settings).scores.overall;
      return { key: c.key, score, delta: score - base };
    }),
  };
}


######################################################################
# FILE 25/38: public/src/services/api.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Persistence adapter.
 *   remote — Node backend (/api/auth, /api/missions)
 *   local  — browser localStorage (used automatically when the backend is not reachable,
 *            e.g. when index.html is served by a static host). Passwords are SHA-256 + salt hashed.
 */
import { getState, setState } from '../state/store.js';
import { checkBackend } from './scienceData.js';
import { getItem, setItem, uid, sha256 } from '../utils/storage.js';

const LOCAL_USERS = 'lunar-ai-users';
const LOCAL_MISSIONS = 'lunar-ai-missions';

const readLS = (k) => {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(getItem(k) || '{}');
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
};
const writeLS = (k, v) => setItem(k, JSON.stringify(v));

async function remote(path, { method = 'GET', body } = {}) {
  const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
  const { token } = getState();
  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  const r = await fetch(path, { method, headers, body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined });
  const j = await r.json().catch(() => ({ ok: false, error: 'BAD_RESPONSE' }));
  return j;
}

export async function initBackend() {
  const ok = await checkBackend();
  setState({ backend: ok ? 'remote' : 'local' }, { silent: true });
  return ok ? 'remote' : 'local';
}

const isRemote = () => getState().backend === 'remote';

export async function register(email, password) {
  if (isRemote()) return remote('/api/auth/register', { method: 'POST', body: { email, password } });
  const users = readLS(LOCAL_USERS);
  const key = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (users[key]) return { ok: false, error: 'EMAIL_EXISTS' };
  const salt = uid();
  users[key] = { email: key, salt, hash: await sha256(salt + password), createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
  writeLS(LOCAL_USERS, users);
  return { ok: true };
}

export async function login(email, password) {
  if (isRemote()) return remote('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: { email, password } });
  const u = readLS(LOCAL_USERS)[email.trim().toLowerCase()];
  if (!u || (await sha256(u.salt + password)) !== u.hash) return { ok: false, error: 'INVALID_CREDENTIALS' };
  return { ok: true, token: `local-${uid()}`, user: { email: u.email } };
}

export async function logout() {
  if (isRemote()) await remote('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});
}

export async function forgotPassword(email) {
  if (isRemote()) return remote('/api/auth/forgot', { method: 'POST', body: { email } });
  return { ok: false, error: 'EMAIL_SERVICE_NOT_CONFIGURED' };
}

export async function verifySession() {
  const { token, user } = getState();
  if (!token || !user) return false;
  if (!isRemote()) return token.startsWith('local-');
  const r = await remote('/api/auth/me').catch(() => ({ ok: false }));
  return r.ok;
}

export async function listMissions() {
  if (isRemote()) {
    const r = await remote('/api/missions');
    return r.ok ? r.missions : [];
  }
  const all = readLS(LOCAL_MISSIONS)[getState().user?.email] || {};
  return Object.values(all).sort((a, b) => (b.updatedAt || '').localeCompare(a.updatedAt || ''));
}

export async function saveMission(record) {
  if (isRemote()) {
    const r = await remote('/api/missions', { method: 'POST', body: record });
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.error || 'SAVE_FAILED');
    return r.mission;
  }
  const email = getState().user?.email;
  const all = readLS(LOCAL_MISSIONS);
  all[email] = all[email] || {};
  const id = record.id || uid();
  const rec = { ...record, id, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
  all[email][id] = rec;
  writeLS(LOCAL_MISSIONS, all);
  return rec;
}

export async function deleteMission(id) {
  if (isRemote()) return remote(`/api/missions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
  const email = getState().user?.email;
  const all = readLS(LOCAL_MISSIONS);
  if (all[email]) delete all[email][id];
  writeLS(LOCAL_MISSIONS, all);
  return { ok: true };
}
