# FILE 26/38: public/src/services/assistant.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Mission Assistant.
 *
 * 1) If the server has ANTHROPIC_API_KEY configured, questions are sent to /api/assistant
 *    together with a compact JSON of the CURRENT mission analysis (LLM mode).
 * 2) Otherwise a deterministic rule-based engine answers from the same data.
 * Both modes only use computed mission data — no generic chatbot answers.
 */
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { t, lang } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { sensitivityStudy } from './analysisEngine.js';
import { generateMissionSummary, riskExplanation } from './insights.js';
import { num, deg, pct } from '../utils/format.js';
import { presetById } from '../data/sites.js';

const label = (s) => (presetById(s.siteId) ? t(presetById(s.siteId).nameKey) : s.name || t('site.custom'));

function compactContext() {
  const { mission, analysis, comparison } = getState();
  if (!analysis) return { mission, analysis: null };
  return {
    mission,
    analysis: { sources: analysis.sources, metrics: analysis.metrics, scores: analysis.scores, risks: analysis.risks, terrain: { ...analysis.terrain, horizonProfile: undefined } },
    comparison: comparison?.result
      ? {
          recommendedSite: comparison.sites[comparison.result.recommendedIndex]?.key,
          sites: comparison.sites.map((s, i) => ({ key: s.key, label: label(s), lat: s.lat, lon: s.lon, date: s.date, time: s.time, overall: comparison.analyses[i]?.scores.overall, metrics: comparison.analyses[i]?.metrics })),
        }
      : null,
  };
}

const has = (q, words) => words.some((w) => q.includes(w));

export function ruleBasedAnswer(question) {
  const q = question.toLowerCase();
  const { analysis: a, comparison: c, mission, settings } = getState();
  // Lunar resources / water ice — reference knowledge, answerable even before an analysis
  if (has(q, ['ice', 'water', 'volatile', 'resource', 'جليد', 'ثلج', 'مياه', 'الماء', 'ماء', 'موارد'])) {
    const la = Number(a ? a.site.lat : mission.latitude);
    const lo = Number(a ? a.site.lon : mission.longitude);
    const near = (pl, pn) => Math.abs(la - pl) < 1.2 && Math.abs(((lo - pn + 540) % 360) - 180) < 8;
    let site = t('ai.ice.lowLat');
    if (near(-85.3, -42.1)) site = t('ai.ice.cabeus');
    else if (near(-89.67, 129.78)) site = t('ai.ice.shackleton');
    else if (Math.abs(la) >= 80) site = t('ai.ice.polar');
    else if (Math.abs(la) >= 70) site = t('ai.ice.highLat');
    return `${t('ai.ice.general')}\n\n${site}\n\n${t('ai.ice.caveat')}`;
  }

  if (!a) return t('ai.noAnalysis');
  const m = a.metrics;
  const src = t(`src.${a.sources.ephemeris}`);

  // Which location has the best communication visibility?
  if (has(q, ['communication visibility', 'best communication', 'best comm', 'earth visibility', 'أفضل اتصال', 'رؤية الأرض', 'أفضل رؤية'])) {
    if (!c?.result) {
      return t('ai.commBestSingle', { dtePct: pct(m.dteFraction), earthPct: pct(m.earthVisibleFraction), maxEarthEl: deg(m.maxEarthEl) });
    }
    const ranked = c.sites.map((x, k) => ({ x, a: c.analyses[k] })).sort((p1, p2) => p2.a.metrics.earthVisibleFraction - p1.a.metrics.earthVisibleFraction || p2.a.metrics.maxEarthEl - p1.a.metrics.maxEarthEl);
    const lines = ranked.map((r) => `• ${t('compare.site')} ${r.x.key} (${label(r.x)}): ${pct(r.a.metrics.earthVisibleFraction)} · DTE ${pct(r.a.metrics.dteFraction)} · max ${deg(r.a.metrics.maxEarthEl)}`);
    return t('ai.commBest', { site: `${t('compare.site')} ${ranked[0].x.key} — ${label(ranked[0].x)}` }) + '\n' + lines.join('\n');
  }

  // What are the illumination conditions?
  if (has(q, ['illumination', 'sunlight', 'lighting', 'light', 'shadow', 'إضاءة', 'الإضاءة', 'ضوء', 'ظل'])) {
    return t('ai.illum', {
      sunEl: deg(m.sunEl), minSun: deg(m.minSunEl), maxSun: deg(m.maxSunEl), sunlight: num(m.sunlightHours, 0), window: num(m.windowHours, 0),
      pct: pct(m.sunlightFraction), dark: num(m.longestDarkH, 0),
      next: m.sunVisible ? (m.timeUntilSunsetH === null ? t('common.beyondWindow') : `${num(m.timeUntilSunsetH, 0)} ${t('unit.h')}`) : t('analysis.sunBelow'),
      src,
    });
  }

  // What are the main mission risks?
  if (has(q, ['main risk', 'mission risk', 'risks', 'المخاطر', 'مخاطر'])) {
    const order = Object.entries(a.risks).sort((x, y) => x[1].score - y[1].score);
    const lines = order.map(([k, r]) => `• ${t(`risk.${k}`)} — ${t(`risk.level.${r.level}`)}: ${riskExplanation(k, a)}`);
    return t('ai.risk', { overall: num(a.overallRisk, 0) }) + '\n' + lines.join('\n');
  }

  // Which site is better? / Why is Site X better?
  if (has(q, ['which site', 'better', 'best', 'recommend', 'landing site', 'موقع هبوط', 'أين', 'أفضل', 'احسن', 'أحسن', 'توصي', 'افضل', 'why is site', 'لماذا الموقع', 'ليه الموقع'])) {
    if (!c?.result || c.result.recommendedIndex === null) return t('ai.noComparison');
    const i = c.result.recommendedIndex;
    const s = c.sites[i];
    const reasons = c.result.reasons.map((r) => '• ' + t(r.key, { v: num(r.v, 1) })).join('\n');
    const table = c.sites.map((x, k) => `${t('compare.site')} ${x.key} (${label(x)}): ${num(c.analyses[k].scores.overall, 0)}/100`).join('\n');
    return t('ai.best', { site: `${t('compare.site')} ${s.key} — ${label(s)}`, score: num(c.analyses[i].scores.overall, 0) }) + '\n' + table + (reasons ? `\n\n${t('ai.because')}\n${reasons}` : '');
  }

  // What if I change the landing date?
  if (has(q, ['date', 'day', 'when', 'تاريخ', 'يوم', 'موعد', 'امتى', 'متى'])) {
    const sens = sensitivityStudy(mission, settings);
    const lines = sens.cases.filter((x) => x.key.startsWith('sens.date')).map((x) => `• ${t(x.key)}: ${num(x.score, 0)}/100 (${x.delta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${num(x.delta, 0)})`);
    return t('ai.date', { base: num(sens.base, 0) }) + '\n' + lines.join('\n') + '\n\n' + t('ai.dateExplain');
  }

  // How does solar elevation affect power?
  if (has(q, ['power', 'solar', 'energy', 'panel', 'طاقة', 'الطاقة', 'شمس', 'الشمس', 'ألواح', 'كهرباء'])) {
    return t('ai.power', {
      sunEl: deg(m.sunEl), sunlight: num(m.sunlightHours, 0), window: num(m.windowHours, 0), power: num(m.avgPowerW, 0),
      peak: num(m.peakPowerW, 0), panel: t(`settings.panel.${settings.panelType}`), src,
    });
  }

  // Why is communication unavailable?
  if (has(q, ['comm', 'earth', 'dte', 'signal', 'اتصال', 'الاتصال', 'الأرض', 'الارض', 'إشارة'])) {
    const key = m.dteNow ? 'ai.commOk' : 'ai.commNo';
    return t(key, {
      earthEl: deg(m.earthEl), horizon: deg(a.points[a.grid.startIdx]?.earthMask, 1),
      min: deg(settings.minDteElevation, 1), dtePct: pct(m.dteFraction), maxEarthEl: deg(m.maxEarthEl), longest: num(m.commLongestH, 0), src,
    });
  }

  // Explain this chart
  if (has(q, ['chart', 'graph', 'plot', 'رسم', 'الرسم', 'مخطط', 'منحنى'])) {
    return t('ai.chart', { minSun: deg(m.minSunEl), maxSun: deg(m.maxSunEl), maxEarth: deg(m.maxEarthEl), sunlightPct: pct(m.sunlightFraction), dtePct: pct(m.dteFraction) });
  }

  if (has(q, ['terrain', 'slope', 'elevation', 'تضاريس', 'انحدار', 'ميل', 'ارتفاع الموقع'])) {
    return t('ai.terrain', { slope: deg(a.terrain.slopeDeg, 1), rough: num(a.terrain.roughness, 2), horizon: deg(a.terrain.horizonDeg, 1), score: num(a.scores.terrain, 0), src: t(`src.${a.sources.terrain}`) });
  }

  if (has(q, ['risk', 'danger', 'safe', 'خطر', 'مخاطر', 'أمان'])) {
    const lines = Object.entries(a.risks).map(([k, r]) => `• ${t(`risk.${k}`)}: ${t(`risk.level.${r.level}`)} (${num(r.score, 0)})`);
    return t('ai.risk', { overall: num(a.overallRisk, 0) }) + '\n' + lines.join('\n');
  }

  if (has(q, ['sunset', 'dark', 'night', 'غروب', 'ظلام', 'ليل'])) {
    return t('ai.sunset', { sunset: m.timeUntilSunsetH === null ? t('common.beyondWindow') : num(m.timeUntilSunsetH, 0) + ' ' + t('unit.h'), dark: num(m.longestDarkH, 0) });
  }

  if (has(q, ['source', 'live', 'nasa', 'jpl', 'data', 'مصدر', 'بيانات', 'ناسا'])) {
    return t('ai.sources', { eph: src, terr: t(`src.${a.sources.terrain}`) });
  }

  const s = generateMissionSummary(a, c);
  return `${t('ai.summaryIntro')}\n• ${s.strength}\n• ${s.risk}\n• ${s.recommendation}\n\n${t('ai.tryAsking')}`;
}

export async function ask(question) {
  const health = window.__lunarHealth;
  if (health?.llmAssistant && getState().analysis) {
    try {
      const r = await fetch('/api/assistant', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ question, context: compactContext(), lang: lang() }),
      });
      const j = await r.json();
      if (j.ok) return { text: j.answer, engine: 'llm' };
    } catch {
      /* fall back to rule-based */
    }
  }
  return { text: ruleBasedAnswer(question), engine: 'rules' };
}


######################################################################
# FILE 27/38: public/src/services/ephemeris.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Analytic lunar ephemeris (Scientific Data Layer — ESTIMATED source).
 *
 * Used when live JPL Horizons data is unavailable, and in DEMO mode.
 * Method: Jean Meeus, "Astronomical Algorithms" (2nd ed.)
 *   - Ch. 25  low-precision solar coordinates            (~0.01°)
 *   - Ch. 47  Moon position, main periodic terms only     (~0.3° in λ, ~0.2° in β)
 *   - Ch. 53  optical libration (sub-Earth point) and the
 *             Sun's selenographic coordinates (sub-solar point)
 * Physical libration and nutation are neglected (< ~0.05°).
 *
 * Expected accuracy of local Sun/Earth elevation at a lunar site: a few tenths of a degree.
 * That is adequate for planning visualisation, but NOT for operations — near the poles
 * the Sun skims the horizon, so always confirm with LIVE JPL Horizons data.
 *
 * Coordinate frame: selenographic (mean-Earth / polar-axis style)
 *   x -> (lat 0, lon 0)   y -> (lat 0, lon 90°E)   z -> north pole
 */

export const MOON_RADIUS_KM = 1737.4;
export const AU_KM = 149597870.7;
export const EARTH_RADIUS_KM = 6378.137;
export const SUN_RADIUS_KM = 695700;
export const SOLAR_CONSTANT = 1361; // W/m² at 1 AU (TSI)

const D2R = Math.PI / 180;
const R2D = 180 / Math.PI;
const sin = (d) => Math.sin(d * D2R);
const cos = (d) => Math.cos(d * D2R);
const norm360 = (d) => ((d % 360) + 360) % 360;
export const norm180 = (d) => {
  const x = norm360(d);
  return x > 180 ? x - 360 : x;
};

export function julianDay(date) {
  return date.getTime() / 86400000 + 2440587.5;
}

function centuries(date) {
  return (julianDay(date) - 2451545.0) / 36525;
}

/** Geocentric apparent ecliptic longitude of the Sun (deg) and distance (AU). Meeus ch.25 */
export function sunGeocentric(date) {
  const T = centuries(date);
  const L0 = 280.46646 + 36000.76983 * T + 0.0003032 * T * T;
  const M = 357.52911 + 35999.05029 * T - 0.0001537 * T * T;
  const e = 0.016708634 - 0.000042037 * T;
  const C = (1.914602 - 0.004817 * T) * sin(M) + (0.019993 - 0.000101 * T) * sin(2 * M) + 0.000289 * sin(3 * M);
  const trueLon = L0 + C;
  const nu = M + C;
  const R = (1.000001018 * (1 - e * e)) / (1 + e * cos(nu));
  const omega = 125.04 - 1934.136 * T;
  const lambda = trueLon - 0.00569 - 0.00478 * sin(omega);
  return { lambda: norm360(lambda), R };
}

/** Geocentric ecliptic coordinates of the Moon (deg, km). Meeus ch.47 main terms. */
export function moonGeocentric(date) {
  const T = centuries(date);
  const Lp = 218.3164477 + 481267.88123421 * T;
  const D = 297.8501921 + 445267.1114034 * T;
  const M = 357.5291092 + 35999.0502909 * T;
  const Mp = 134.9633964 + 477198.8675055 * T;
  const F = 93.272095 + 483202.0175233 * T;

  const lambda =
    Lp +
    6.288774 * sin(Mp) + 1.274027 * sin(2 * D - Mp) + 0.658314 * sin(2 * D) +
    0.213618 * sin(2 * Mp) - 0.185116 * sin(M) - 0.114332 * sin(2 * F) +
    0.058793 * sin(2 * D - 2 * Mp) + 0.057066 * sin(2 * D - M - Mp) +
    0.053322 * sin(2 * D + Mp) + 0.045758 * sin(2 * D - M) -
    0.040923 * sin(M - Mp) - 0.03472 * sin(D) - 0.030383 * sin(M + Mp);

  const beta =
    5.128122 * sin(F) + 0.280602 * sin(Mp + F) + 0.277693 * sin(Mp - F) +
    0.173237 * sin(2 * D - F) + 0.055413 * sin(2 * D - Mp + F) +
    0.046271 * sin(2 * D - Mp - F) + 0.032573 * sin(2 * D + F) + 0.017198 * sin(2 * Mp + F);

  const dist =
    385000.56 - 20905.355 * cos(Mp) - 3699.111 * cos(2 * D - Mp) -
    2955.968 * cos(2 * D) - 569.925 * cos(2 * Mp) + 48.888 * cos(M);

  const Omega = 125.0445479 - 1934.1362891 * T;
  return { lambda: norm360(lambda), beta, dist, F: norm360(F), Omega: norm360(Omega) };
}

const I_MOON = 1.54242; // inclination of mean lunar equator to ecliptic (deg)

/** Selenographic longitude/latitude of the point that directs toward ecliptic direction (lam, bet). Meeus ch.53 */
function selenographic(lam, bet, Omega, F) {
  const W = lam - Omega;
  const A = Math.atan2(sin(W) * cos(bet) * cos(I_MOON) - sin(bet) * sin(I_MOON), cos(W) * cos(bet)) * R2D;
  const lon = norm180(A - F);
  const lat = Math.asin(-sin(W) * cos(bet) * sin(I_MOON) - sin(bet) * cos(I_MOON)) * R2D;
  return { lon, lat };
}

/** Sub-Earth point (optical libration) and Earth–Moon distance. */
export function subEarthPoint(date) {
  const m = moonGeocentric(date);
  const { lon, lat } = selenographic(m.lambda, m.beta, m.Omega, m.F);
  return { lon, lat, distKm: m.dist };
}

/** Sub-solar point on the Moon and Sun–Moon distance. */
export function subSolarPoint(date) {
  const s = sunGeocentric(date);
  const m = moonGeocentric(date);
  const ratio = m.dist / (s.R * AU_KM);
  // heliocentric longitude/latitude of the Moon (Meeus 53)
  const lamH = s.lambda + 180 + ratio * R2D * cos(m.beta) * sin(s.lambda - m.lambda);
  const betH = ratio * m.beta;
  const { lon, lat } = selenographic(lamH, betH, m.Omega, m.F);
  // Sun-Moon distance ≈ Sun-Earth distance (difference < 0.3%)
  return { lon, lat, distKm: s.R * AU_KM, distAU: s.R };
}

function unit(latDeg, lonDeg) {
  return [cos(latDeg) * cos(lonDeg), cos(latDeg) * sin(lonDeg), sin(latDeg)];
}

/**
 * Topocentric azimuth/elevation of a body seen from a lunar surface site.
 * Elevation is relative to the local ellipsoidal (spherical) horizon — terrain NOT included.
 * Azimuth: from local north, clockwise through east (same convention as JPL Horizons).
 */
export function topocentric(site, body) {
  const r = MOON_RADIUS_KM + (site.altKm || 0);
  const up = unit(site.lat, site.lon);
  const obs = up.map((c) => c * r);
  const tgt = unit(body.lat, body.lon).map((c) => c * body.distKm);
  const rel = [tgt[0] - obs[0], tgt[1] - obs[1], tgt[2] - obs[2]];
  const len = Math.hypot(...rel);
  const east = [-sin(site.lon), cos(site.lon), 0];
  const north = [-sin(site.lat) * cos(site.lon), -sin(site.lat) * sin(site.lon), cos(site.lat)];
  const dot = (a, b) => a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2];
  const el = Math.asin(dot(rel, up) / len) * R2D;
  const az = norm360(Math.atan2(dot(rel, east), dot(rel, north)) * R2D);
  return { az, el, rangeKm: len };
}

/** Angular radius (deg) of a sphere of radius r at distance d. */
export function angularRadius(radiusKm, distKm) {
  return Math.asin(radiusKm / distKm) * R2D;
}

/** Fraction of a disk visible above a straight horizon line. x = (centerEl - horizon)/radius */
export function diskVisibleFraction(centerEl, horizonEl, radiusDeg) {
  const x = (centerEl - horizonEl) / radiusDeg;
  if (x >= 1) return 1;
  if (x <= -1) return 0;
  return 1 - (Math.acos(x) - x * Math.sqrt(1 - x * x)) / Math.PI;
}

/** Selenographic unit vector (for 3D view): [x, y, z] with z = north. */
export const selenoUnit = unit;


######################################################################
# FILE 28/38: public/src/services/insights.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Natural-language generation from CALCULATED values only (no invented facts).
 * Used by the Dashboard "AI Mission Summary", Risk page explanations, and the report.
 */
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { RISK_KEYS } from './analysisEngine.js';
import { num, deg, pct } from '../utils/format.js';
import { presetById } from '../data/sites.js';

const label = (s) => (presetById(s.siteId) ? t(presetById(s.siteId).nameKey) : s.name || t('site.custom'));

const hrs = (h) => (h === null || h === undefined ? t('common.beyondWindow') : `${num(h, 0)} ${t('unit.h')}`);

function vals(a) {
  const m = a.metrics;
  return {
    sunEl: deg(m.sunEl),
    maxSunEl: deg(m.maxSunEl),
    sunlight: num(m.sunlightHours, 0),
    sunlightPct: pct(m.sunlightFraction),
    window: num(m.windowHours, 0),
    dark: num(m.longestDarkH, 0),
    sunset: hrs(m.timeUntilSunsetH),
    sunrise: hrs(m.timeUntilSunriseH),
    power: num(m.avgPowerW, 0),
    peak: num(m.peakPowerW, 0),
    energy: num(m.energyKWh, 1),
    earthEl: deg(m.earthEl),
    maxEarthEl: deg(m.maxEarthEl),
    earthPct: pct(m.earthVisibleFraction),
    dtePct: pct(m.dteFraction),
    commLongest: num(m.commLongestH, 0),
    earthset: hrs(m.earthsetH),
    slope: deg(a.terrain.slopeDeg, 1),
    rough: num(a.terrain.roughness, 2),
    horizon: deg(a.terrain.horizonDeg, 1),
    score: num(a.scores.overall, 0),
  };
}

export function generateMissionSummary(a, comparison) {
  if (!a) return null;
  const v = vals(a);
  const sorted = [...RISK_KEYS].sort((x, y) => a.scores[y] - a.scores[x]);
  const strongest = sorted[0];
  const weakest = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
  const m = a.metrics;

  const comm = m.dteFraction >= 0.8 ? t('sum.comm.good', v) : m.dteFraction >= 0.3 ? t('sum.comm.partial', v) : t('sum.comm.poor', v);
  const power = m.sunlightFraction >= 0.7 ? t('sum.power.good', v) : m.sunlightFraction >= 0.3 ? t('sum.power.partial', v) : t('sum.power.poor', v);
  const terrain = a.scores.terrain >= 70 ? t('sum.terrain.good', v) : a.scores.terrain >= 40 ? t('sum.terrain.partial', v) : t('sum.terrain.poor', v);

  let rec = a.scores.overall >= 70 ? t('sum.rec.go', v) : a.scores.overall >= 40 ? t('sum.rec.conditional', v) : t('sum.rec.nogo', v);
  const ri = comparison?.result?.recommendedIndex;
  if (ri !== null && ri !== undefined && ri !== 0 && comparison.sites?.[ri]) {
    rec += ' ' + t('sum.rec.alt', { site: `${t('compare.site')} ${comparison.sites[ri].key} — ${label(comparison.sites[ri])}`, score: num(comparison.analyses[ri].scores.overall, 0) });
  }
  return {
    strength: t(`sum.strength.${strongest}`, { ...v, s: num(a.scores[strongest], 0) }),
    risk: t(`sum.risk.${weakest}`, { ...v, s: num(a.scores[weakest], 0) }),
    comm,
    power,
    terrain,
    recommendation: rec,
    caveat: a.sources.ephemeris === 'LIVE' ? '' : t('sum.caveat'),
  };
}

export function riskExplanation(key, a) {
  const v = vals(a);
  return t(`riskexp.${key}.${a.risks[key].level}`, v);
}

export function whyThisSite(a) {
  const v = vals(a);
  const out = [];
  for (const k of RISK_KEYS) if (a.scores[k] >= 70) out.push(t(`why.${k}`, { ...v, s: num(a.scores[k], 0) }));
  if (!out.length) out.push(t('why.none', v));
  return out;
}


######################################################################
# FILE 29/38: public/src/services/missionService.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Mission actions: validate → analyze → compare → save/load.
 * This is the glue that pushes one mission object through the whole pipeline:
 *   Planner → Scientific Data Layer → Analysis Engine → Comparison → Risk → Report
 */
import { getState, setState, emptyMission } from '../state/store.js';
import { analyzeMission, compareSites } from './analysisEngine.js';
import { PRESET_SITES, presetById } from '../data/sites.js';
import * as api from './api.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { toast, showLoader, hideLoader, updateLoader } from '../components/ui.js';

export function validateMission(m) {
  const e = {};
  if (!String(m.name || '').trim()) e.name = 'val.required';
  const la = Number(m.latitude);
  const lo = Number(m.longitude);
  if (m.latitude === '' || !Number.isFinite(la) || la < -90 || la > 90) e.latitude = 'val.lat';
  if (m.longitude === '' || !Number.isFinite(lo) || lo < -180 || lo > 360) e.longitude = 'val.lon';
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(m.date || '')) e.date = 'val.required';
  else {
    const y = Number(m.date.slice(0, 4));
    if (y < 1900 || y > 2199) e.date = 'val.dateRange';
  }
  if (!/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(m.time || '')) e.time = 'val.required';
  const d = Number(m.duration);
  if (!Number.isFinite(d) || d < 0.25 || d > 28) e.duration = 'val.duration';
  if (!String(m.vehicleType || '').trim()) e.vehicleType = 'val.required';
  if (m.objective === 'custom' && !String(m.objectiveCustom || '').trim()) e.objectiveCustom = 'val.required';
  const tr = m.terrain || {};
  if (tr.slopeDeg !== '' && tr.slopeDeg != null && (Number(tr.slopeDeg) < 0 || Number(tr.slopeDeg) > 60)) e.slopeDeg = 'val.slope';
  if (tr.horizonDeg !== '' && tr.horizonDeg != null && (Number(tr.horizonDeg) < 0 || Number(tr.horizonDeg) > 30)) e.horizonDeg = 'val.horizon';
  return e;
}

/** Round numbers & drop heavy fields so analyses can be stored compactly. */
function compact(analysis, keepPoints = true) {
  const r = (v) => (typeof v === 'number' ? Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000 : v);
  const pts = keepPoints
    ? analysis.points.map((p) => {
        const o = {};
        for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(p)) o[k] = Array.isArray(v) ? v.map(r) : r(v);
        return o;
      })
    : undefined;
  return { ...analysis, points: pts };
}

export function siteLabel(site) {
  return site.siteId && presetById(site.siteId) ? t(presetById(site.siteId).nameKey) : site.name || t('site.custom');
}

/** Default comparison set: Site A = mission site, B/C = other presets in the same region. */
export function defaultComparisonSites(mission) {
  const region = mission.region === 'north' ? 'north' : 'south';
  const others = PRESET_SITES.filter((p) => p.region === region && p.id !== mission.siteId);
  const pool = others.length >= 2 ? others : PRESET_SITES.filter((p) => p.id !== mission.siteId);
  const A = { key: 'A', siteId: mission.siteId || '', name: mission.siteName || '', lat: Number(mission.latitude), lon: Number(mission.longitude), date: mission.date, time: mission.time, fromMission: true };
  const mk = (key, p) => ({ key, siteId: p.id, name: '', lat: p.lat, lon: p.lon, date: mission.date, time: mission.time, fromMission: false });
  return [A, mk('B', pool[0]), mk('C', pool[1])];
}

export async function runComparison(sites, { silent = false } = {}) {
  const { mission, settings, analysis } = getState();
  if (!silent) showLoader(t('loading.compare'));
  try {
    const analyses = [];
    for (const s of sites) {
      if (!silent) updateLoader(`${t('loading.compare')} — ${t('compare.site')} ${s.key}`);
      const m = {
        ...mission,
        id: `${mission.id || 'draft'}-${s.key}`,
        latitude: s.lat,
        longitude: s.lon,
        date: s.date,
        time: s.time,
        // imported terrain only applies to the mission's own site
        terrain: s.fromMission && Number(s.lat) === Number(mission.latitude) && Number(s.lon) === Number(mission.longitude) ? mission.terrain : {},
      };
      const reuse = s.fromMission && analysis && analysis.site.lat === Number(s.lat) && analysis.site.lon === Number(s.lon) && s.date === mission.date && s.time === mission.time;
      const a = reuse ? analysis : await analyzeMission(m, settings);
      analyses.push(compact(a, false));
    }
    const result = compareSites(analyses);
    const named = sites.map((s) => ({ ...s, label: siteLabel(s) }));
    setState({ comparison: { sites: named, analyses, result, computedAt: new Date().toISOString() } });
    return result;
  } finally {
    if (!silent) hideLoader();
  }
}

export async function analyzeCurrentMission() {
  const { mission, settings } = getState();
  const errors = validateMission(mission);
  if (Object.keys(errors).length) return { ok: false, errors };
  showLoader(settings.demoMode ? t('loading.demo') : t('loading.horizons'));
  try {
    const m = { ...mission, latitude: Number(mission.latitude), longitude: Number(mission.longitude) };
    const analysis = compact(await analyzeMission(m, settings));
    setState({ analysis, mission: { ...mission, status: 'analyzed' }, snapshot: null });
    updateLoader(t('loading.compare'));
    const prev = getState().comparison?.sites;
    const sites = prev && prev.length === 3
      ? [{ ...defaultComparisonSites(mission)[0] }, prev[1], prev[2]].map((s) => ({ ...s, date: s.date || mission.date, time: s.time || mission.time }))
      : defaultComparisonSites(mission);
    await runComparison(sites, { silent: true });
    const src = analysis.sources.ephemeris;
    if (src === 'LIVE') toast(t('toast.live'), 'success');
    else if (analysis.sources.ephemerisReason === 'LIVE_FAILED') toast(t('toast.liveFailed'), 'error');
    else toast(t('toast.estimated'), 'info');
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) {
    toast(`${t('toast.analysisFailed')}: ${e.message}`, 'error', { force: true });
    return { ok: false, errors: {} };
  } finally {
    hideLoader();
  }
}

export async function saveCurrentMission() {
  const { mission, analysis, comparison } = getState();
  if (!String(mission.name || '').trim()) {
    toast(t('val.nameToSave'), 'error', { force: true });
    return null;
  }
  const record = {
    ...mission,
    status: analysis && analysis.missionId === mission.id ? 'analyzed' : mission.status || 'draft',
    score: analysis ? analysis.scores.overall : null,
    analysis: analysis || null,
    comparison: comparison?.result ? comparison : null,
  };
  try {
    const saved = await api.saveMission(record);
    const updatedMission = { ...mission, id: saved.id };
    const patch = { mission: updatedMission };
    if (analysis && (!analysis.missionId || analysis.missionId === mission.id)) patch.analysis = { ...analysis, missionId: saved.id };
    setState(patch);
    await refreshHistory();
    toast(t('toast.saved'), 'success');
    return saved;
  } catch (e) {
    toast(`${t('toast.saveFailed')}: ${e.message}`, 'error', { force: true });
    return null;
  }
}

export async function refreshHistory() {
  try {
    const history = await api.listMissions();
    setState({ history });
  } catch {
    setState({ history: [] });
  }
}

export function loadMissionRecord(rec) {
  const { analysis, comparison, score, updatedAt, ...mission } = rec;
  setState({
    mission: { ...emptyMission(), ...mission },
    analysis: analysis || null,
    comparison: comparison || { sites: null, analyses: [], result: null, computedAt: null },
    snapshot: null,
  });
}

export async function duplicateMissionRecord(rec) {
  const copy = { ...rec, id: null, name: `${rec.name} (${t('history.copy')})` };
  await api.saveMission(copy);
  await refreshHistory();
  toast(t('toast.duplicated'), 'success');
}

export async function deleteMissionRecord(rec) {
  await api.deleteMission(rec.id);
  if (getState().mission.id === rec.id) setState({ mission: { ...getState().mission, id: null } });
  await refreshHistory();
  toast(t('toast.deleted'), 'success');
}

/** Make the recommended comparison site the mission site and re-run the analysis. */
export async function adoptRecommendedSite() {
  const { comparison, mission } = getState();
  const i = comparison?.result?.recommendedIndex;
  if (i === null || i === undefined) return;
  const s = comparison.sites[i];
  const region = s.lat < 0 ? 'south' : 'north';
  setState({
    mission: {
      ...mission,
      siteId: s.siteId || 'custom',
      siteName: s.siteId ? '' : s.name,
      region: s.siteId ? region : 'custom',
      latitude: s.lat,
      longitude: s.lon,
      date: s.date,
      time: s.time,
      terrain: s.fromMission ? mission.terrain : { elevationM: '', slopeDeg: '', horizonDeg: '' },
    },
  });
  // keep B/C as the alternatives, A becomes the adopted site
  const sites = comparison.sites.map((x, k) => (k === 0 ? { ...s, key: 'A', fromMission: true } : k === i ? { ...comparison.sites[0], key: x.key, fromMission: false } : x));
  setState({ comparison: { ...comparison, sites } });
  return analyzeCurrentMission();
}

/**
 * Report Generator — HTML report, PDF (html2canvas + jsPDF, print fallback) and CSV.
 */
import { esc, download } from '../utils/dom.js';
import { t, lang } from '../i18n/i18n.js';
import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { RISK_KEYS, COMPARE_METRICS, sensitivityStudy } from './analysisEngine.js';
import { generateMissionSummary, riskExplanation, whyThisSite } from './insights.js';
import { sourceExplanation } from '../components/sourcesPanel.js';
import { siteLabel } from './missionService.js';
import { sourceBadge, levelChip } from '../components/ui.js';
import { num, deg, pct, lat, lon, elev, utc } from '../utils/format.js';
import { presetById } from '../data/sites.js';
import { createMoonScene } from '../three/moonScene.js';
import { sceneSites } from '../pages/explorer.js';
import { RESOURCES } from '../pages/resources.js';

const REPORT_COLORS = { sun: '#c2410c', earth: '#0b6aa8', mask: '#6a7a92', green: '#047857', purple: '#6d28d9' };

/** Render a Chart.js config to a PNG data URL (light palette for print). */
function chartImage(config, w = 760, h = 300) {
  if (typeof window.Chart !== 'function') return null;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = w;
  canvas.height = h;
  canvas.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:0';
  document.body.appendChild(canvas);
  const rtl = lang() === 'ar';
  const chart = new window.Chart(canvas, {
    ...config,
    options: {
      responsive: false,
      animation: false,
      devicePixelRatio: 2,
      plugins: { legend: { rtl, labels: { color: '#0b1b33', boxWidth: 12 } }, title: { display: true, text: config.title, color: '#0b1b33', font: { size: 14 } } },
      scales: config.type === 'radar' ? undefined : {
        x: { ticks: { color: '#43546e', maxTicksLimit: 8, maxRotation: 0 }, grid: { color: '#e5eaf1' } },
        y: { ticks: { color: '#43546e' }, grid: { color: '#e5eaf1' }, ...(config.y || {}) },
      },
    },
  });
  const url = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  chart.destroy();
  canvas.remove();
  return url;
}

export function buildReportCharts(a, c) {
  const P = a.points.slice(0, a.grid.endIdx + 1);
  const labels = P.map((p) => p.t.slice(5, 13).replace('T', ' '));
  const ds = (label, data, color, extra = {}) => ({ label, data, borderColor: color, backgroundColor: color + '33', pointRadius: 0, borderWidth: 2, tension: 0.2, ...extra });
  const imgs = [
    chartImage({ type: 'line', title: t('chart.sunEl'), data: { labels, datasets: [ds(t('chart.sunEl'), P.map((p) => p.sunEl), REPORT_COLORS.sun), ds(t('chart.terrainMask'), P.map((p) => p.sunMask), REPORT_COLORS.mask, { borderDash: [5, 4], borderWidth: 1 })] } }),
    chartImage({ type: 'line', title: t('chart.earthEl'), data: { labels, datasets: [ds(t('chart.earthEl'), P.map((p) => p.earthEl), REPORT_COLORS.earth), ds(t('chart.dteThreshold'), P.map((p) => p.earthMask + Number(getState().settings.minDteElevation)), REPORT_COLORS.mask, { borderDash: [5, 4], borderWidth: 1 })] } }),
    chartImage({ type: 'line', title: t('chart.power'), data: { labels, datasets: [ds(t('chart.power') + ' (W)', P.map((p) => p.powerW), REPORT_COLORS.purple, { fill: true })] } }),
    chartImage({ type: 'line', title: t('chart.comm'), y: { min: 0, max: 1.05 }, data: { labels, datasets: [ds(t('chart.comm'), P.map((p) => (p.dte ? 1 : 0)), REPORT_COLORS.green, { fill: true, stepped: 'before', tension: 0 })] } }),
  ];
  if (c?.result) {
    imgs.push(
      chartImage({
        type: 'bar',
        title: t('chart.suitability'),
        y: { min: 0, max: 100 },
        data: {
          labels: [t('metric.overall'), ...RISK_KEYS.map((k) => t(`cat.${k}`))],
          datasets: c.sites.map((s, i) => ({ label: `${t('compare.site')} ${s.key}`, data: [c.analyses[i].scores.overall, ...RISK_KEYS.map((k) => c.analyses[i].scores[k])], backgroundColor: [REPORT_COLORS.earth, REPORT_COLORS.sun, REPORT_COLORS.purple][i] })),
        },
      }),
    );
  }
  return imgs.filter(Boolean);
}

export async function captureSnapshot() {
  const { snapshot, analysis: a } = getState();
  if (snapshot) return snapshot;
  if (!a) return null;
  const host = document.createElement('div');
  host.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:0;width:900px;height:560px';
  document.body.appendChild(host);
  try {
    const s = await createMoonScene(host, { mode: 'snapshot', width: 900, height: 560 });
    if (!s) return null;
    const { sites, rec } = sceneSites();
    s.setSites(sites, 0, rec);
    const p = a.points[a.grid.startIdx];
    s.setSunEarth(p.sunSub, p.earthSub);
    s.focusSite();
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
    const url = s.snapshot();
    s.dispose();
    return url;
  } catch {
    return null;
  } finally {
    host.remove();
  }
}

export function buildReportHTML({ charts = [], snapshot = null } = {}) {
  const { mission: m, analysis: a, comparison: c, settings, user } = getState();
  const sum = generateMissionSummary(a, c);
  const mm = a.metrics;
  const e = elev(a.terrain.elevationM);
  const site = presetById(m.siteId) ? t(presetById(m.siteId).nameKey) : m.siteName || t('site.custom');
  const recI = c?.result?.recommendedIndex ?? null;
  const sens = sensitivityStudy({ ...m, latitude: a.site.lat, longitude: a.site.lon }, settings);
  const row = (k, v) => `<tr><td>${esc(k)}</td><td class="num">${v}</td></tr>`;
  return `
  <div class="report-doc" id="report-doc" dir="${lang() === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}">
    <div class="row between">

      <div class="small muted" style="text-align:end">${esc(t('report.generated'))}: ${esc(utc(new Date().toISOString()))}<br>${esc(user?.email || '')}</div>
    </div>
    ${a.sources.demoMode ? `<p class="small" style="color:#7e22ce;margin-top:10px"><b>${esc(t('banner.demo'))}</b></p>` : ''}
    <p class="small muted" style="margin-top:10px">${esc(sourceExplanation(a))}</p>

    <h2>1. ${esc(t('report.s.overview'))}</h2>
    <p><b>${esc(m.name)}</b> — ${esc(m.objective === 'custom' ? m.objectiveCustom : t(`objective.${m.objective}`))}${m.objective !== 'custom' && m.objectiveCustom ? ` (${esc(m.objectiveCustom)})` : ''}</p>
    <p class="mt-s">${esc(t('metric.overall'))}: <b>${num(a.scores.overall, 0)} / 100</b> · ${esc(t('risk.overallRisk'))}: ${levelChip(a.scores.overall >= 70 ? 'LOW' : a.scores.overall >= 40 ? 'MODERATE' : 'HIGH')}</p>

    <h2>2. ${esc(t('report.s.params'))}</h2>
    <table><tbody>
      ${row(t('planner.name'), esc(m.name))}
      ${row(t('planner.objective'), esc(m.objective === 'custom' ? m.objectiveCustom : t(`objective.${m.objective}`)))}
      ${row(t('planner.region'), esc(t(`region.${m.region}`)))}
      ${row(t('planner.date'), `${esc(m.date)} ${esc(m.time)} UTC`)}
      ${row(t('planner.duration'), `${num(m.duration, 2)} ${esc(t('unit.days'))}`)}
      ${row(t('planner.vehicle'), esc(m.vehicleType))}
      ${row(t('settings.panel'), `${esc(t(`settings.panel.${settings.panelType}`))}, η=${num(settings.panelEfficiency * 100, 0)}%, ${num(settings.panelAreaM2, 1)} m²`)}
    </tbody></table>

    <h2>3. ${esc(t('report.s.site'))}</h2>
    <table><tbody>
      ${row(t('planner.site'), esc(site))}
      ${row(t('planner.lat'), lat(a.site.lat))}
      ${row(t('planner.lon'), lon(a.site.lon))}
      ${row(t('metric.elevation'), `${e.v} ${e.u} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.terrainFields.elevation)}`)}
    </tbody></table>

    <h2>4. ${esc(t('report.s.solar'))}</h2>
    <table><tbody>
      ${row(t('metric.sunEl'), `${deg(mm.sunEl)} (${esc(t('metric.range'))} ${deg(mm.minSunEl)} … ${deg(mm.maxSunEl)}) ${sourceBadge(a.sources.ephemeris)}`)}
      ${row(t('metric.sunAz'), deg(mm.sunAz, 1))}
      ${row(t('metric.sunlight'), `${num(mm.sunlightHours, 0)} ${esc(t('unit.h'))} / ${num(mm.windowHours, 0)} ${esc(t('unit.h'))} (${pct(mm.sunlightFraction)})`)}
      ${row(t('analysis.untilSunset'), mm.sunVisible ? (mm.timeUntilSunsetH === null ? esc(t('common.beyondWindow')) : `${num(mm.timeUntilSunsetH, 0)} ${esc(t('unit.h'))}`) : esc(t('analysis.sunBelow')))}
      ${row(t('metric.power'), `${num(mm.avgPowerW, 0)} W ${esc(t('analysis.avg'))} · ${esc(t('metric.peak'))} ${num(mm.peakPowerW, 0)} W · ${num(mm.energyKWh, 1)} kWh ${sourceBadge('ESTIMATED')}`)}
    </tbody></table>
    <p class="small mt-s">${esc(sum.power)}</p>

    <h2>5. ${esc(t('report.s.comm'))}</h2>
    <table><tbody>
      ${row(t('metric.earthEl'), `${deg(mm.earthEl)} (${esc(t('metric.max'))} ${deg(mm.maxEarthEl)}) ${sourceBadge(a.sources.ephemeris)}`)}
      ${row(t('analysis.earthAz'), deg(mm.earthAz, 1))}
      ${row(t('metric.earthVis'), pct(mm.earthVisibleFraction))}
      ${row(t('analysis.dte'), pct(mm.dteFraction))}
      ${row(t('metric.comm'), `${num(mm.commLongestH, 0)} ${esc(t('unit.h'))}`)}
    </tbody></table>
    <p class="small mt-s">${esc(sum.comm)}</p>

    <h2>6. ${esc(t('report.s.terrain'))}</h2>
    <table><tbody>
      ${row(t('metric.slope'), `${deg(a.terrain.slopeDeg, 1)} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.terrainFields.slope)}`)}
      ${row(t('analysis.complexity'), num(a.terrain.roughness, 2))}
      ${row(t('analysis.horizonObs'), `${deg(a.terrain.horizonDeg, 1)} ${sourceBadge(a.sources.terrainFields.horizon)}`)}
    </tbody></table>
    <p class="small mt-s">${esc(sum.terrain)}</p>

    <h2>7. ${esc(t('report.s.compare'))}</h2>
    ${c?.result ? `<table><thead><tr><th>${esc(t('compare.metric'))}</th>${c.sites.map((s, i) => `<th>${esc(t('compare.site'))} ${esc(s.key)}${recI === i ? ' ★' : ''}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>
      <tr><td>${esc(t('compare.location'))}</td>${c.sites.map((s) => `<td class="num">${esc(siteLabel(s))}<br>${lat(s.lat)}, ${lon(s.lon)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>
      ${COMPARE_METRICS.map((mt) => `<tr><td>${esc(t(`cmp.${mt.key}`))}</td>${c.analyses.map((x, i) => `<td class="num" ${c.result.best[mt.key]?.includes(i) ? 'style="font-weight:700;color:#047857"' : ''}>${num(mt.path(x), mt.unit === '°' ? 2 : 0)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
    </tbody></table>` : `<p class="muted">${esc(t('compare.notRun'))}</p>`}

    <h2>8. ${esc(t('report.s.risk'))}</h2>
    <table><thead><tr><th>${esc(t('risk.category'))}</th><th>${esc(t('risk.levelH'))}</th><th>${esc(t('risk.explanation'))}</th></tr></thead><tbody>
      ${RISK_KEYS.map((k) => `<tr><td>${esc(t(`risk.${k}`))}</td><td>${levelChip(a.risks[k].level)}</td><td style="white-space:normal">${esc(riskExplanation(k, a))}</td></tr>`).join('')}
    </tbody></table>
    <p class="small mt-s">${esc(t('risk.overallRisk'))}: <b>${num(a.overallRisk, 0)}</b> · ${esc(t('risk.overallSuit'))}: <b>${num(a.scores.overall, 0)}</b></p>
    <p class="small mt-s"><b>${esc(t('risk.change'))}</b> ${sens.cases.map((x) => `${esc(t(x.key))}: ${x.delta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${num(x.delta, 0)}`).join(' · ')}</p>

    <h2>9. ${esc(t('report.s.rec'))}</h2>
    ${recI !== null ? `<p><b>${esc(t('compare.recommendedSite'))}: ${esc(t('compare.site'))} ${esc(c.sites[recI].key)} — ${esc(siteLabel(c.sites[recI]))}</b> (${lat(c.sites[recI].lat)}, ${lon(c.sites[recI].lon)}) · ${num(c.analyses[recI].scores.overall, 0)}/100</p>
      <ul class="list small mt-s">${c.result.reasons.map((r) => `<li>${esc(t(r.key, { v: num(r.v, 1) }))}</li>`).join('')}</ul>` : ''}
    <p class="small mt-s">${esc(sum.recommendation)}</p>
    <ul class="list small mt-s">${whyThisSite(a).map((w) => `<li>${esc(w)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>

    <h2>10. ${esc(t('report.s.charts'))}</h2>
    ${charts.length ? `<div class="rep-charts">${charts.map((src) => `<img src="${src}" alt="chart"/>`).join('')}</div>` : `<p class="muted small">${esc(t('chart.unavailable'))}</p>`}

    <h2>11. ${esc(t('report.s.snapshot'))}</h2>
    ${snapshot ? `<div class="snapshot"><img src="${snapshot}" alt="${esc(t('report.s.snapshot'))}"/></div>` : `<p class="muted small">${esc(t('report.noSnapshot'))}</p>`}

    <h2>12. ${esc(t('report.s.sources'))}</h2>
    <table><thead><tr><th>${esc(t('res.source'))}</th><th>${esc(t('res.integration'))}</th><th>URL</th></tr></thead><tbody>
      ${RESOURCES.map((r) => `<tr><td><b>${esc(r.name)}</b><div class="tiny muted" style="white-space:normal">${esc(t(`res.${r.id}.uses`))}</div></td><td>${sourceBadge(r.id === 'horizons' ? (a.sources.ephemeris === 'LIVE' ? 'LIVE' : 'UNAVAILABLE') : r.status)}</td><td class="mono tiny" style="white-space:normal;word-break:break-all">${esc(r.url)}${r.api ? `<br>${esc(r.api)}` : ''}</td></tr>`).join('')}
    </tbody></table>

    <p class="tiny muted" style="margin-top:26px">${esc(t('report.disclaimer'))}</p>
  </div>`;
}

export async function downloadPDF(el, filename) {
  const { jspdf, html2canvas } = window;
  if (!jspdf?.jsPDF || typeof html2canvas !== 'function') {
    try {
      window.print();
    } catch {
      /* print blocked */
    }
    return 'print';
  }
  const canvas = await html2canvas(el, { scale: 2, backgroundColor: '#ffffff', useCORS: true, logging: false });
  const pdf = new jspdf.jsPDF({ orientation: 'p', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });
  const pageW = 210;
  const pageH = 297;
  const margin = 8;
  const imgW = pageW - margin * 2;
  const pxPerMm = canvas.width / imgW;
  const pageHpx = Math.floor((pageH - margin * 2) * pxPerMm);
  let y = 0;
  let page = 0;
  while (y < canvas.height) {
    const sliceH = Math.min(pageHpx, canvas.height - y);
    const part = document.createElement('canvas');
    part.width = canvas.width;
    part.height = sliceH;
    part.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas, 0, y, canvas.width, sliceH, 0, 0, canvas.width, sliceH);
    if (page > 0) pdf.addPage();
    pdf.addImage(part.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.92), 'JPEG', margin, margin, imgW, sliceH / pxPerMm);
    y += sliceH;
    page++;
  }
  pdf.setProperties({ title: filename, creator: 'LUNAR AI' });
  await download(filename, pdf.output('blob'), 'application/pdf');
  return 'pdf';
}

function csvCell(v) {
  const s = String(v ?? '');
  return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
}

export function exportCSV() {
  const { mission: m, analysis: a, comparison: c, settings } = getState();
  const rows = [];
  const push = (...cells) => rows.push(cells.map(csvCell).join(','));
  const r3 = (v) => (Number.isFinite(v) ? Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000 : v);
  push('LUNAR AI', t('report.docTitle'));
  push(t('report.generated'), new Date().toISOString());
  push(t('csv.ephemerisSource'), a.sources.ephemeris);
  push(t('csv.terrainSource'), a.sources.terrain);
  push(t('csv.demoMode'), a.sources.demoMode ? 'YES' : 'NO');
  push();
  push(`# ${t('report.s.params')}`);
  push(t('csv.field'), t('csv.value'));
  push(t('planner.name'), m.name);
  push(t('planner.objective'), m.objective === 'custom' ? m.objectiveCustom : m.objective);
  push(t('planner.region'), m.region);
  push(t('planner.lat'), a.site.lat);
  push(t('planner.lon'), a.site.lon);
  push(t('planner.date'), m.date);
  push(t('planner.time'), `${m.time} UTC`);
  push(t('planner.duration'), m.duration);
  push(t('planner.vehicle'), m.vehicleType);
  push(t('settings.panel'), `${settings.panelType};eff=${settings.panelEfficiency};area_m2=${settings.panelAreaM2}`);
  push();
  push(`# ${t('csv.metrics')}`);
  push(t('csv.field'), t('csv.value'));
  Object.entries(a.metrics).forEach(([k, v]) => push(k, typeof v === 'boolean' ? (v ? 'true' : 'false') : r3(v)));
  Object.entries(a.terrain).filter(([k]) => k !== 'horizonProfile').forEach(([k, v]) => push(`terrain.${k}`, r3(v)));
  push();
  push(`# ${t('csv.scores')}`);
  push(t('risk.category'), t('metric.score'), t('risk.levelH'));
  RISK_KEYS.forEach((k) => push(k, r3(a.scores[k]), a.risks[k].level));
  push('overall', r3(a.scores.overall), '');
  push('overallRisk', r3(a.overallRisk), '');
  if (c?.result) {
    push();
    push(`# ${t('report.s.compare')}`);
    push(t('compare.site'), 'lat', 'lon', 'date', 'time', ...COMPARE_METRICS.map((x) => x.key), t('compare.recommendedSite'));
    c.sites.forEach((s, i) => push(s.key, s.lat, s.lon, s.date, s.time, ...COMPARE_METRICS.map((x) => r3(x.path(c.analyses[i]))), i === c.result.recommendedIndex ? 'YES' : ''));
  }
  push();
  push(`# ${t('csv.timeseries')}`);
  push('time_utc', 'sun_el_deg', 'sun_az_deg', 'sun_disk_visible_frac', 'terrain_mask_sun_deg', 'earth_el_deg', 'earth_az_deg', 'earth_visible', 'dte_available', 'power_w', 'in_mission_window');
  a.points.slice(0, a.grid.endIdx + 1).forEach((p, i) =>
    push(p.t, r3(p.sunEl), r3(p.sunAz), r3(p.sunFrac), r3(p.sunMask), r3(p.earthEl), r3(p.earthAz), p.earthVisible ? 1 : 0, p.dte ? 1 : 0, r3(p.powerW), i >= a.grid.startIdx ? 1 : 0),
  );
  const name = `lunar-ai_${(m.name || 'mission').replace(/[^\w؀-ۿ-]+/g, '_')}_${m.date}.csv`;
  download(name, '﻿' + rows.join('\n'), 'text/csv;charset=utf-8');
}


######################################################################
# FILE 31/38: public/src/services/scienceData.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Scientific Data Layer.
 *
 * Provides Sun / Earth positions for a lunar surface site from one of two sources:
 *   LIVE       — JPL Horizons via our backend proxy (/api/horizons)
 *   ESTIMATED  — local analytic ephemeris (ephemeris.js, Meeus)
 *
 * The source is always returned alongside the data so the UI can label it.
 * Failed live requests are NEVER silently replaced: the caller receives
 * { source: 'ESTIMATED', liveError } and the UI shows that live data was unavailable.
 */
import { subSolarPoint, subEarthPoint, topocentric } from './ephemeris.js';

const clientCache = new Map();
let backendAvailable = null;

export async function checkBackend() {
  if (backendAvailable !== null) return backendAvailable;
  try {
    const r = await fetch('/api/health', { cache: 'no-store' });
    const j = await r.json();
    backendAvailable = Boolean(j.ok);
    window.__lunarHealth = j;
  } catch {
    backendAvailable = false;
  }
  return backendAvailable;
}

export function getSunPosition(site, date) {
  const p = subSolarPoint(date);
  const t = topocentric(site, p);
  return { az: t.az, el: t.el, distKm: p.distKm, distAU: p.distAU, subLat: p.lat, subLon: p.lon };
}

export function getEarthPosition(site, date) {
  const p = subEarthPoint(date);
  const t = topocentric(site, p);
  return { az: t.az, el: t.el, distKm: t.rangeKm, subLat: p.lat, subLon: p.lon };
}

/** Fetch a Horizons az/el series through the backend proxy (cached client-side). */
export async function fetchHorizonsSeries({ target, lat, lon, start, hours, step = '1h' }) {
  const key = [target, lat.toFixed(4), lon.toFixed(4), start, hours, step].join('|');
  if (clientCache.has(key)) return clientCache.get(key);
  const qs = new URLSearchParams({ target, lat, lon, start, hours, step });
  const p = (async () => {
    const ctrl = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 25_000);
    try {
      const r = await fetch(`/api/horizons?${qs}`, { signal: ctrl.signal });
      const j = await r.json();
      if (!j.ok) throw new Error(j.message || j.error || `HTTP ${r.status}`);
      return j;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  })();
  clientCache.set(key, p);
  try {
    return await p;
  } catch (e) {
    clientCache.delete(key); // allow retry later
    throw e;
  }
}

/**
 * Get Sun & Earth series for a site/time grid.
 * Returns { sun:[{az,el}], earth:[{az,el}], source, liveError?, meta }
 */
export async function getEphemerisSeries(site, times, { allowLive = true, stepHours = 1 } = {}) {
  const estimate = () => ({
    sun: times.map((t) => getSunPosition(site, t)),
    earth: times.map((t) => getEarthPosition(site, t)),
  });

  if (!allowLive) return { ...estimate(), source: 'ESTIMATED', reason: 'DEMO_MODE' };
  if (!(await checkBackend())) return { ...estimate(), source: 'ESTIMATED', reason: 'NO_BACKEND' };

  const start = times[0].toISOString();
  const hours = Math.round((times[times.length - 1] - times[0]) / 3600000);
  const step = `${stepHours}h`;
  try {
    const [sunJ, earthJ] = await Promise.all([
      fetchHorizonsSeries({ target: 'sun', lat: site.lat, lon: site.lon, start, hours, step }),
      fetchHorizonsSeries({ target: 'earth', lat: site.lat, lon: site.lon, start, hours, step }),
    ]);
    const est = estimate();
    const byTime = (rows) => new Map(rows.map((r) => [Date.parse(r.t), r]));
    const sMap = byTime(sunJ.rows);
    const eMap = byTime(earthJ.rows);
    let matched = 0;
    const sun = times.map((t, i) => {
      const r = sMap.get(t.getTime());
      if (r) matched++;
      return r ? { ...est.sun[i], az: r.az, el: r.el } : null;
    });
    const earth = times.map((t, i) => {
      const r = eMap.get(t.getTime());
      return r ? { ...est.earth[i], az: r.az, el: r.el } : null;
    });
    if (matched < times.length * 0.9 || sun.includes(null) || earth.includes(null)) {
      throw new Error('Horizons series did not cover the requested time grid');
    }
    // keep the estimate alongside for model-vs-live validation
    const diff = sun.reduce((m, s, i) => Math.max(m, Math.abs(s.el - est.sun[i].el)), 0);
    return {
      sun,
      earth,
      source: 'LIVE',
      meta: { provider: 'JPL Horizons', retrievedAt: sunJ.retrievedAt, cached: sunJ.cached, maxModelDiffDeg: diff },
    };
  } catch (e) {
    return { ...estimate(), source: 'ESTIMATED', reason: 'LIVE_FAILED', liveError: e.message };
  }
}


######################################################################
# FILE 32/38: public/src/services/terrain.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Terrain layer.
 *
 * Lunar AI does NOT ship a digital elevation model (DEM). Two sources are supported:
 *
 *  1. IMPORTED — values the planner reads from NASA Moon Trek / QuickMap (LOLA-derived
 *     elevation, slope and horizon) and enters in the Mission Planner. These are used as-is.
 *
 *  2. DEMO — when nothing is imported, a deterministic procedural model generates
 *     plausible-looking placeholder values seeded by the coordinates. They are NOT
 *     measurements and are always labelled "DEMO / ESTIMATED" in the UI.
 *
 * To make this LIVE, replace demoTerrain() with sampling of a LOLA polar DEM
 * (e.g. LDEM_80S_20M from the PDS Geosciences Node) on the server.
 */

function hash(lat, lon, salt) {
  const s = Math.sin(lat * 12.9898 + lon * 78.233 + salt * 37.719) * 43758.5453;
  return s - Math.floor(s);
}

export function demoTerrain(lat, lon) {
  const polar = Math.abs(lat) > 80;
  const elevationM = Math.round((hash(lat, lon, 1) - 0.35) * (polar ? 6500 : 4000));
  const slopeDeg = +(2 + hash(lat, lon, 2) * 16).toFixed(1);
  const roughness = +(0.15 + hash(lat, lon, 3) * 0.6).toFixed(2); // 0..1 terrain complexity index
  const horizonBase = +(0.2 + hash(lat, lon, 4) * (polar ? 1.3 : 0.8)).toFixed(2);
  // horizon mask varies with azimuth (sum of two harmonics) — placeholder shape only
  const p1 = hash(lat, lon, 5) * 360;
  const p2 = hash(lat, lon, 6) * 360;
  const a1 = hash(lat, lon, 7) * 0.8;
  const a2 = hash(lat, lon, 8) * 0.4;
  const horizonAt = (az) =>
    Math.max(0, horizonBase + a1 * Math.sin(((az - p1) * Math.PI) / 180) + a2 * Math.sin(((2 * az - p2) * Math.PI) / 180));
  return { elevationM, slopeDeg, roughness, horizonBase, horizonAt, source: 'DEMO' };
}

/** Merge imported values (if any) over the demo model. */
export function resolveTerrain(mission) {
  const demo = demoTerrain(mission.latitude, mission.longitude);
  const imp = mission.terrain || {};
  const has = (v) => v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== '' && Number.isFinite(Number(v));
  const out = { ...demo, fieldSources: { elevation: 'DEMO', slope: 'DEMO', horizon: 'DEMO', roughness: 'DEMO' } };
  if (has(imp.elevationM)) {
    out.elevationM = Number(imp.elevationM);
    out.fieldSources.elevation = 'IMPORTED';
  }
  if (has(imp.slopeDeg)) {
    out.slopeDeg = Number(imp.slopeDeg);
    out.fieldSources.slope = 'IMPORTED';
    // terrain-complexity index derived from the imported slope (simple proxy)
    out.roughness = +Math.min(1, 0.1 + out.slopeDeg / 25).toFixed(2);
    out.fieldSources.roughness = 'IMPORTED';
  }
  if (has(imp.horizonDeg)) {
    const h = Number(imp.horizonDeg);
    out.horizonBase = h;
    out.horizonAt = () => h; // uniform imported horizon elevation
    out.fieldSources.horizon = 'IMPORTED';
  }
  const all = Object.values(out.fieldSources);
  out.source = all.every((s) => s === 'IMPORTED') ? 'IMPORTED' : all.some((s) => s === 'IMPORTED') ? 'MIXED' : 'DEMO';
  return out;
}
