# FILE 33/38: public/src/state/store.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Central state (single source of truth).
 *
 * Every screen reads the SAME `mission`, `analysis` and `comparison` objects from here.
 * Pages never create their own data — they subscribe and render.
 */

import { getItem, setItem } from '../utils/storage.js';

const LS_KEY = 'lunar-ai-state-v1';

export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
  lang: 'en',
  theme: 'dark',
  units: 'metric',
  notifications: true,
  demoMode: false,
  decimals: 2,
  panelType: 'vertical',
  panelEfficiency: 0.3,
  panelAreaM2: 10,
  minDteElevation: 1,
  weightsPreset: 'balanced',
  dashboardCards: {
    sunEl: true, sunAz: true, sunlight: true, earthEl: true, earthVis: true,
    comm: true, power: true, terrainRisk: true, overall: true,
  },
};

export function emptyMission() {
  const d = new Date();
  return {
    id: null,
    name: '',
    objective: 'science',
    objectiveCustom: '',
    region: 'south',
    siteId: 'shackleton',
    siteName: '',
    latitude: -89.67,
    longitude: 129.78,
    date: d.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    time: '00:00',
    duration: 7,
    vehicleType: '',
    terrain: { elevationM: '', slopeDeg: '', horizonDeg: '' },
    status: 'draft',
  };
}

const initial = {
  user: null,
  token: null,
  backend: null, // 'remote' | 'local'
  settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS },
  mission: emptyMission(),
  analysis: null,
  comparison: { sites: null, analyses: [], result: null, computedAt: null },
  history: [],
  snapshot: null, // 3D snapshot dataURL for the report
  route: 'auth',
};

let state = load();
const listeners = new Set();

function load() {
  try {
    const raw = getItem(LS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return structuredClone(initial);
    const saved = JSON.parse(raw);
    return {
      ...structuredClone(initial),
      ...saved,
      settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...(saved.settings || {}), dashboardCards: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS.dashboardCards, ...(saved.settings?.dashboardCards || {}) } },
      route: 'auth',
    };
  } catch {
    return structuredClone(initial);
  }
}

let saveTimer;
function persist() {
  clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  saveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    const { user, token, backend, settings, mission, analysis, comparison, snapshot, remember } = state;
    const keepSession = remember !== false;
    try {
      setItem(
        LS_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({ user: keepSession ? user : null, token: keepSession ? token : null, backend, settings, mission, analysis, comparison, snapshot, remember }),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('State not persisted:', e.message);
    }
  }, 120);
}

export function getState() {
  return state;
}

export function setState(patch, { silent = false } = {}) {
  const prev = state;
  state = { ...state, ...(typeof patch === 'function' ? patch(state) : patch) };
  persist();
  if (!silent) listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(state, prev));
}

export function updateSettings(patch) {
  setState((s) => ({ settings: { ...s.settings, ...patch } }));
}

export function subscribe(fn) {
  listeners.add(fn);
  return () => listeners.delete(fn);
}

export function resetSession() {
  setState({ user: null, token: null, history: [] });
}


######################################################################
# FILE 34/38: public/src/three/moonArt.js
######################################################################

/**
 * 2D Moon artwork (no network, no WebGL needed): a procedurally cratered lunar
 * texture, lit spheres for thumbnails/hero, a horizon landscape strip and a surface tile.
 * Everything is generated once and cached as data URLs — one consistent Moon visual language.
 * These are illustrations, not data.
 */

let texCache = null;
const mapCache = new Map();
const urlCache = new Map();

function rng(seed) {
  let s = seed;
  return () => ((s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647);
}

/* ---------- smooth value noise (periodic in x) ---------- */
function makeNoise(seed) {
  const P = 256;
  const r = rng(seed);
  const v = new Float32Array(P * P).map(() => r());
  const fade = (t) => t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
  return (x, y) => {
    const xi = Math.floor(x);
    const yi = Math.floor(y);
    const xf = fade(x - xi);
    const yf = fade(y - yi);
    const a = (((xi % P) + P) % P);
    const b = (a + 1) % P;
    const c = (((yi % P) + P) % P);
    const d = (c + 1) % P;
    const top = v[c * P + a] + (v[c * P + b] - v[c * P + a]) * xf;
    const bot = v[d * P + a] + (v[d * P + b] - v[d * P + a]) * xf;
    return top + (bot - top) * yf;
  };
}

/*
 * Approximate near-side maria (selenographic lat/lon in degrees, semi-axes in degrees).
 * Positions follow the real Moon so the familiar "face" is recognisable. Artwork, not data.
 */
const MARIA = [
  [34, -17, 21, 16], [24, -55, 22, 26], [8, -50, 18, 22], [-6, -42, 14, 14],   // Imbrium, Procellarum (N, central, S)
  [2, -30, 11, 9], [12, -10, 8, 6],                                            // Insularum, Sinus Medii/Aestuum
  [28, 17, 12, 11], [8, 30, 15, 12], [1, 40, 8, 7], [17, 59, 9, 7],            // Serenitatis, Tranquillitatis (+ east lobe), Crisium
  [-7, 51, 10, 13], [-15, 35, 6, 6], [-20, -15, 13, 11], [-12, -24, 7, 6],     // Fecunditatis, Nectaris, Nubium, Cognitum
  [-24, -39, 7, 6], [57, -5, 38, 6], [50, 25, 12, 4], [14, 4, 6, 5],           // Humorum, Frigoris, Frigoris E, Vaporum
  [-31, -63, 5, 5], [-20, -93, 6, 9], [2, 84, 6, 6],                           // Schickard area, Orientale, Smythii
];
/* Bright young ray craters: lat, lon, radius(deg), ray length(deg) */
const RAY_CRATERS = [[-43.3, -11.2, 1.4, 34], [9.6, -20.1, 1.6, 24], [8.1, -38, 0.9, 16], [23.7, -47.4, 0.7, 10], [16.2, 46.8, 0.5, 9], [-8.9, 61, 0.6, 9]];

/** Albedo + height maps (equirectangular, lon 0 at the centre). */
function buildMaps(W = 1024, H = 512) {
  if (mapCache.has(W)) return mapCache.get(W);
  const n1 = makeNoise(3);
  const n2 = makeNoise(9);
  const alb = new Float32Array(W * H);
  const hgt = new Float32Array(W * H);
  const mare = new Float32Array(W * H);
  const lonOf = (x) => (x / W) * 360 - 180;
  const latOf = (y) => 90 - (y / H) * 180;
  const px = (lon) => ((((lon + 180) / 360) * W) % W + W) % W;
  const py = (lat) => ((90 - lat) / 180) * H;

  // highlands: fBm albedo variation
  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
      const u = (x / W) * 32;
      const v = (y / H) * 16;
      let f = 0;
      let amp = 0.5;
      let fr = 1;
      for (let o = 0; o < 5; o++) {
        f += amp * n1(u * fr, v * fr);
        amp *= 0.5;
        fr *= 2.03;
      }
      alb[y * W + x] = 0.7 + (f - 0.5) * 0.22 + (n2(u * 9, v * 9) - 0.5) * 0.06;
      hgt[y * W + x] = (f - 0.5) * 0.6;
    }
  }
  // maria: dark, smooth basins with noisy shorelines
  for (const [la, lo, a, b] of MARIA) {
    const cosl = Math.max(0.2, Math.cos((la * Math.PI) / 180));
    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(py(la + b * 1.9)));
    const y1 = Math.min(H - 1, Math.ceil(py(la - b * 1.9)));
    const span = Math.ceil(((a * 1.9) / cosl / 360) * W);
    const cx = Math.round(px(lo));
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
      for (let k = -span; k <= span; k++) {
        const x = (cx + k + W) % W;
        let dlon = lonOf(x) - lo;
        dlon = ((dlon + 540) % 360) - 180;
        const dx = (dlon * cosl) / a;
        const dy = (latOf(y) - la) / b;
        const d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) + (n2((x / W) * 26, (y / H) * 13) - 0.5) * 0.6 + (n1((x / W) * 90, (y / H) * 45) - 0.5) * 0.12;
        const m = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (1.05 - d) / 0.55)) * (0.75 + 0.25 * n1((x / W) * 140, (y / H) * 70));
        const i = y * W + x;
        if (m > mare[i]) mare[i] = m;
      }
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    const m = mare[i];
    if (m <= 0) continue;
    const x = i % W;
    const y = (i / W) | 0;
    const tone = 0.47 + (n1((x / W) * 60, (y / H) * 30) - 0.5) * 0.07 + (n2((x / W) * 200, (y / H) * 100) - 0.5) * 0.04;
    alb[i] = alb[i] * (1 - m) + tone * m;
    hgt[i] = hgt[i] * (1 - m * 0.85) - m * 0.15;
  }
  // craters: bowl + raised rim in the height map, brighter fresh rims in albedo
  const r = rng(21);
  const crater = (la, lo, rad, fresh) => {
    const cosl = Math.max(0.12, Math.cos((la * Math.PI) / 180));
    const rpy = (rad / 180) * H;
    const rpx = rpy / cosl;
    const cx = px(lo);
    const cy = py(la);
    const ext = 1.6;
    for (let y = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - rpy * ext)); y <= Math.min(H - 1, Math.ceil(cy + rpy * ext)); y++) {
      for (let x0 = Math.floor(cx - rpx * ext); x0 <= Math.ceil(cx + rpx * ext); x0++) {
        const x = ((x0 % W) + W) % W;
        const dx = (x0 - cx) / rpx;
        const dy = (y - cy) / rpy;
        const d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (d > ext) continue;
        const i = y * W + x;
        let h;
        if (d < 1) h = -(1 - d * d) * 0.9 + 0.25; // bowl
        else h = 0.25 * Math.exp(-((d - 1) * (d - 1)) / 0.06); // rim + ejecta
        hgt[i] += h * (0.12 + rad * 0.1);
        if (fresh) alb[i] += 0.06 * Math.max(0, 1.3 - d);
        else alb[i] += d < 0.8 ? -0.022 * (1 - d * 0.6) : d < 1.15 ? 0.018 : 0;
      }
    }
  };
  for (let k = 0; k < 4200; k++) {
    const la = Math.asin(r() * 2 - 1) * (180 / Math.PI);
    const lo = r() * 360 - 180;
    const rad = 0.22 + 3.6 * Math.pow(r(), 6);
    const i = (Math.round(py(la)) % H) * W + Math.round(px(lo)) % W;
    if (mare[i] > 0.5 && r() < 0.6) continue; // maria are younger, fewer craters
    crater(la, lo, rad, r() < 0.12);
  }
  const ray = new Float32Array(W * H);
  for (const [la, lo, rad, len] of RAY_CRATERS) {
    crater(la, lo, rad, true);
    // ray system: bright streaks radiating from the crater
    const rays = 40;
    for (let q = 0; q < rays; q++) {
      const ang = r() * Math.PI * 2;
      const L = len * (0.4 + r() * 0.8);
      for (let s = rad * 1.6; s < L; s += 0.12) {
        const lat2 = la + Math.sin(ang) * s;
        const lon2 = lo + (Math.cos(ang) * s) / Math.max(0.2, Math.cos((la * Math.PI) / 180));
        if (Math.abs(lat2) > 88) break;
        const xi = Math.round(px(lon2)) % W;
        const yi = Math.round(py(lat2));
        if (yi < 0 || yi >= H) break;
        const fall = 0.018 * (1 - s / L);
        for (let ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) for (let oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {
          const yy = yi + oy;
          if (yy < 0 || yy >= H) continue;
          ray[yy * W + ((xi + ox + W) % W)] += fall * (ox === 0 && oy === 0 ? 1 : 0.45);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  // fine regolith speckle (tiny fresh/old craters below texture resolution)
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) alb[i] += Math.min(ray[i], 0.045) + (r() - 0.5) * 0.035;
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) alb[i] = Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1, alb[i]));
  const out = { W, H, alb, hgt };
  mapCache.set(W, out);
  return out;
}

/** Equirectangular Moon albedo texture (used by the 3D globe when NASA textures are unavailable). */
const texCacheBy = new Map();
export function moonTexture(w = 1024, h = 512) {
  if (texCacheBy.has(w)) return texCacheBy.get(w);
  const { W, H, alb } = buildMaps(w, h);
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = W;
  c.height = H;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  const img = ctx.createImageData(W, H);
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    const v = alb[i] * 255;
    img.data[i * 4] = v * 1.0;
    img.data[i * 4 + 1] = v * 0.985;
    img.data[i * 4 + 2] = v * 0.96;
    img.data[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  texCacheBy.set(w, c);
  return c;
}

/** Height map canvas (bump map for the 3D globe fallback). */
export function moonHeightTexture(w = 1024, h = 512) {
  const { W, H, hgt } = buildMaps(w, h);
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = W;
  c.height = H;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  const img = ctx.createImageData(W, H);
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, 128 + hgt[i] * 70));
    img.data.set([v, v, v, 255], i * 4);
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  return c;
}

/**
 * Photo-like lit Moon (near side facing the viewer) as a transparent PNG data URL.
 * Shading: Lommel–Seeliger (how the real regolith reflects light) blended with Lambert,
 * per-pixel normals from the crater height map, so craters cast relief near the terminator.
 */
export function moonImage(size = 256, o = {}) {
  const key = `m${size}|${o.light || ''}`;
  if (urlCache.has(key)) return urlCache.get(key);
  const { W, H, alb, hgt } = buildMaps();
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = c.height = size;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  const img = ctx.createImageData(size, size);
  const R = size / 2 - 1;
  const L0 = o.light || [0.5, 0.28, 0.82]; // near-full Moon, like the hero view
  const ll = Math.hypot(...L0);
  const L = L0.map((v) => v / ll);
  const tint = o.tint || [1.0, 1.0, 1.02];
  const sample = (arr, u, v) => {
    const x = u * W - 0.5;
    const y = Math.min(H - 1.001, Math.max(0, v * H - 0.5));
    const x0 = Math.floor(x);
    const y0 = Math.floor(y);
    const fx = x - x0;
    const fy = y - y0;
    const xa = ((x0 % W) + W) % W;
    const xb = (xa + 1) % W;
    const yb = Math.min(H - 1, y0 + 1);
    const a = arr[y0 * W + xa] + (arr[y0 * W + xb] - arr[y0 * W + xa]) * fx;
    const b = arr[yb * W + xa] + (arr[yb * W + xb] - arr[yb * W + xa]) * fx;
    return a + (b - a) * fy;
  };
  const bump = 0.55 * (size / 512);
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
      const nx = (x - size / 2) / R;
      const ny = -(y - size / 2) / R;
      const r2 = nx * nx + ny * ny;
      if (r2 > 1) continue;
      const nz = Math.sqrt(1 - r2);
      const lat = Math.asin(ny);
      const lon = Math.atan2(nx, nz);
      const u = lon / (2 * Math.PI) + 0.5;
      const v = 0.5 - lat / Math.PI;
      const a = sample(alb, u, v);
      // height gradient → tangent-space normal perturbation
      const du = 1 / W;
      const dv = 1 / H;
      const hx = (sample(hgt, u + du, v) - sample(hgt, u - du, v)) * bump;
      const hy = (sample(hgt, u, v - dv) - sample(hgt, u, v + dv)) * bump;
      // tangent frame on the sphere
      const ex = [Math.cos(lon), 0, -Math.sin(lon)];
      const ey = [-Math.sin(lat) * Math.sin(lon), Math.cos(lat), -Math.sin(lat) * Math.cos(lon)];
      let N = [nx - hx * ex[0] - hy * ey[0], ny - hx * ex[1] - hy * ey[1], nz - hx * ex[2] - hy * ey[2]];
      const nl = Math.hypot(...N);
      N = N.map((q) => q / nl);
      const mu0 = Math.max(0, N[0] * L[0] + N[1] * L[1] + N[2] * L[2]);
      const mu = Math.max(0.05, N[2]);
      const ls = mu0 / (mu0 + mu); // Lommel–Seeliger
      const shade = (0.8 * ls * 2 + 0.2 * mu0) * 1.18;
      const earthshine = 0.025;
      const val = a * (shade + earthshine);
      const i = (y * size + x) * 4;
      img.data[i] = Math.min(255, 255 * val * tint[0]);
      img.data[i + 1] = Math.min(255, 255 * val * tint[1]);
      img.data[i + 2] = Math.min(255, 255 * val * tint[2]);
      img.data[i + 3] = r2 > 0.988 ? Math.round(255 * (1 - r2) / 0.012) : 255;
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  const url = c.toDataURL('image/png');
  urlCache.set(key, url);
  return url;
}

/** Foreground lunar terrain strip (transparent above the ridge line). */
export function landscapeImage(w = 1200, h = 220) {
  const key = `l${w}x${h}`;
  if (urlCache.has(key)) return urlCache.get(key);
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = w;
  c.height = h;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  const rnd = rng(5);
  const ridge = [];
  for (let x = 0; x <= w; x += 6) {
    const f = x / w;
    const yv = h * 0.42 + Math.sin(f * 5.2 + 1) * h * 0.08 + Math.sin(f * 13 + 2) * h * 0.035 + (rnd() - 0.5) * h * 0.02 - Math.max(0, Math.sin((f - 0.55) * 3.2)) * h * 0.18;
    ridge.push([x, yv]);
  }
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(0, h);
  ridge.forEach(([x, y]) => ctx.lineTo(x, y));
  ctx.lineTo(w, h);
  ctx.closePath();
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, h * 0.25, 0, h);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#7c879c');
  g.addColorStop(0.35, '#3f4a63');
  g.addColorStop(1, '#141c33');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.save();
  ctx.clip();
  // craters in perspective (flattened ellipses), smaller toward the horizon
  for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) {
    const y = h * 0.4 + Math.pow(rnd(), 0.7) * h * 0.6;
    const depth = (y - h * 0.35) / (h * 0.65);
    const rx = (2 + rnd() * 26) * depth * (w / 1200) * 1.4;
    const x = rnd() * w;
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(10,14,30,${0.28 * depth})`;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x, y, rx, rx * 0.28, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(200,212,235,${0.18 * depth})`;
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, rx * 0.08);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x, y, rx, rx * 0.28, 0, Math.PI * 0.95, Math.PI * 2.05);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
  // rim light along the ridge
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(190,210,255,0.55)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.4;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ridge.forEach(([x, y], i) => (i ? ctx.lineTo(x, y) : ctx.moveTo(x, y)));
  ctx.stroke();
  const url = c.toDataURL('image/png');
  urlCache.set(key, url);
  return url;
}

/** Square surface tile (illustrative, generated at tile scale so it stays sharp). */
export function surfaceTile(size = 320, lon = 0) {
  const key = `t${size}|${Math.round(lon)}`;
  if (urlCache.has(key)) return urlCache.get(key);
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = c.height = size;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  const rnd = rng(1000 + Math.round(((lon % 360) + 360) % 360));
  ctx.fillStyle = '#8f9196';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
  for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) {
    ctx.fillStyle = rnd() < 0.5 ? 'rgba(50,52,58,0.07)' : 'rgba(235,236,240,0.07)';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(rnd() * size, rnd() * size, 2 + rnd() * 18, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 160; i++) {
    const r = Math.max(1.5, size * 0.11 * Math.pow(rnd(), 3.2));
    const x = rnd() * size;
    const y = rnd() * size;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x, y);
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(-r * 0.25, -r * 0.25, 0, 0, 0, r);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(30,32,38,0.55)');
    g.addColorStop(0.75, 'rgba(60,62,68,0.25)');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(60,62,68,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(245,246,250,0.2)';
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.8, r * 0.12);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 0.92, Math.PI * 0.1, Math.PI * 0.9);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // faint coordinate grid
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,170,255,0.18)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let k = 1; k < 4; k++) {
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo((k * size) / 4, 0); ctx.lineTo((k * size) / 4, size); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, (k * size) / 4); ctx.lineTo(size, (k * size) / 4); ctx.stroke();
  }
  const g2 = ctx.createRadialGradient(size / 2, size / 2, size * 0.2, size / 2, size / 2, size * 0.75);
  g2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(10,25,70,0)');
  g2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(10,25,70,0.45)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g2;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
  const url = c.toDataURL('image/png');
  urlCache.set(key, url);
  return url;
}


######################################################################
# FILE 35/38: public/src/three/moonScene.js
######################################################################

/**
 * 3D Moon (Three.js / WebGL).
 *
 * Textures (in order of preference):
 *   1. NASA SVS "CGI Moon Kit" LRO LROC colour mosaic + LOLA elevation — via our same-origin
 *      proxy /api/texture/* (https://svs.gsfc.nasa.gov/4720)
 *   2. three.js sample moon texture (CDN)
 *   3. procedural cratered texture generated on a canvas (offline fallback, labelled as such)
 *
 * Frame: selenographic (x→lon0, y→lon90E, z→north)  mapped to three.js as (x, z, −y),
 * which matches SphereGeometry's equirectangular UVs (lon 0 at the texture centre).
 */

import { moonTexture, moonHeightTexture } from './moonArt.js';

let THREE = null;
let OrbitControls = null;

export async function loadThree() {
  if (THREE) return true;
  try {
    THREE = await import('three');
    ({ OrbitControls } = await import('three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js'));
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('Three.js unavailable:', e.message);
    return false;
  }
}

const toThree = ([x, y, z]) => new THREE.Vector3(x, z, -y);
export function latLonToVec(lat, lon, r = 1) {
  const la = (lat * Math.PI) / 180;
  const lo = (lon * Math.PI) / 180;
  return toThree([Math.cos(la) * Math.cos(lo) * r, Math.cos(la) * Math.sin(lo) * r, Math.sin(la) * r]);
}

function loadImage(src) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image();
    img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    img.onload = () => resolve(img);
    img.onerror = () => reject(new Error(`image failed: ${src}`));
    img.src = src;
  });
}

async function firstImage(candidates) {
  for (const c of candidates) {
    try {
      const img = await loadImage(c.src);
      return { img, label: c.label, kind: c.kind };
    } catch {
      /* try next */
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/* ---------- procedural fallback texture ---------- */
function proceduralMoonCanvas(w = 2048, h = 1024) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = w;
  c.height = h;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  let seed = 7;
  const rnd = () => ((seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#8d8a85';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  // large-scale albedo variation (maria-like regions)
  for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
    const x = rnd() * w;
    const y = h * 0.25 + rnd() * h * 0.5;
    const r = 60 + rnd() * 220;
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, r);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(60,58,56,0.55)');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(60,58,56,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x, y, r * 1.4, r, rnd() * 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  // fine grain noise
  const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let i = 0; i < img.data.length; i += 4) {
    const n = (rnd() - 0.5) * 22;
    img.data[i] += n;
    img.data[i + 1] += n;
    img.data[i + 2] += n;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  // craters (power-law size distribution)
  for (let i = 0; i < 1400; i++) {
    const r = Math.max(1.2, 46 * (w / 2048) * Math.pow(rnd(), 4));
    const x = rnd() * w;
    const y = rnd() * h;
    const sx = 1 / Math.max(0.25, Math.sin((y / h) * Math.PI)); // stretch toward poles (equirectangular)
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(x, y);
    ctx.scale(Math.min(sx, 4), 1);
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(-r * 0.15, -r * 0.15, r * 0.1, 0, 0, r);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(40,38,36,0.32)');
    g.addColorStop(0.75, 'rgba(70,68,66,0.14)');
    g.addColorStop(0.92, 'rgba(210,206,198,0.22)');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(210,206,198,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  return c;
}

/* ---------- colour maps for elevation / slope layers ---------- */
function ramp(t) {
  // perceptual blue → teal → yellow → red ramp
  const stops = [
    [0, [33, 46, 120]],
    [0.25, [32, 120, 180]],
    [0.5, [60, 180, 140]],
    [0.75, [235, 205, 80]],
    [1, [200, 60, 40]],
  ];
  for (let i = 1; i < stops.length; i++) {
    if (t <= stops[i][0]) {
      const [t0, c0] = stops[i - 1];
      const [t1, c1] = stops[i];
      const f = (t - t0) / (t1 - t0);
      return c0.map((v, k) => Math.round(v + (c1[k] - v) * f));
    }
  }
  return stops[stops.length - 1][1];
}

function layerCanvases(elevImg) {
  const w = 1024;
  const h = 512;
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = w;
  c.height = h;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.drawImage(elevImg, 0, 0, w, h);
  const src = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data;
  const H = new Float32Array(w * h);
  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) H[i] = src[i * 4] / 255;

  const elevC = document.createElement('canvas');
  elevC.width = w;
  elevC.height = h;
  const eCtx = elevC.getContext('2d');
  const eImg = eCtx.createImageData(w, h);
  const slopeC = document.createElement('canvas');
  slopeC.width = w;
  slopeC.height = h;
  const sCtx = slopeC.getContext('2d');
  const sImg = sCtx.createImageData(w, h);
  let maxG = 0;
  const G = new Float32Array(w * h);
  for (let y = 1; y < h - 1; y++) {
    const coslat = Math.max(0.05, Math.cos(((y / h) - 0.5) * Math.PI));
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      const xl = (x - 1 + w) % w;
      const xr = (x + 1) % w;
      const dx = (H[y * w + xr] - H[y * w + xl]) / coslat;
      const dy = H[(y + 1) * w + x] - H[(y - 1) * w + x];
      const g = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      G[y * w + x] = g;
      if (g > maxG) maxG = g;
    }
  }
  const gCap = maxG * 0.35 || 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {
    const [r, g, b] = ramp(H[i]);
    eImg.data.set([r, g, b, 255], i * 4);
    const [r2, g2, b2] = ramp(Math.min(1, G[i] / gCap));
    sImg.data.set([r2, g2, b2, 255], i * 4);
  }
  eCtx.putImageData(eImg, 0, 0);
  sCtx.putImageData(sImg, 0, 0);
  return { elevC, slopeC };
}

function textSprite(text, color) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = 128;
  c.height = 64;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,8,16,0.75)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(4, 4, 120, 56, 12);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.font = 'bold 30px Archivo, sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(text, 64, 34);
  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  const s = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: tex, depthTest: false, transparent: true }));
  s.scale.set(0.09, 0.045, 1);
  s.renderOrder = 10;
  return s;
}

function starField(count = 5000) {
  const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  const pos = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  const col = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const u = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    const th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const r = 300 + Math.random() * 100;
    const s = Math.sqrt(1 - u * u);
    pos.set([r * s * Math.cos(th), r * u, r * s * Math.sin(th)], i * 3);
    const b = 0.55 + Math.random() * 0.45;
    const tint = Math.random();
    col.set(tint < 0.1 ? [b, b * 0.85, b * 0.7] : tint < 0.2 ? [b * 0.8, b * 0.9, b] : [b, b, b], i * 3);
  }
  g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
  g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3));
  return new THREE.Points(g, new THREE.PointsMaterial({ size: 0.9, sizeAttenuation: false, vertexColors: true }));
}

/**
 * @param {HTMLElement} container
 * @param {{mode?: 'explorer'|'hero'|'snapshot', width?: number, height?: number}} opts
 */
export async function createMoonScene(container, opts = {}) {
  const mode = opts.mode || 'explorer';
  if (!(await loadThree())) return null;

  const width = opts.width || container.clientWidth || 800;
  const height = opts.height || container.clientHeight || 600;
  const hero = mode === 'hero';
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, preserveDrawingBuffer: !hero, alpha: hero });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
  renderer.setSize(width, height, false);
  renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  if (mode !== 'snapshot') container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
  renderer.domElement.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Interactive 3D Moon');
  renderer.domElement.setAttribute('role', 'img');

  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  if (hero) renderer.setClearColor(0x000000, 0); // transparent: the page's sky shows through
  else {
    scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x000000);
    scene.add(starField(5000));
  }

  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(hero ? 24 : 40, width / height, 0.01, 1000);
  const sunLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 3.2);
  const ambient = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x8899bb, 0.05); // ~earthshine
  scene.add(sunLight, ambient);

  // ---- textures ----
  const colorCandidates = [
    { src: '/api/texture/moon-color', label: 'NASA SVS CGI Moon Kit — LRO LROC', kind: 'IMPORTED' },
    { src: 'https://threejs.org/examples/textures/planets/moon_1024.jpg', label: 'three.js sample texture', kind: 'EXTERNAL' },
  ];
  const [colorRes, elevRes] = await Promise.all([
    firstImage(colorCandidates),
    firstImage([{ src: '/api/texture/moon-elevation', label: 'NASA SVS CGI Moon Kit — LRO LOLA', kind: 'IMPORTED' }]),
  ]);
  let colorTex;
  let textureLabel;
  if (colorRes) {
    colorTex = new THREE.Texture(colorRes.img);
    textureLabel = colorRes.label;
  } else {
    colorTex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(moonTexture(2048, 1024));
    textureLabel = 'procedural (offline fallback)';
  }
  colorTex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  colorTex.anisotropy = renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy();
  colorTex.needsUpdate = true;

  let bumpTex = null;
  let layers = null;
  if (elevRes) {
    bumpTex = new THREE.Texture(elevRes.img);
    bumpTex.needsUpdate = true;
    if (mode === 'explorer') {
      try {
        const { elevC, slopeC } = layerCanvases(elevRes.img);
        layers = { elevation: new THREE.CanvasTexture(elevC), slope: new THREE.CanvasTexture(slopeC) };
        Object.values(layers).forEach((tx) => (tx.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace));
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('layer maps unavailable', e);
      }
    }
  } else if (!colorRes) {
    bumpTex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(moonHeightTexture(2048, 1024));
  }

  const seg = hero ? [256, 128] : [320, 160];
  const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map: colorTex,
    roughness: 1,
    metalness: 0,
    bumpMap: bumpTex,
    bumpScale: 2.2,
    displacementMap: elevRes ? bumpTex : null,
    displacementScale: elevRes ? 0.012 : 0,
    displacementBias: elevRes ? -0.006 : 0,
  });
  const moon = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, seg[0], seg[1]), material);
  scene.add(moon);

  // ---- overlays (explorer/snapshot) ----
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  scene.add(group);
  const sunGroup = new THREE.Group();
  const earthGroup = new THREE.Group();
  const markerGroup = new THREE.Group();
  const trajGroup = new THREE.Group();
  group.add(sunGroup, earthGroup, markerGroup, trajGroup);

  // Sun visual: glowing sprite far away + direction line
  const glowC = document.createElement('canvas');
  glowC.width = glowC.height = 128;
  const gctx = glowC.getContext('2d');
  const gg = gctx.createRadialGradient(64, 64, 0, 64, 64, 64);
  gg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,250,230,1)');
  gg.addColorStop(0.2, 'rgba(255,220,150,0.9)');
  gg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,180,80,0)');
  gctx.fillStyle = gg;
  gctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
  const sunSprite = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: new THREE.CanvasTexture(glowC), transparent: true, depthWrite: false }));
  sunSprite.scale.set(6, 6, 1);
  sunGroup.add(sunSprite);

  const earthMesh = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.12, 48, 24),
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3b7bd6, emissive: 0x0b2244, roughness: 0.6 }),
  );
  earthGroup.add(earthMesh);

  let sunArrow = null;
  let earthArrow = null;
  let sunDir = new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0);
  let earthDir = new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0);
  let siteVec = null;
  let pulse = null;

  const defaultCam = () => {
    if (hero) {
      // near side (selenographic lon 0 = +X) faces the camera, north up — the Moon as seen from Earth
      camera.position.set(5.2, 0, 0);
      camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
      return;
    }
    const south = siteVec ? siteVec.y < 0 : true;
    camera.position.set(1.6, south ? -2.4 : 2.4, 2.0);
    camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
  };
  defaultCam();

  let controls = null;
  if (mode === 'explorer') {
    controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
    controls.enableDamping = true;
    controls.dampingFactor = 0.08;
    controls.minDistance = 1.08;
    controls.maxDistance = 12;
    controls.enablePan = true;
    controls.screenSpacePanning = true;
    controls.rotateSpeed = 0.6;
    controls.zoomSpeed = 0.8;
    controls.keys = { LEFT: 'ArrowLeft', UP: 'ArrowUp', RIGHT: 'ArrowRight', BOTTOM: 'ArrowDown' };
    controls.listenToKeyEvents(renderer.domElement);
    renderer.domElement.tabIndex = 0;
  }

  function placeArrows() {
    [sunArrow, earthArrow].forEach((a) => a && group.remove(a));
    sunArrow = earthArrow = null;
    if (!siteVec) return;
    const origin = siteVec.clone().multiplyScalar(1.004);
    sunArrow = new THREE.ArrowHelper(sunDir, origin, 0.42, 0xf2c14e, 0.05, 0.03);
    earthArrow = new THREE.ArrowHelper(earthDir, origin, 0.42, 0x6ea2ff, 0.05, 0.03);
    sunArrow.visible = sunGroup.visible;
    earthArrow.visible = earthGroup.visible;
    group.add(sunArrow, earthArrow);
  }

  const api = {
    textureLabel,
    textureKind: colorRes?.kind || 'PROCEDURAL',
    hasLayers: Boolean(layers),

    /** sunSeleno / earthSeleno: [lat, lon] sub-solar / sub-Earth points */
    setSunEarth(sunSub, earthSub) {
      sunDir = latLonToVec(sunSub[0], sunSub[1]).normalize();
      earthDir = latLonToVec(earthSub[0], earthSub[1]).normalize();
      sunLight.position.copy(sunDir.clone().multiplyScalar(10));
      sunSprite.position.copy(sunDir.clone().multiplyScalar(60));
      earthMesh.position.copy(earthDir.clone().multiplyScalar(7)); // not to scale (real ≈ 220 lunar radii)
      earthMesh.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
      placeArrows();
    },

    /** sites: [{key, lat, lon}], missionIndex, recommendedIndex */
    setSites(sites, missionIndex = 0, recommendedIndex = null) {
      markerGroup.clear();
      trajGroup.clear();
      pulse = null;
      sites.forEach((s, i) => {
        const isRec = i === recommendedIndex;
        const isMission = i === missionIndex;
        const color = isRec ? 0xf2c14e : isMission ? 0x6ea2ff : 0xe7edf6;
        const p = latLonToVec(s.lat, s.lon);
        const n = p.clone().normalize();
        const stem = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.0025, 0.0025, 0.07, 8), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color }));
        stem.position.copy(n.clone().multiplyScalar(1.035));
        stem.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), n);
        const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(isRec ? 0.016 : 0.012, 16, 8), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color }));
        head.position.copy(n.clone().multiplyScalar(1.072));
        const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(0.018, isRec ? 0.03 : 0.024, 48), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, depthWrite: false }));
        ring.position.copy(n.clone().multiplyScalar(1.004));
        ring.lookAt(n.clone().multiplyScalar(2));
        const label = textSprite(isRec ? `${s.key} ★` : s.key, isRec ? '#f2c14e' : isMission ? '#6ea2ff' : '#e7edf6');
        label.position.copy(n.clone().multiplyScalar(1.12));
        markerGroup.add(stem, head, ring, label);
        if (isRec) pulse = ring;
      });
      const ms = sites[missionIndex];
      siteVec = ms ? latLonToVec(ms.lat, ms.lon) : null;
      if (siteVec) {
        // Illustrative descent: polar low-lunar-orbit (≈100 km) → powered descent → site
        const n = siteVec.clone().normalize();
        const axis = new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(n, new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0.001)).normalize();
        const orbitR = 1 + 100 / 1737.4;
        const pts = [];
        for (let k = 0; k <= 200; k++) {
          const ang = (k / 200) * Math.PI * 2;
          pts.push(n.clone().applyAxisAngle(axis, ang).multiplyScalar(orbitR));
        }
        const orbit = new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts), new THREE.LineDashedMaterial({ color: 0x8aa0c0, dashSize: 0.02, gapSize: 0.015, transparent: true, opacity: 0.6 }));
        orbit.computeLineDistances();
        const descent = [];
        for (let k = 0; k <= 60; k++) {
          const f = k / 60;
          const ang = -0.55 * (1 - f);
          const alt = 1 + (orbitR - 1) * Math.pow(1 - f, 1.6) + 0.002;
          descent.push(n.clone().applyAxisAngle(axis, ang).multiplyScalar(alt));
        }
        const dLine = new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(descent), new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x6ea2ff }));
        trajGroup.add(orbit, dLine);
      }
      placeArrows();
    },

    setLayer(name, on) {
      if (name === 'sun') {
        sunGroup.visible = on;
        if (sunArrow) sunArrow.visible = on;
      } else if (name === 'earth') {
        earthGroup.visible = on;
        if (earthArrow) earthArrow.visible = on;
      } else if (name === 'site') markerGroup.visible = on;
      else if (name === 'trajectory') trajGroup.visible = on;
      else if (name === 'terrain') {
        material.bumpScale = on ? 2.2 : 0;
        material.displacementScale = on && elevRes ? 0.012 : 0;
        material.displacementBias = on && elevRes ? -0.006 : 0;
        material.needsUpdate = true;
      } else if (name === 'illumination') {
        sunLight.intensity = on ? 3.2 : 0.0;
        ambient.intensity = on ? 0.05 : 2.2;
      }
    },

    /** base map: 'color' | 'elevation' | 'slope' */
    setBaseMap(kind) {
      material.map = kind !== 'color' && layers?.[kind] ? layers[kind] : colorTex;
      material.needsUpdate = true;
    },

    resetView() {
      defaultCam();
      controls?.target.set(0, 0, 0);
      controls?.update();
    },

    focusSite() {
      if (!siteVec) return;
      const n = siteVec.clone().normalize();
      const side = new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(n, new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0.001)).normalize();
      const to = n.clone().multiplyScalar(1.55).add(side.multiplyScalar(0.35));
      const from = camera.position.clone();
      const fromT = controls ? controls.target.clone() : new THREE.Vector3();
      const toT = n.clone().multiplyScalar(0.98);
      const t0 = performance.now();
      const step = (now) => {
        const f = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / 900);
        const e = f < 0.5 ? 2 * f * f : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * f + 2, 2) / 2;
        camera.position.lerpVectors(from, to, e);
        if (controls) controls.target.lerpVectors(fromT, toT, e);
        camera.lookAt(controls ? controls.target : toT);
        if (f < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
      };
      requestAnimationFrame(step);
    },

    snapshot() {
      renderer.render(scene, camera);
      return renderer.domElement.toDataURL('image/png');
    },

    resize(w, h) {
      renderer.setSize(w, h, false);
      camera.aspect = w / h;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    },

    dispose() {
      running = false;
      ro?.disconnect();
      controls?.dispose();
      renderer.dispose();
      scene.traverse((o) => {
        o.geometry?.dispose?.();
        if (o.material) [].concat(o.material).forEach((m) => m.dispose());
      });
      renderer.domElement.remove();
    },
  };

  if (hero) {
    group.visible = false;
    // near-full Moon: light almost from the viewer, slightly offset so crater relief shows
    sunLight.position.set(10, 3.5, 5.5);
    sunLight.intensity = 3.4;
    ambient.intensity = 0.12;
    material.bumpScale = 3.2;
    // gentle drag-to-rotate (no zoom/pan) for the interactive hero
    if (opts.interactive !== false) {
      controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
      controls.enableZoom = false;
      controls.enablePan = false;
      controls.enableDamping = true;
      controls.dampingFactor = 0.06;
      controls.rotateSpeed = 0.45;
      controls.autoRotate = true;
      controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.35;
      renderer.domElement.style.cursor = 'grab';
    }
  }

  let running = mode !== 'snapshot';
  let ro = null;
  if (mode !== 'snapshot') {
    ro = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      const w = container.clientWidth;
      const h = container.clientHeight;
      if (w && h) api.resize(w, h);
    });
    ro.observe(container);
    const loop = (now) => {
      if (!running) return;
      if (!document.hidden) {
        if (pulse) {
          const s = 1 + 0.25 * Math.sin(now / 280);
          pulse.scale.set(s, s, s);
        }
        controls?.update();
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  return api;
}

/**
 * 2D fallback Moon (no WebGL / no CDN): orthographic render of the procedural
 * cratered texture with Lambertian sunlight — keeps the same Moon visual language offline.
 */
export function drawMoon2D(container) {
  const w = container.clientWidth || 800;
  const h = container.clientHeight || 600;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.className = 'moon2d';
  canvas.width = w;
  canvas.height = h;
  container.appendChild(canvas);
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle = '#000';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let i = 0; i < (w * h) / 1800; i++) {
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.2 + Math.random() * 0.6;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, 1, 1);
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  const tex = proceduralMoonCanvas(1024, 512);
  const td = tex.getContext('2d').getImageData(0, 0, 1024, 512).data;
  const R = Math.min(w, h) * 0.36;
  const cx = w * 0.5;
  const cy = h * 0.44;
  const L = [-0.55, -0.25, 0.8];
  const ll = Math.hypot(...L);
  const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let y = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - R)); y < Math.min(h, cy + R); y++) {
    for (let x = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cx - R)); x < Math.min(w, cx + R); x++) {
      const nx = (x - cx) / R;
      const ny = (y - cy) / R;
      const r2 = nx * nx + ny * ny;
      if (r2 > 1) continue;
      const nz = Math.sqrt(1 - r2);
      const lat = Math.asin(-ny);
      const lon = Math.atan2(nx, nz);
      const u = Math.floor(((lon / (2 * Math.PI)) + 0.5) * 1023);
      const v = Math.floor((0.5 - lat / Math.PI) * 511);
      const k = (v * 1024 + u) * 4;
      const lambert = Math.max(0, (nx * L[0] + -ny * -L[1] + nz * L[2]) / ll);
      const shade = 0.03 + 1.05 * Math.pow(lambert, 0.8);
      const i = (y * w + x) * 4;
      img.data[i] = Math.min(255, td[k] * shade);
      img.data[i + 1] = Math.min(255, td[k + 1] * shade);
      img.data[i + 2] = Math.min(255, td[k + 2] * shade);
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  return canvas;
}

/** Small DOM helpers (no framework). */

export function esc(v) {
  return String(v ?? '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

export const $ = (sel, root = document) => root.querySelector(sel);
export const $$ = (sel, root = document) => [...root.querySelectorAll(sel)];

/** Delegated event listener; returns an unsubscribe function. */
export function on(root, type, selector, handler) {
  const fn = (e) => {
    const el = e.target.closest(selector);
    if (el && root.contains(el)) handler(e, el);
  };
  root.addEventListener(type, fn);
  return () => root.removeEventListener(type, fn);
}

/**
 * Save a generated file. Inside a hosted claude.ai artifact the platform "downloads"
 * capability is used (the viewer confirms); elsewhere a normal browser download.
 */
export async function download(filename, content, mime = 'text/plain') {
  const blob = content instanceof Blob ? content : new Blob([content], { type: mime });
  try {
    const dl = window.claude?.use ? await Promise.race([window.claude.use('downloads'), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r(null), 1500))]) : null;
    if (dl) {
      await dl.save({ filename, data: blob });
      return true;
    }
  } catch {
    /* fall back to a normal download */
  }
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 2000);
  return true;
}

export function cssVar(name) {
  return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name).trim();
}

  moon: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M21 12.8A9 9 0 1 1 11.2 3a7 7 0 0 0 9.8 9.8z"/></svg>',
  globe: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M3 12h18M12 3a14 14 0 0 1 0 18M12 3a14 14 0 0 0 0 18"/></svg>',
  user: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4"/><path d="M4 21a8 8 0 0 1 16 0"/></svg>',
  menu: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h16"/></svg>',
  close: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg>',
  spark: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 3l1.8 5.2L19 10l-5.2 1.8L12 17l-1.8-5.2L5 10l5.2-1.8z"/><path d="M19 15l.8 2.2L22 18l-2.2.8L19 21l-.8-2.2L16 18l2.2-.8z"/></svg>',

  pause: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 5h4v14H7zM13 5h4v14h-4z"/></svg>',
  ext: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M14 4h6v6M20 4l-9 9M18 14v5a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H5a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V7a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h5"/></svg>',
  send: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M22 2L11 13M22 2l-7 20-4-9-9-4z"/></svg>',
};

######################################################################
# FILE 37/38: public/src/utils/format.js
######################################################################

import { getState } from '../state/store.js';
import { t } from '../i18n/i18n.js';

const dec = () => Number(getState().settings.decimals ?? 2);

export function num(v, d = dec()) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || !Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return '—';
  return Number(v).toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: d, maximumFractionDigits: d });
}

export const deg = (v, d) => (Number.isFinite(v) ? `${num(v, d)}°` : '—');
export const pct = (frac, d = 0) => (Number.isFinite(frac) ? `${num(frac * 100, d)}%` : '—');
export const int = (v) => num(v, 0);

/** Elevation in user units (metric m / imperial ft). */
export function elev(m) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(m)) return { v: '—', u: '' };
  return getState().settings.units === 'imperial' ? { v: int(m * 3.28084), u: t('unit.ft') } : { v: int(m), u: t('unit.m') };
}

export function lat(v) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return '—';
  return `${num(Math.abs(v), 3)}° ${t(v < 0 ? 'dir.S' : 'dir.N')}`;
}
export function lon(v) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return '—';
  return `${num(Math.abs(v), 3)}° ${t(v < 0 ? 'dir.W' : 'dir.E')}`;
}

export function utc(iso) {
  if (!iso) return '—';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return `${d.toISOString().slice(0, 10)} ${d.toISOString().slice(11, 16)} UTC`;
}

export function hm(hourIndex) {
  const h = ((hourIndex % 24) + 24) % 24;
  return `${String(Math.floor(h)).padStart(2, '0')}:00`;
}


######################################################################
# FILE 38/38: public/src/utils/storage.js
######################################################################

/**
 * Safe key-value storage.
 * Uses localStorage when the browser allows it; falls back to sessionStorage, then to
 * in-memory storage (e.g. sandboxed iframes / private mode, where localStorage throws).
 * With the memory fallback, accounts and missions last for the current visit.
 */
const memory = new Map();

function pick() {
  for (const name of ['localStorage', 'sessionStorage']) {
    try {
      const s = window[name];
      const k = '__lunar_probe__';
      s.setItem(k, '1');
      s.removeItem(k);
      return s;
    } catch {
      /* not available */
    }
  }
  return null;
}
const backend = pick();

export const storageKind = backend === null ? 'memory' : backend === window.localStorage ? 'local' : 'session';

export function getItem(key) {
  try {
    if (backend) return backend.getItem(key);
  } catch {
    /* fall through */
  }
  return memory.has(key) ? memory.get(key) : null;
}

export function setItem(key, value) {
  memory.set(key, value); // always keep an in-memory copy so reads succeed even if a write is refused
  try {
    backend?.setItem(key, value);
  } catch {
    /* quota or policy — memory copy remains */
  }
}

/** UUID that also works outside secure contexts. */
export function uid() {
  try {
    if (crypto.randomUUID) return crypto.randomUUID();
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
  return 'id-' + Date.now().toString(36) + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
}

/** SHA-256 hex (Web Crypto when available, pure-JS fallback otherwise). */
export async function sha256(text) {
  try {
    if (crypto?.subtle) {
      const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(text));
      return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
    }
  } catch {
    /* fall back */
  }
  return sha256js(text);
}

function sha256js(ascii) {
  const rr = (v, n) => (v >>> n) | (v << (32 - n));
  const K = [];
  const H = [];
  let p = 0;
  for (let c = 2; p < 64; c++) {
    let prime = true;
    for (let d = 2; d * d <= c; d++) if (c % d === 0) { prime = false; break; }
    if (!prime) continue;
    if (p < 8) H[p] = (Math.pow(c, 1 / 2) * 2 ** 32) | 0;
    K[p++] = (Math.pow(c, 1 / 3) * 2 ** 32) | 0;
  }
  const bytes = [...new TextEncoder().encode(ascii)];
  const bitLen = bytes.length * 8;
  bytes.push(0x80);
  while (bytes.length % 64 !== 56) bytes.push(0);
  for (let i = 7; i >= 0; i--) bytes.push(i >= 4 ? 0 : (bitLen >>> (i * 8)) & 0xff);
  const h = H.slice();
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 64) {
    const w = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < 16; j++) w[j] = (bytes[i + j * 4] << 24) | (bytes[i + j * 4 + 1] << 16) | (bytes[i + j * 4 + 2] << 8) | bytes[i + j * 4 + 3];
    for (let j = 16; j < 64; j++) {
      const s0 = rr(w[j - 15], 7) ^ rr(w[j - 15], 18) ^ (w[j - 15] >>> 3);
      const s1 = rr(w[j - 2], 17) ^ rr(w[j - 2], 19) ^ (w[j - 2] >>> 10);
      w[j] = (w[j - 16] + s0 + w[j - 7] + s1) | 0;
    }
    let [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, hh] = h;
    for (let j = 0; j < 64; j++) {
      const t1 = (hh + (rr(e, 6) ^ rr(e, 11) ^ rr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[j] + w[j]) | 0;
      const t2 = ((rr(a, 2) ^ rr(a, 13) ^ rr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c))) | 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) | 0; d = c; c = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) | 0;
    }
    h[0] = (h[0] + a) | 0; h[1] = (h[1] + b) | 0; h[2] = (h[2] + c) | 0; h[3] = (h[3] + d) | 0;
    h[4] = (h[4] + e) | 0; h[5] = (h[5] + f) | 0; h[6] = (h[6] + g) | 0; h[7] = (h[7] + hh) | 0;
  }
  return h.map((v) => (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')).join('');
}
